### [1] [Engineering correlated phases through manipulation of Van Hove singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07714)
*Thomas P. Sheerin,Maria Ramirez,Chris A. Hooley,Luke C. Rhodes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过热斑parquet重整化群和截断幺正泛函重整化群两种方法，系统研究了二维方格Hubbard模型中Van Hove奇点（VHS）的奇异函数形式如何影响关联电子系统的有序相。通过调节单一跳跃参数t₃，VHS发散强度可从对数型过渡到幂律型再到劈裂型，且不同区间显著影响预测的有序态；同时在Van Hove填充附近，铁磁态对掺杂极不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Van Hove奇点的确切函数形式已被近期研究表明能深刻影响关联电子系统实现何种有序相，但对其完整的物理图景仍不清楚。作者希望系统阐明VHS发散的不同函数形式如何决定量子材料中涌现的有序相，从而为材料设计提供指导原则。

Method: 采用两种互补的微观理论方法：（1）热斑parquet重整化群（hot-spot parquet RG）和（2）截断幺正泛函重整化群（truncated-unity functional RG），对二维方格Hubbard模型在VHS位于或靠近费米能级时进行研究。通过调节单一跳跃参数t₃控制VHS发散的强度（对数型、幂律型、劈裂型），并考察掺杂依赖。

Result: 发现t₃的变化可将VHS密度态发散从对数型（t₃<t₃c）经幂律型（t₃=t₃c）转变为劈裂为两个常规对数型奇点（t₃>t₃c）的三种不同区间，且这些区间对预测的有序态有强烈影响。此外，在Van Hove填充下出现的铁磁态对费米能级极小的移动极不稳定，往往让位于依赖于电子或空穴掺杂的不同有序态。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了调控VHS特性以控制关联材料中有序相的重要性，并为在新型系统中工程设计这些相提供了具体的设计规则。

Abstract: Controlling the ordered phases of correlated electron systems remains a central challenge in quantum materials design. Divergences in the electronic density of states, known as Van Hove singularities (VHSs), are one obvious route to such control. It is clear from recent work that the exact functional form of these divergences can profoundly affect which phases are realized; a full picture, however, remains elusive. In this work, we use both the hot-spot parquet renormalization group and the truncated-unity functional renormalization group to theoretically study the emergent correlated states of a two-dimensional square-lattice Hubbard model with VHSs at or near the Fermi level. By varying a single hopping parameter, $t_3$, we are able to change the strength of the VHS divergence in the density of states from logarithmic (for $t_3 < t_{3c}$) to power-law (for $t_3 = t_{3c}$). Further increase of $t_3$ ($t_3 > t_{3c}$) causes each original Van Hove point to split into two, both of the conventional logarithmic type. We show that which of these regimes we are in strongly influences the predicted ordered states. We also study the dependence on doping, and find that the ferromagnetic state that occurs at Van Hove filling in these models is unstable to very small shifts in the Fermi level, often giving way to distinct ordered states depending on whether the model is electron- or hole-doped. These results highlight the importance of tuning VHS properties to control ordered states in correlated materials, and offer design rules to engineer these phases in novel systems.

</details>


### [2] [Metallic Gross-Neveu criticality and superconductivity on the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ SLAC fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07990)
*Feng-Yu Zhang,Yin-Kai Yu,Zi-Xiang Li,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个SO(3)对称的双层SLAC费米子模型，随着层间相互作用增强，系统经历两个相变：第一个是从狄拉克半金属到SO(3)破缺有序相的连续相变，属于N=6的Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类；第二个是进入SO(3)对称超导态的量子涨落驱动的第一类相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越传统Gross-Neveu-Yukawa(GNY)范式的狄拉克临界性已成为凝聚态物理学的前沿问题，作者希望探索标准GNY普适类之外的新的量子临界行为，并寻找具有非平凡对称性的超导平台。

Method: 构建SO(3)对称的双层SLAC免子模型，调控层间相互作用强度；采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛(QMC)模拟，系统测量临界指数并判定相变性质。

Result: 第一相变为连续相变，属于N=6不可约狄拉克锥的Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类，精确提取了相应临界指数；第二相变为量子涨落驱动的第一类相变，系统进入层间SO(3)对称超导态。

Conclusion: 该研究为探索超越标准GNY普适类的狄拉克临界性提供了新见解，同时为实现具有SO(3)对称性的超导态提供了新平台。

Abstract: The realization of Dirac criticality beyond the conventional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) paradigm has become a major frontier in condensed matter physics. In this work, we introduce an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric bilayer SLAC fermion model with tunable inter-layer interactions that exhibits a rich quantum phase diagram. As the interaction strength increases, the system undergoes two distinct phase transitions. The primary transition is a continuous boundary separating a Dirac semimetal (DSM) from an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-broken ordered phase. Crucially, this transition evades the standard GNY universality class because the emergent order only gaps out a subset of the itinerant fermions. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations, we establish that this transition belongs to the Gross-Neveu-$\mathrm{SO}(3)$ universality class with $N=6$ irreducible Dirac cones and precisely extract the corresponding critical exponents. At stronger couplings, a second transition drives the system into an inter-layer $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric superconducting (SC) state. We provide strong numerical evidence that this transition is a quantum-fluctuation-driven first-order transition. Our study provides new insights into the exploration of Dirac criticality beyond the standard GNY universality class, and also offers a novel platform to realize superconductivity featuring $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry.

</details>


### [3] [Quantum geometric localization length and localization criticality in an ideally flat Chern band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08042)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出孤立理想平坦Chern带的局域化长度由量子几何决定，并通过转移矩阵计算在最大局域化混合Wannier基中研究局域化相变及其临界标度，发现调节Chern带的量子度量可观察到从Dirac固定点控制的普适区到临界指数连续变化的非普适区的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图从量子几何角度解释平坦Chern带中的局域化现象，并为量子霍尔系统中长期存在的整数量子霍尔相变临界性争议提供新的见解。

Method: 在最大局域化混合Wannier基中进行转移矩阵计算，利用该基的空间展宽由量子几何长度精确表征的特性，通过调节Chern带的量子度量来研究局域化长度及其临界标度行为。

Result: 发现调节量子度量时存在从由Dirac固定点控制的普适区到临界指数连续变化的非普适区的交叉；在普适区内，局域化长度对量子几何长度呈现出显著的线性依赖。

Conclusion: 局域化长度在平坦Chern带中由量子几何决定，这一量子几何视角为理解扭转莫尔超晶格等量子霍尔系统中的局域化提供了新途径，并为整数量子霍尔相变临界性争议提供了新的启示。

Abstract: We propose that the localization length in an isolated, ideally flat Chern band is set by quantum geometry. We explore the corresponding localization transition and its critical scaling by applying transfer matrix calculations in the maximally localized hybrid Wannier basis, whose spatial spread is exactly characterized by a quantum geometric length. Remarkably, upon tuning the quantum metric of the Chern band, we observe a crossover from a universal regime controlled by the Dirac fixed point to a non-universal regime with continuously varying critical exponents. Within the universal regime, the localization length exhibits a pronounced linear dependence on the quantum geometric length, supporting its quantum geometric nature. These findings provide a novel quantum geometric perspective on the localization in quantum Hall systems such as twisted moiré superlattices, and shed new light on the long-standing controversy over the criticality of the integer quantum Hall transition.

</details>


### [4] [Momentum-Selective Electron and Spin Dynamics under Ultrafast Photoexcitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08104)
*Gusein Bedirkhanov,Nagamalleswararao Dasari,Alexander I. Lichtenstein,Evgeny A. Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用实时多体理论框架，解析了光激发关联电子体系中动量选择的超快动力学过程，揭示了电子加热的节点-反节点各向异性、反铁磁涨落的非热淬灭式激发及其动量空间级联弛豫等关键机制，为解释动量分辨泵浦-探测实验提供了统一微观框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨光谱学已能直接观测关联量子材料中动量选择的非平衡动力学，但现有非平衡方法无法同时自洽地刻画强局域电子关联与非局域集体涨落的相互作用，亟需一个能够统一解释动量分辨超快实验的理论框架。

Method: 采用最近发展的实时多体理论框架（real-time many-body framework），对光激发关联电子体系进行数值模拟，分别追踪电子加热的动量选择性、非热谱权重转移、反铁磁涨落的非热动力学以及实时局域自旋极化率等可观测量。

Result: 预测了电子加热中瞬态的节点-反节点各向异性，解释了时间分辨光电子发射和拉曼实验中争论的动量依赖响应；识别了导致反节点能隙内瞬态态的非热谱权重转移；发现反铁磁涨落经历强非热、类淬灭的激发，远高于电子温度但保持关联长度不变，随后通过动量空间磁激子级联向低动量模式弛豫；并识别出局域磁矩形成及其光致熔化的实时动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超快电子与磁性动力学的统一微观图像，为动量分辨泵浦-探测实验的解读提供了理论框架，加深了对强关联材料非平衡行为的理解。

Abstract: Recent advances in time-resolved spectroscopies have enabled direct access to the momentum-selective nonequilibrium dynamics of correlated quantum materials, revealing a strongly momentum-dependent response of electrons and collective excitations. Interpreting these observations requires a real-time theoretical framework that consistently captures the interplay between strong local electronic correlations and nonlocal collective fluctuations, a capability that remains beyond state-of-the-art nonequilibrium approaches. Using a recently developed real-time many-body framework, we resolve the momentum-selective ultrafast dynamics of a photoexcited correlated electron system. We predict a transient nodal-antinodal anisotropy in electronic heating, providing a microscopic explanation for the momentum-dependent response debated in time-resolved photoemission and Raman experiments, and identify the nonthermal spectral-weight transfer responsible for the transient antinodal in-gap states observed in ultrafast photoemission. We further uncover a momentum-selective magnetic response, in which antiferromagnetic fluctuations undergo a strongly nonthermal, quench-like excitation far above the electronic temperature while preserving their correlation length, before relaxing through a momentum-space magnon cascade toward lower-momentum modes. Finally, by tracking the real-time local spin susceptibility, we identify a dynamical, experimentally accessible signature of local-moment formation and its photoinduced melting. Our results establish a unified microscopic picture of ultrafast electronic and magnetic dynamics, providing a framework for interpreting momentum-resolved pump-probe experiments.

</details>


### [5] [Fermionic Lattice Supersolidity in the Attractive Three-Color Fermi-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08178)
*Xiang Li,Yu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了方形光学晶格上三分色费米-哈伯德模型，通过行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟发现，在量子简并温度下，中等至强相互作用的双色子系统与弱耦合的第三色环境相互作用会产生晶格费米超固态，该状态同时具有电荷密度波和色超流序，且易于用现有实验技术探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的实验成功实现了方形光学晶格上半填充三分色费米-哈伯德模型，这为探索超越传统SU(2)系统的奇特物质态提供了全新平台。作者旨在研究具有颜色依赖性吸引相互作用下的新奇量子态，并寻找实验上可实现的超固态费米子系统。

Method: 使用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟方法，研究方形光学晶格上带有颜色依赖性吸引相互作用的三分色费米-哈伯德模型。模型中包含中等至强相互作用的双色子系统与弱耦合的第三色环境，在量子简并温度下进行分析。

Result: 在量子简并温度下，体系从中等至强相互作用的双色子系统与弱耦合第三色环境的相互作用中涌现出晶格费米超固态。该超固态以电荷密度波与色超流序共存为特征，对现有实验探测技术具有很高的可行性。

Conclusion: 方形光学晶格上的吸引性三分色费米-哈伯德模型为探索超冷晶格费米子的超固态提供了实验上易于实现的系统，仅需简单的晶格几何构型和易于调节的格点间相互作用即可实现。

Abstract: The recent experimental realization of the half-filled three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice provides a novel platform for exploring exotic states of matter beyond conventional SU(2) systems. In this Letter, we investigate the three-color Fermi-Hubbard model with color-dependent attractive interactions using determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We find that, at quantum degenerate temperatures, a lattice Fermi supersolid state emerges from the interplay between a moderately-to-strongly interacting two-color subsystem and a weakly coupled third-color environment. This supersolid state, characterized by the coexistence of charge-density-wave and color-superfluid orders, is highly promising for experimental detection with current techniques. Our results demonstrate that the attractive three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice offers an experimentally accessible system for exploring the supersolidity of ultracold lattice fermions, requiring only a simple lattice geometry and easily tunable onsite interactions.

</details>


### [6] [Plasmon modes in quadratic and cubic nodal line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08185)
*Wei Li,Jing-Rong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在RPA框架下系统研究了三维二次和三次色散节点线半金属（NLSMs）的等离激元模式，发现其频率随载流子密度的标度律分别为ω_p~n^{1/2}和ω_p~n^{2/3}（大掺杂）→n^{3/4}（小掺杂）。其中小掺杂的n^{3/4}律因态密度发散导致r_s>1而定量不可靠，可观测窗口被限制在10^18~10^19 cm^-3。两种体系在薄环极限下共享等离激元各向异性Ω_p^z/Ω_p^⊥→√2，但密度标度指数才是区分二次与三次色散的真正标志性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性色散NLSMs的等离激元已被充分研究，但高阶色散（二次和三次）NLSMs的集体激发尚未被理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究高阶色散NLSMs中的等离激元行为及其可实验检验的区分信号。

Method: 在随机相近似（RPA）框架下，通过三维Lindhard积分解析推导单圈极化率，并对系数进行数值评估。分析了二次和三次NLSMs在不同掺杂区域的载流子密度标度律、能态密度功率律、带内（Drude）带间贡献的相对大小，以及薄环极限下的等离激元各向异性。

Result: 发现二次NLSMs的等离激元频率标度为ω_p~n^{1/2}，三次NLSMs在大掺杂时为ω_p~n^{2/3}、小掺杂时交叉到ω_p~n^{3/4}，后者的定量可靠性受限于态密度发散导致的强关联（r_s>1）。带内响应主导等离激元频率，带间贡献可忽略（小两到三个数量级）。两者共享√2各向异性双峰结构。

Conclusion: 高阶色散NLSMs的等离激元展现出与态密度幂律相关的独特密度标度律，其相空间幂计数与双层和三层石墨烯具有类比性。密度标度指数是区分二次与三次色散的决定性可观测信号，可通过HREELS实验检验，为高阶色散NLSMs的集体动力学提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Nodal line semimetals (NLSMs) host distinctive topological properties and unconventional collective excitations. While plasmons in linear NLSMs are well studied, those of higher-order dispersive NLSMs remain poorly understood. We investigate plasmon modes in 3D quadratic and cubic NLSMs within the random phase approximation (RPA), valid for $r_s\ll1$ but breaking down in the small-doping cubic regime where the density of states diverges. Deriving the one-loop polarizability and evaluating its coefficients numerically from the full 3D Lindhard integral, we find distinct carrier-density scaling laws: quadratic NLSMs exhibit $ω_p\sim n^{1/2}$, while cubic NLSMs show $ω_p\sim n^{2/3}$ at large doping crossing over to $ω_p\sim n^{3/4}$ at small doping. The $n^{3/4}$ law is not quantitatively reliable: the diverging density of states drives $r_s>1$ already at $n\sim10^{18}$ cm$^{-3}$, limiting the observable window to at most $10^{18}\lesssim n\lesssim10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$; below this, beyond-RPA correlations are essential. These scalings originate from the power-law density of states and intraband (Drude) response, and are analogous in phase-space power counting to bilayer and trilayer graphene, respectively. The long-wavelength intraband polarization dominates the plasmon frequency, while interband contributions are subleading (two to three orders of magnitude smaller for $Ω\llμ$). Both systems share a plasmon anisotropy $Ω_p^z/Ω_p^\perp\to\sqrt{2}$ in the thin-ring limit, a generic consequence of the torus Fermi-surface geometry. This $\sqrt{2}$ doublet, though observable by HREELS, cannot distinguish quadratic from cubic dispersion; the density scaling exponent is the true distinguishing signature. Our results provide a framework for the collective dynamics of higher-order dispersive NLSMs and suggest experimentally testable signatures accessible by HREELS.

</details>


### [7] [Quantum Formulation of Chiral Vortical Effect in Weyl Semi-metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08269)
*B. Q. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了手征涡旋效应（CVE）的完全量子力学表述，通过精确求解微观自旋波函数的演化，对半经典理论与假定的分布函数f_CVE进行了自下而上的定量检验。研究表明CVE是在无基态Floquet谱上实现的非平衡现象，而非热平衡分布（费米分布）所能描述。仅当慢旋转、高化学势和各向同性三个条件同时满足时，费米分布才近似成立，此时可恢复半经典结果；否则将展现"空态"、偏离j_CVE~μ²半经典公式及与费米速度无关的电荷泵浦等量子现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVE的描述基于半经典框架，缺乏全量子层面的严格验证。本文旨在建立CVE的完全量子表述，以定量检验半经典理论的适用边界，并澄清CVE与传统手征输运（如手征磁效应）在物理本质上的差异。

Method: 作者发展了一种完全量子化的CVE公式，精确求解旋转外尔费米子的微观自旋波函数演化，并在此基础上分析不同参考系中假定的分布函数f_CVE的结构与有效性。通过对Floquet谱的分析，系统比较量子结果与半经典理论的预测。

Result: 发现f_CVE基于无基态的Floquet谱，与费米分布形式有本质区别，表明CVE是非平衡现象。仅当慢旋转（ωR/v_F≪1）、高化学势（μ/(ℏv_F R)≫1）和各向同性三个条件同时满足时，费米分布近似成立并恢复半经典结果（包括电流响应系数和磁化贡献）。否则出现量子效应：空态现象、与半经典j_CVE~μ²公式的偏离、以及与费米速度无关的电荷泵浦。理论基于半金属体系，实验上更易实现探测。

Conclusion: CVE本质上是非平衡量子现象，其分布函数区别于热平衡费米分布。半经典理论仅在特定极限条件下成立，超出该条件则需全量子描述。基于半金属的理论框架为实验探测CVE提供了更可行的路径。

Abstract: The chiral vortical effect (CVE) is the generation of an axial current in a rotating Weyl fermion; its description is presently based on semiclassical frameworks. In this work, we develop a fully quantum formulation for CVE, solving the exact evolution of microscopic spinful wavefunctions, which enables a bottom-up quantitative test of semi-classical theories and postulated distributions $f_{\text{CVE}}$ in different reference frames. Notably, it shows that $f_{\text{CVE}}$ is over a ground-state-free Floquet spectrum, qualitatively distinct from a thermal equilibrium distribution (i.e., fermi form $f_F$), underscoring CVE as a non-equilibrium phenomenon, distinguished from other chiral transports. Motivation: 传统极化子模型仅考虑线性电子-声子耦合，无法解释强非谐极性材料（如SrTiO₃）中出现的异常凹形迁移率和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限违背等反常输运现象。作者旨在发展一种能够处理任意非线性电子-声子相互作用的计算方法，以探索软非谐晶格模式作为异常输运微观机制的可能性。

Result: 在中等耦合下，迁移率呈现三种温度区间：井内限制、与势垒的热竞争、以及势垒不敏感的高温动力学，产生传统线性耦合模型没有的凹形温度依赖。在强耦合下，迁移率呈非单调变化，其温度演化伴随光学谱权重的特征性再分布。对SrTiO₃相关参数的计算定性重现了异常凹形迁移率和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限违背的起始。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持非线性耦合到软非谐晶格模式作为稀释极性金属中异常输运的微观机制，为理解SrTiO₃等材料中的反常输运现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop an unbiased X-propagator method for calculating finite-temperature optical conductivity $σ(ω)$ and dc mobility $μ$ for arbitrary nonlinear electron-phonon interaction. We apply it to a polaron coupled to a double-well lattice potential, a minimal model for strongly anharmonic polar materials. At moderate coupling, the mobility exhibits three temperature regimes associated with confinement within one well, thermal competition with the barrier, and barrier-insensitive high-temperature dynamics. This sequence produces a concave temperature dependence of the mobility that is absent in conventional linear-coupling polaron models. At strong coupling, the mobility becomes nonmonotonic, and its temperature evolution is reflected in a characteristic redistribution of optical spectral weight. For parameters relevant to SrTiO$_3$, our results qualitatively reproduce both the anomalous concave mobility and the onset of violation of the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, thereby supporting nonlinear coupling to a soft anharmonic lattice mode as a microscopic mechanism for anomalous transport in dilute polar metals.

</details>


### [15] [Tunable Multiband Geometry and Fractional Phases in Higher Vortexable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09911)
*Xiaohan Wan,Siddhartha Sarkar,Ting Cao,Mark Rudner,Di Xiao,Kai Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了更高涡旋度（higher vortexability）莫尔体系中分数填充下的多体相，通过完全精确对角化保留完整平带希尔伯特空间，揭示了由波函数几何驱动的多带拓扑相级联，包括阿贝尔与非阿贝尔拓扑序相，并证明几何本身可作为调控竞争拓扑态的直接参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为更高涡旋度是实现类似更高朗道能级量子几何的拓扑平带的途径。作者强调一个互补视角：更高涡旋度不仅提供拓扑平带，还提供一个可调的多带结构，其中波函数几何可以连续变化，而能带色散、简并度和拓扑性质保持不变。这为研究几何驱动的多体拓扑相提供了理想平台。

Method: 研究者对分数填充的更高涡旋度莫尔体系进行系统精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究，保留完整平带希尔伯特空间而非投影到单一能带，采用多带处理方式，在零磁场下探索多体相的行为。

Result: 多带处理揭示了零磁场下的阿贝尔和非阿贝尔相级联，包括整数和分数激子绝缘体、阿贝尔分数量子霍尔（费米子陈绝缘体）、Moore-Read态和Read-Rezayi态。在固定填充下，不同相之间通过仅由波函数几何变化驱动的转变或渡越相连。对于Moore-Read和Read-Rezayi态相关的填充，计算表明带间混合会移动最优量子几何区域，但在屏蔽库仑相互作用下不会抑制非阿贝尔拓扑序。

Conclusion: 研究确立了更高涡旋度莫尔带作为零磁场下探索几何驱动多带拓扑相的可调平台，证明了量子几何本身可以作为直接调控参数用于在竞争拓扑态之间进行选择。

Abstract: Higher vortexability is often viewed as a route to topological flat bands with higher-Landau-level-like quantum geometry. Here we emphasize a complementary perspective: it provides a tunable multiband structure in which wave function geometry can be varied continuously while the band dispersion, degeneracy, and topology remain fixed. We perform systematic exact-diagonalization studies of many-body phases in fractionally filled higher vortexable moiré systems, retaining the full flat-band Hilbert space rather than projecting onto a single band. The multiband treatment reveals a cascade of Abelian and non-Abelian phases at zero magnetic field, including integer and fractional exciton insulators, Abelian fractional Chern insulators, Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states. At fixed filling, different phases are connected through transitions or crossovers driven solely by changes in wave function geometry, highlighting quantum geometry itself as a direct tuning parameter between competing topological states. At fillings associated with Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states, our calculations show that interband mixing shifts the optimal quantum geometry regime without suppressing non-Abelian topological order under screened Coulomb interaction. Our results establish higher vortexable moiré bands as a tunable platform for exploring geometry-driven multiband topological phases at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [16] [Cell Natural Orbitals in Interacting Topological Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09932)
*Nishchhal Verma,Harshitra Mahalingam,Daniel Muñoz-Segovia,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出利用奇异值分解带投影密度形状因子，引入"胞内自然轨道"(CNOs)作为带投影单粒子密度矩阵的本征态，实现对拓扑带投影相互作用进行基于几何特性的截断，并在手征极限魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中验证了该方法能够识别主导相互作用通道与次主导通道的层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑带由于Wannier函数的指数局域化被破坏，难以用有限范围的局域轨道表示投影相互作用，这挑战了传统上以局域轨道表示带内相互作用的标准范式。需要一种新的框架来忠实捕捉拓扑带的形状因子和量子几何，同时保持实空间关联分析的可行性。

Method: 对带投影密度形状因子进行奇异值分解，定义胞内自然轨道(CNOs)为单胞约化单粒子密度矩阵的本征态，其占据数衡量忠实表示带波函数重叠所需的最小轨道复杂度。基于CNO的占据数实现对Hilbert空间的几何截断，并通过对各CNO通道的相互作用矩阵元进行层级排序，区分需要动力学自能处理的主通道和可静态平均场处理的次通道。

Result: 在手征极限魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中，主导CNO中心位于AA位点，类似于重费米子模型中的f-费米子。次主导CNO通道携带逐渐减弱的相互作用矩阵元，可用静态平均场处理；主导通道则需要动力学自能。CNO包络函数的动量依赖性源于胞内电荷密度的变化，进而产生单粒子谱函数的色散。

Conclusion: CNOs建立了一个连接带拓扑与实空间关联的几何信息桥梁，为分析量子材料中的相互作用和涌现相提供了系统化框架，揭示出带投影相互作用中存在由底层波函数决定的内在层级结构。

Abstract: Topological bands exhibit obstruction to exponentially localized and symmetric Wannier functions, challenging the standard paradigm of representing projected interactions in terms of local orbitals with finite range. To faithfully capture the form factors and quantum geometry of topological bands we introduce a singular-value decomposition of the band-projected density form factors, enabling a geometry-based truncation scheme of the Hilbert space, exposing an intrinsic hierarchy on band-projected interactions that is determined by the underlying wavefunctions. This decomposition is most naturally described in terms of Cell Natural Orbitals (CNOs), as the eigenstates of the unit-cell reduced one-particle density matrix, whose occupation provide a measure of the minimal orbital complexity required to faithfully represent the band wavefunctions overlaps. The CNO decomposition identifies systematically the minimal number of local orbitals needed to reproduce short-ranged interactions while resolving the hierarchy of interaction strengths across CNO channels. Applied to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene in the chiral limit, we find that the dominant CNO is centered at the AA site, resembling the $f$-fermion of the heavy-fermion model. The subdominant CNO channels carry progressively weaker interaction matrix elements, allowing them to be treated at the static mean-field level, while the dominant channel requires a dynamical self-energy. The formalism illustrates how variations of charge density within the unit cell generate momentum dependence in the CNO envelope function and, consequently, dispersion in the single-particle spectral function. More broadly, our results establish CNOs as a geometry-informed bridge between band topology and real-space correlations, providing a systematic framework for analyzing interactions and emergent phases in quantum materials.

</details>

### [17] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖具体机器人配置的深度强化学习（DRL）控制方法，用于缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPR）。该方法训练一个执行器层面的策略（ALP），控制每个电机达到目标缆绳长度，而非传统DRL方法直接控制整个机器人到达期望末端位置。单个共享策略可应用于任意CDPR配置，且避免了前向运动学的求解。策略在仿真中训练并成功迁移到真实CDPR，实验表明ALP在鲁棒性和精度上均优于传统RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDPR配置多样、控制复杂，DRL虽然可以学习其非线性动力学，但传统DRL方法训练时间长，且学到的策略难以推广到不同配置或不同执行器数量的CDPR上。

Method: 提出执行器层面的DRL策略（ALP）：控制每个电机实现目标缆绳长度，而非直接控制整个机器人的末端位置。该策略具有两个优势：(i) 单个共享策略可适用于任意CDPR配置，与执行器数量无关；(ii) 依赖逆运动学，避免了更具挑战性的前向运动学问题。训练在仿真中进行，随后迁移至真实CDPR。

Result: 实验表明ALP在鲁棒性和精度上均超越传统强化学习方法。作者成功用仅在仿真4电机平面CDPR（2D）上训练的策略控制真实8电机CDPR（3D运动），证明了方法不受执行器数量和位置限制的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次将执行器层面的DRL策略应用于CDPR控制，展示了对任意配置CDPR的通用性，为DRL在机器人控制中的可扩展性与泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [18] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SC²-WM，一个自校正世界模型框架，通过内部反馈实现VLN-CE中的闭环决策，包含状态级别规划修正和模型级别条件世界感知适应，提升导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN-CE任务需要智能体在部分可观测环境下做精细导航决策，但现有方法大多采用开环执行，缺乏检测和纠正推理过程中内部状态漂移的机制。

Method: SC²-WM框架引入内部反馈用于闭环决策：1) 利用世界模型预判产生反馈，在执行动作前进行状态级别规划修正；2) 引入条件世界感知适应，在测试时当反馈表明模型容量不足时选择性更新世界模型，实现模型级别修正。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准上的实验表明，该方法提升了导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过世界模型内部反馈的闭环决策机制，能够有效纠正状态漂移和模型能力不足带来的导航偏差，显著提升了连续环境下视觉语言导航的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [19] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督视觉操控方法，机器人自动在目标位姿周围生成演示数据并直接从腕部单目RGB图像学习相对位姿校正，无需相机-机器人外参标定。方法在仿真（平面偏差从9.69mm降至5.38mm）和真实UR5e机器人（两个物体成功率66.6%和63.6%）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操控依赖物体特定编程、人工数据标注或校准感知管线，限制了实际场景的快速部署。虽然示教学习提供了更直接的替代方案，但收集演示仍需要人类遥操作或动觉教学。作者旨在消除这些人工干预，实现无需外参标定且可自动生成演示的自监督视觉操作。

Method: 提出三阶段流程：(1) 利用ROS 2和Isaac Sim自动生成带标注的图像-位姿对，无需显式相机-机器人外参标定；(2) 分别生成平面精调数据集和三维粗趋近数据集，训练卷积网络从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移和旋转；(3) 执行时采用从粗到精控制策略，先通过含高度变化的模型接近物体，再用平面数据精调最终对齐。

Result: 仿真中精调阶段将平面散布从9.69mm降至5.38mm。真实实验中，在UR5e协作机器人上对三个物理物体进行端到端抓取尝试，无旋转条件下两个物体成功率达66.6%和63.6%，旋转条件下保持部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自动生成的演示数据能以较少的设置投入支持实际视觉操控，验证了自监督学习框架的可行性。但深度预测和物体依赖泛化仍是待解决的挑战，表明该方法在通用性和鲁棒性方面还有提升空间。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [20] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出AgenticRobotics——一个后端无关的控制平面，将编码智能体的"主智能体-子智能体-工具"架构移植到机器人策略改进中，通过持久化的训练-评估-改进事务和证据门控的晋升机制，实现可审计、可恢复、可验证的自动化机器人策略优化闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略改进领域面临一个核心挑战：与编码工具不同，机器人工具（训练策略、训练流水线、数据采集）会频繁失败，且工具质量必须被测量、记录并在底层工件变化时失效。现有方法缺乏对这一"工具会坏"特性的系统性处理，导致自动化闭环不可靠、不可审计、难以恢复。

Method: 采用"主LLM控制器+可丢弃工人+持久事务"架构。核心组件包括：不可变目标（immutable objective）、控制器拥有的测量机制、提交键控的崩溃恢复（commit-keyed crash recovery）、证据分级的技能库、以及带标准化记录调用面的工具注册表。每次工具调用都记录质量指标，工件变化触发工具过期。

Result: 实验表明：硬化的晋升门控将误晋升概率从0.005-0.021降至0.001每轮；在可选停止下提供任意时间有效的决策；在异常终止注入下实现零丢失或重复效果；签名验证器能捕获六类工件篡改。

Conclusion: 该系统的价值定位是"可操作性声明"而非"选择性声明"：操作员可以离开，是因为晋升被证据门控、状态可恢复、能力质量来源于记录——而非因为该循环比人类选择出更好的检查点。在作者测量的单一谱系上，它并不比人类更优。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [21] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种零成本的自动化直接偏好优化（DPO）流水线，用于无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）。核心发现是感知质量比语言推理能力更重要——轻量级2B模型配合高保真视觉输入即可媲美7B大模型；但纯行为克隆存在OOD场景碰撞率高的鲁棒性缺陷。AeroDPO通过物理仿真状态回滚自动提取因果推理错误作为拒绝样本，并结合解耦特权干预合成避碰偏好样本，在未映射场景中将成功率提升至49.16%并大幅抑制碰撞率，刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉语言导航的极简端到端范式依赖数十亿参数的大语言模型，推理延迟过高，难以满足边缘部署的实时性需求。作者质疑这一参数密集型范式的必要性，并通过跨尺度评估发现感知质量比语言推理容量更重要。同时，纯行为克隆缺乏负反馈机制，导致智能体在分布外场景中无法内化空间约束，碰撞率过高。因此需要一种不依赖人工标注的自动化方法来解决鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出AeroDPO流水线，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于确定性物理仿真状态回滚的零成本自动化DPO管线——检测到碰撞后自动回退环境状态，提取导致碰撞的因果推理错误作为拒绝动作；(2) 解耦特权干预机制，通过特权信息合成避碰的偏好动作；(3) 离线视觉语言审查器，过滤视觉歧义以保障训练数据质量。整体形成一个自动化的数据飞轮，为2B轻量级模型提供正负偏好对进行DPO训练。

Result: AeroDPO在未映射场景中将2B模型的成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，建立了自主空中智能体的新SOTA。跨尺度评估显示轻量级2B模型搭配高保真视觉输入即可完全匹配7B基线的总体成功率，验证了感知质量优于推理容量的关键洞察。

Conclusion: 该工作挑战了大参数模型的必要性假设，证明感知质量是UAV-VLN中的主导因素。同时，AeroDPO展示了如何通过物理仿真与自动化数据合成，在不依赖人工标注的前提下获得DPO所需的偏好信号，为边缘场景下的鲁棒导航策略提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [22] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-ART（Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy），一个针对力与触觉感知的机器人学习方法的统一分类框架，从多模态感知和多阶段系统设计两个维度系统性地梳理了该领域的最新研究进展，旨在弥合语义任务理解与反应式物理执行之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触敏感操作任务的成功不仅依赖视觉感知和运动生成，还需要力调节和自适应控制。尽管近年机器人在力、触觉、视觉、语言和本体感觉等多模态融合以及多阶段架构方面取得了显著进展，但现有综述尚未从统一视角同时涵盖多模态感知与多阶段系统设计来回顾力与触觉感知的机器人学习方法，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出TF-ART分类框架，将各方法映射到统一的分层结构中，从四个关键维度刻画现有工作：(1) 观测模态的组织方式；(2) 异构感官输入的编码与融合；(3) 跨多阶段的动作生成与精化；(4) 学习策略与反应式机器人末端控制之间的连接。此外，进一步考察物理交互的任务设置与基础设施需求。

Result: 本文是一篇综述性工作，通过TF-ART框架系统性地组织和分类了当前力与触觉感知的机器人学习方法，整合了算法视角与工程实践视角，为研究者提供了领域全景和技术图谱。

Conclusion: TF-ART框架能够有效地将力与触觉感知机器人学习的多样化方法组织为统一的分层结构，同时涵盖多模态感知和多阶段系统设计，为后续研究提供了系统化的分析与归纳基础。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [23] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出投影-回缩MPPI（PR-MPPI）方法，在采样动力学内部强制约束（闭环等式+不等式），解决传统MPPI仅以软惩罚方式近似处理约束的问题，在14自由度双臂系统上验证约束满足到数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制因无梯度、可并行处理非凸代价而广泛用于操作任务，但操作任务常需全程满足约束（如双臂形成闭环运动学链、关节极限、避障）。传统MPPI仅通过代价函数将约束作为软惩罚处理，只能近似满足，且在强任务代价下会失效。

Method: PR-MPPI在每步rollout中对采样速度进行投影以同时满足两类约束：等式约束将速度限制到一个子空间，各不等式约束将其限制在该子空间内的半空间，从而保证不等式处理不破坏等式。由于投影仅一阶满足约束且有限步会离开等式产生漂移，进一步将返回的控制命令回缩到约束上，达到数值容忍精度，且与任务权重无关。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统上验证：仿真中，返回命令在关节极限压力测试和随机避障中均能将闭环等式满足到数值容忍精度；真实硬件上，Unitree H1-2人形机器人的双臂能反应式躲避移动障碍物。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI通过在采样动力学内强制执行约束（而非仅作为软惩罚），并辅以回缩操作消除投影的一阶近似误差，能够在不依赖任务权重的情况下将约束精确满足到数值容忍度，在仿真和真实硬件上均验证有效。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [24] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将几何智能嵌入材料系统的自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，该模块由共轭立方八面体-八面体单元组成，集成了机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合功能，并通过机械臂和移动装配器演示了家具及建筑尺度的可重构、可循环利用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料系统与机器人视为独立的设计问题，智能主要集中在硬件、传感、运动规划和控制中。本文旨在探索如何将几何智能编码在架构化材料系统中，以同时满足机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚集等多重要求。

Method: 设计了一种自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，由共轭的立方八面体-八面体单元组成。立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对齐的明确表面，八面体特征融入可螺丝释放的按扣式连接器及相应接收器。采用嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z轴的互锁聚集。通过机械臂和移动装配器搭建家具及建筑尺度的结构进行验证。

Result: 系统成功组装出座椅、跨结构、曲面和垂直框架等多种构型。压缩测试显示复合模块刚度为4,556 N/mm，最大载荷为3,445 N，压缩模量为17.5 MPa。模块可拆卸并在不同构型间重复使用。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将几何智能嵌入材料系统可实现机器人自主装配与结构性能的一体化设计，支持可重构和循环建造，为机器人建造提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [25] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的凸多面体间可微有符号距离（Hölder signed distance），通过引入Hölder最小/最大算子替换经典SDF中的min-max算子，以闭式解实现处处可微的计算，消除了迭代算法的收敛问题并支持GPU并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划与控制中需要计算集合间距离且要求梯度可微以支持实时优化，但经典欧几里得有符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微；现有替代方法往往牺牲可微性、符号信息或计算效率。

Method: 首先提出Hölder最小和Hölder最大可微算子，然后用其替换经典SDF公式中的原始min-max算子，得到Hölder有符号距离。该距离为闭式解析解，不依赖迭代算法，天然适合GPU并行化。

Result: 通过与现有方法的运行时对比验证了计算优势，并在机器人机械臂实验中证明了其在控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: Hölder有符号距离兼具可微性、符号信息与计算效率，为机器人控制中基于梯度的实时优化提供了一种无需迭代、可并行的新方案。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [26] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LIRA，一种局部跨层动作条件机制，通过深度感知信息路由来改进VLM特征到动作解码器的接口，在多个机器人操作基准上超越VLA-Adapter基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型利用预训练VLM特征生成机器人动作，但VLM特征到动作解码器的路由接口未被充分探索。现有设计要么只暴露表示层级中的窄部分，要么将每个解码器块严格匹配到单个VLM层，限制了跨深度访问互补任务证据的能力。

Method: LIRA将VLM到动作的条件化建模为深度感知信息路由。它作用于任务token特征和从中间VLM状态导出的LIRA Query特征，为每个并行融合块分配一个以其对应VLM层为中心的深度对齐局部窗口。并行融合块聚合相邻LIRA Query特征，并将其与任务token特征和本体感知输入集成，然后进行动作预测。该接口保持主干架构、动作解码器和监督训练方案不变。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操作中，LIRA在相同0.5B参数配置下改进了VLA-Adapter基线的主要聚合指标。在LIBERO-Plus零样本迁移中，LIRA将平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提升18.9个百分点，表明在受控分布偏移下的鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: LIRA通过深度对齐的局部窗口路由机制，实现了跨层特征的有效聚合，在不改变主干架构和训练流程的前提下显著提升了VLA模型的性能与鲁棒性，为VLM到动作解码器的接口设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [27] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，结合抓取生成、可行性预测与自适应调整，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联的紧凑表征来提升机器人抓取的成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。人类能够无缝整合视觉感知与触觉反馈实现稳定、自适应的抓取，这一能力在机器人系统中尚未被有效复现。

Method: 提出统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含三个核心模块：抓取生成、可行性预测与自适应细化。核心创新在于一种高效的视觉-触觉表征，将物体几何信息与触觉反馈紧密结合，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联，支持规划阶段的接触感知抓取姿态生成和接触后的触觉引导细化，使系统能够推理细粒度的手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界中进行了综合实验，结果表明该方法的抓取成功率显著提升，并在多种物体上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该统一视觉-触觉融合框架通过整合接触前后的视觉与触觉信息，有效增强了机器人抓取的稳定性与泛化性，为触觉引导的自适应抓取提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [28] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于扩展现实（XR）的3D可视化平台，通过结合5-DOF机器视觉追踪算法和3D打印机电编码器实现6-DOF导管实时追踪，并在患者特定3D心脏模型中可视化。20名医学新生在概念验证研究中分别使用3D沉浸式视图和传统2D视图导航ICE导管至六个解剖靶点，结果表明3D模式在速度（快5倍以上）、导管行程（减少约5倍）、靶向精度和操作一致性方面均显著优于2D模式，且用户在3D模式下表现出更平滑的深度轴运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术已有进展，但大多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，这限制了术者的深度感知和空间理解能力，影响手术效率与精度的提升。

Method: 开发了一个XR平台，结合定制机器视觉算法（提供5-DOF导管追踪）与3D打印机电编码器（测量导管滚动角），实现完整6-DOF导管运动重建，并在患者特异性3D心脏模型中实时可视化。通过20名无经验医学新生在3D沉浸式视图与传统2D导管室视图两种条件下导航ICE导管至六个解剖靶点的对比实验进行评估，并辅以运动学分析和主观评分。

Result: 3D条件下参与者平均用时54.6秒、行程1,939mm，而2D条件下分别为267.5秒和7,854mm，3D模式快5倍多且导管行程减少约5倍。3D模式还提高了靶向精度并降低了表现变异性，用户对3D的准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值评分更高。运动学分析显示3D用户深度轴导航更平滑，2D用户则依赖反复矫正动作。

Conclusion: XR-based 3D可视化能够显著提升经皮心脏介入手术的训练效率、操作精度和运动控制能力，为未来临床介入手术的3D可视化应用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [29] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GWM-VLA是一种几何感知的潜在世界模型框架，通过结合几何感知多视角状态编码、全局上下文条件的目标视角预测以及共享潜在动作表征，提升VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性，在仿真和真实环境中均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在视觉和环境分布偏移下性能显著退化。虽然潜在世界建模有望提升鲁棒性，但现有方法通常独立编码相机视角并预测整体场景动态，未明确建模视角间的几何关系。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于VGGT-Ω的几何感知多视角状态编码，在每个时间步联合聚合多视角观测；(2) 全局上下文条件的目标视角预测，仅预测选定目标视角（实验中为手腕视角）的下一时间步补丁令牌，避免预测完整多视角状态；(3) 共享潜在动作表征同时条件化潜在世界模型和流匹配动作头，使潜在预测监督和真实机器人动作监督共同塑造同一潜在动作表征。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验均证明了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性，相比现有方法在视觉和环境变化下表现出更强的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过在潜在世界建模中显式建模多视角几何关系，并以手腕视角为目标视角聚焦末端执行器运动和局部夹爪-物体交互，GWM-VLA有效提升了VLA模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [30] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER，一种具有可移动铰接点和内部质量重定位能力的可重构履带机器人，通过钢带弹簧机构在弯曲区域局部化柔性同时保持主体高刚度，实现了跨台阶、跨越悬挂平台和跨沟等卓越的越障性能（分别达车体长度的74%、66%和59%），据称是已报道履带式移动机器人中最好的越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带机器人在非结构化环境中广泛使用，但其越障能力受到前端可达性与运动稳定性之间根本性权衡的限制。现有设计难以同时提升这两方面性能，需要一种新机制来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: TRASER采用钢带弹簧机制，将柔性局部化在弯曲区域而保持其余车体高刚度，从而同时改善前端可达性和质心（CoM）移位能力。机器人能够重定位铰接点和内部质量，并建立了几何模型和静力学模型来分析铰接点位置和质心位置对台阶和沟壑越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，TRASER分别实现了车体长度74%的台阶越障、66%的悬挂平台越障以及59%的沟壑越障，这些结果为已报道履带式移动机器人中最高的越障能力。

Conclusion: 通过可移动铰接点与内部质量重定位相结合的设计，以及钢带弹簧机制对刚柔性质的优化分配，TRASER有效地突破了履带机器人前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的传统权衡，为履带式移动机器人的越障能力设定了新的性能基准。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [31] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的多机器人公式化方法，用于解决"最大容量下的有序存储与检索问题"。通过利用无重排（relocation-free）排列的几何性质，本文开发了一个在线、优先级驱动的多智能体路径规划算法，在消除巷道的高密度仓储中实现无死锁的规模化操作，并实验证明在机器人数量等于网格宽度时，makespan接近线性改善，且鲁棒性支持的开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库在存储密度与检索吞吐量之间存在根本性权衡。拼图式存储（PBS）架构通过消除巷道来提升容量，但在这种高密度空间中协调多个机器人在计算上具有挑战性，因为容易发生死锁。现有集中式规划器难以在保证完整性的同时实现可扩展性。

Method: 本文聚焦于可从单一边界访问的矩形网格，要求负载先储满容量再按预定出发序列高效检索。方法的核心是利用无重排排列的几何性质来指导一个在线、优先级驱动的多智能体路径规划算法。该算法利用存储排列的具体不变量（invariants）来保证完整性（completeness）并防止死锁，从而实现可扩展性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在makespan上相对于机器人数量实现了近似线性的改善，最高可达m=C（C为网格宽度）。此外，支持鲁棒性的算法开销可忽略不计；系统使用鲁棒存储排列来处理出发序列的不确定性，在执行速度上与不鲁棒的基线相比没有显著损失。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了几何可行性（geometric feasibility）与执行效率（execution efficiency）之间的鸿沟，通过利用无重排排列的特性，提出了一种兼顾完整性、死锁预防和可扩展性的多机器人路径规划方法，并验证了鲁棒性支持不会以显著性能代价为代价。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [32] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回旋曲线（clothoid/Euler spiral）与自适应前视距离机制相结合的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，通过准反馈控制算法生成最优回旋曲线并加入超前滤波器补偿横向动力学滞后，经TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证了其在多样驾驶场景下的横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在横向控制中面临路径跟踪精度与乘坐舒适性、侧翻风险之间的矛盾。传统控制方法在曲率突变处易导致乘客不适和车辆失稳，需要一种能够平滑过渡曲率并自适应调整控制参数的策略，以兼顾跟踪精度与行驶稳定性。

Method: 采用基于Euler螺旋（回旋曲线）的横向控制策略，通过自适应前视距离机制根据实时车辆动力学状态和道路几何信息动态调整前视距离；在每个时间步通过准反馈控制算法构造最优回旋曲线以生成转向输入；引入超前滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学滞后，提升控制响应速度与系统稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证，该控制器在多种驾驶场景下均表现出显著的横向控制性能提升，包括更好的路径跟踪精度、更平滑的曲率过渡以及更低的侧翻风险。

Conclusion: 该基于回旋曲线与自适应前视的综合横向控制策略有效提升了自动驾驶车辆的横向稳定性与路径跟踪能力。未来工作将扩展至高速场景，并进一步优化以提高铰接式车辆的离轨误差控制。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [33] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了CoCoNav，一种结合在线共形校准与运行时认证规划的群体导航框架，通过水平特定共形PI控制器自适应轨迹误差边界，并采用"松弛-验证"规划器保证MPC可行性，在仿真与四足机器人实验中取得了安全性与效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式导航方法易产生振荡行为，而预测式规划器要么将预测视为精确、要么依赖限制性误差模型；将保守不确定性集作为硬约束又会使MPC不可行。需要一种既能应对不确定且漂移的预测误差、又能保证求解可行性的导航方法。

Method: (1) 在线共形校准：采用水平特定的共形比例-积分(PI)控制器，自适应调整轨迹误差界，以调节长期经验覆盖率，应对变化的预测误差；(2) relax-then-verify规划器：先用软约束MPC生成名义轨迹保证求解可行性，再结合应急机动轨迹，对照校准后的误差界进行独立的安全认证，通过后才执行。

Result: 在仿真和四足机器人实验中，CoCoNav相较于基线方法在碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率之间取得了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过将共形校准与运行时认证解耦（软约束求解+硬约束验证），CoCoNav有效兼顾了MPC可行性与导航安全性，为动态人群环境下的安全导航提供了一种实用且鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高海拔雪地环境的无人机（UAV）与地面无人车（UGV）协同导航框架，通过高效U-Net网络结合合成雪景数据增强实现96.5%的实时道路分割精度，利用EKF融合GPS/IMU实现无人机定位（误差±0.5米），并通过YOLOv5视觉跟踪管线确定UGV位置，最终采用动态路径规划算法应对积雪偏移，实现在模糊测试环境中的成功导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高海拔雪地地形中，能见度低和地面不稳定导致传统导航方法失效，亟需一种能在计算资源受限条件下工作的UAV-UGV协同导航方案。

Method: 该方法包含四个核心组件：(1) 定制的轻量级U-Net架构，满足实时道路分割的计算约束，并采用新颖的合成雪数据增强技术；(2) 基于扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）的UAV定位，融合机载GPS和IMU数据；(3) 基于YOLOv5和UAV RGB-D相机深度数据的视觉跟踪管线用于UGV定位；(4) 利用分割结果进行动态路径规划，以适应积雪漂移。

Result: 道路分割准确率达到96.5%；UAV最大位置误差为±0.5米；动态路径规划算法使系统在能见度受限的测试环境中以最小偏差成功完成导航任务。

Conclusion: 该协同框架有效解决了高海拔雪地环境下的导航难题，证明了合成数据增强和轻量级深度学习架构在资源受限的自主导航系统中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [35] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过材料选择而非解剖结构在指尖垫内创建约束边界，以扩展薄平物体（如硬币）的精密抓取操作范围。混合刚度（中心软、两侧硬）的仿真指尖在多种测试条件下比均匀软质指尖表现出更高的抓取成功率，而边界位置成为指尖设计的关键变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取硬币等薄平物体具有挑战性，传统方法需要重新定向物体、接触其底面或添加专用指甲机构。先前研究已证明刚性指甲能阻止软垫变形并形成几何约束以改善精密抓取，本文进一步探索是否可以通过材料选择在垫内创建额外的约束边界来扩展该约束成立的条件范围。

Method: 制造了中心为Shore E10硅胶、两侧为Shore A60硅胶的混合刚度仿真指尖，并与均匀软质E10指尖进行比较。使用自动化装置执行斜向旋转指尖捏合，让指垫接触硬币侧面并从无间隙的平齐接触状态将其提起。在水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转等条件下测试两种指尖的成功率。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持高成功率，包括几何约束形成阶段和向稳定抓取的过渡阶段。无指甲的指尖在所有36个条件中均失败。但在沿手指轴方向变化硬币位置时，均匀软质指尖表现更好，呈现条件依赖的权衡。经硬币尺寸调优后，两种指尖均能抓取全部六种日本硬币面额。

Conclusion: 薄物抓取的操作范围不仅取决于指垫软度，还取决于刚度在带指甲支撑的指垫内的分布位置。边界位置（刚度过渡位置）可被视为指尖设计中的候选设计变量，为机器人指尖设计提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>

*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPF，一种免训练的多模态概率融合框架，用于检测和纠正机器人演示数据集中"行为正确但指令错误"的指令-轨迹不匹配（ITM）问题。该方法将每个模态视为专家，结合局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布，并通过预测熵加权的专家乘积进行融合，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人数据上取得了最优的检测与标签校正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略训练所用的机器人演示数据集中存在一种隐蔽且有害的失败模式：轨迹行为正确但配对的自然语言指令错误（即ITM）。这种不匹配与失败的rollout不同，往往看起来合理，容易污染策略学到的语言-行为映射关系，亟需一种事后审计方法来识别和纠正这些问题。

Method: 提出MMPF（Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion）框架，它是一种免训练的审计框架。核心内容包括：1）将每个模态视为一个独立的专家；2）通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布；3）使用预测熵加权在产品专家（Product of Experts）框架下融合各模态的信息，实现ITM检测与标签校正。

Result: 在LIBERO基准（注入指令不匹配）和含噪声的真实机器人数据上，MMPF取得了最强的整体ITM检测与标签纠正准确率。同时，在需要语言来消除任务歧义的场景中，审计能显著提升下游策略学习性能。真实机器人实验也证明该方法能改善策略性能，并揭示了过滤演示与重新标注之间的权衡。

Conclusion: MMPF作为一种简单有效的免训练审计框架，能够可靠地检测和纠正视觉-语言-动作策略训练数据中的指令-轨迹不匹配问题，且能通过改进数据质量来提升下游策略学习效果，是一种实用且通用的数据审计方案。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [37] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表示对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），克服2D像素空间与3D动作执行空间之间的表示鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）通常在2D像素空间中表示视频，而机器人动作在3D空间执行，存在表示鸿沟。虽然近期3D方法引入了3D信息，但未能充分利用3D结构的内在动态特性。

Method: 提出4D-WAM训练策略，通过两个互补目标将3D轨迹场的时空知识对齐到WAMs中：1）运动对齐（motion alignment），对齐相邻帧间的时序特征变化，促进局部4D感知；2）目的地对齐（destination alignment），通过最小化源帧与其注意力相似性分布之间的差距，引导模型预测最终目的地。两个目标共同提供局部运动监督和长时程目标引导。

Result: 在多种基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，4D-WAM在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和通用性方面均取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过局部运动监督与长时程目的地引导的结合，使WAMs能够学习轨迹级别的时空表示，有效弥合了2D视频表示与3D动作执行之间的鸿沟，提升了模型的整体性能。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>
