### [106] [A Recursive Algorithm for Routing amid Convex Polygonal Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08388)
*Siddharth Gaur,R. Inkulu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对平面多边形障碍域（由h个两两不相交的凸多边形障碍物构成，共n个顶点），提出了一种预处理算法，为域内所有顶点计算路由表，使得数据包从任意顶点到任意其他顶点均可路由。算法在O(n² lg n)时间内为每个顶点分配大小为O(√h·lg h·lg n)的唯一标签，并计算大小为O(h·lg n + √h·lg h·min((1/ε)^O(lg α), n)·lg n)的路由表，路由路径具有(7+ε)·lg h的乘法拉伸比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有障碍物的多边形域中进行几何路由，目标是设计紧凑的路由表与标签方案，使得数据包在无全局信息的情况下高效路由，同时保证路径长度近似最优（即较小的拉伸比）。本文旨在解决该问题，兼顾预处理时间、标签大小、路由表大小与路由路径质量。

Method: 提出一种针对凸多边形障碍域的预处理算法。该算法利用障碍物的几何结构（如α参数反映的几何特性）和障碍物数量的平方根层次结构，为每个顶点设计唯一标签，并基于标签计算局部路由表；路由决策通过局部路由表与数据包头信息在O(1)时间内确定下一跳，从而引导数据包沿具有有界拉伸比的路径前进。

Result: 预处理时间复杂度为O(n² lg n)；每个顶点分配大小为O(√h·lg h·lg n)的标签；每个顶点的路由表大小为O(h·lg n + √h·lg h·min((1/ε)^O(lg α), n)·lg n)；路由路径的乘法拉伸比为(7+ε)·lg h，其中ε>0为输入参数，α>1为几何参数。

Conclusion: 该算法在具有h个凸多边形障碍物的域上，以多项式级别的预处理代价实现了紧凑的标签与路由表，并保证了O(lg h)级别的乘法拉伸比，为有障碍环境下的几何路由提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Given a polygonal domain $\cal P$ comprising $h$ pairwise disjoint convex polygonal obstacles in the plane, together defined with $n$ vertices, this paper presents an algorithm to preprocess $\cal P$ to compute routing tables at the vertices of $\cal P$ so that a data packet from any vertex of $\cal P$ is routed to any other vertex belonging to $\cal P$. At every vertex $v$ of $\cal P$ along the routing path, until the packet reaches its destination, the next hop is determined using the routing tables at $v$ and the information stored in the packet header. In $O(n^2(\lg{n}))$ time, our preprocessing algorithm assigns a unique label of size $O(\sqrt{h} (\lg{h}) \lg{n})$ to each vertex of $\cal P$ and computes routing tables of size $O(h\lg{n} + \sqrt{h}(\lg{h})(\min((\frac{1}ε)^{O( \lg α)},n))$ $\lg {n})$ at each vertex of $\cal P$. The routing path output has a $(7 + ε)(\lg{h})$ multiplicative stretch. Here, $ε> 0$ is an input parameter and $α> 1$ is a geometric parameter.

</details>


### [107] [Towards Lower Bounds for Geometric Spanners in High Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08706)
*Robert Krauthgamer,Nir Petruschka*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维ℓ_p空间中几何spanner的拉伸比（stretch）与边数（size）之间的权衡关系。主要贡献包括：(1) 为Har-Peled等人(SODA 2013)的下界提供了一个简单证明，即ℓ₂范数下点集{0,1}ᵈ的任意2-hop t-spanner至少需要(2ᵈ)^{1+Ω(1/t²)}条边，且该证明可推广到含Steiner顶点的spanner；(2) 建立了受限跳数spanner与一般spanner之间的联系：若n点度量空间中每个子集都有大小为μ|Y|的t-spanner，则该度量空间存在大小为O(n(μ+log n))的O(t)-hop O(t)-spanner，从而证明了受限跳数的下界可传递给某个子集的一般spanner下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何spanner在计算几何和网络设计中有广泛应用，其核心问题是在拉伸比与边数之间取得最优权衡。然而在高维空间（尤其是ℓ_p空间）中，这一权衡关系仍未被充分理解。先前Har-Peled等人给出的下界证明较为复杂，且Steiner顶点情形下的结果不明确。此外，受限跳数spanner与一般spanner之间的联系缺乏系统性研究，本文旨在简化下界证明并建立两者之间的桥梁。

Method: 本文采用纯数学/组合方法：(1) 对Har-Peled等人关于{0,1}ᵈ点集在ℓ₂范数下2-hop t-spanner的边数下界给出一个更简洁的证明，该证明基于对点集结构的精细分析，并扩展到允许Steiner顶点的情况；(2) 通过构造性论证建立受限跳数spanner与一般spanner之间的归约关系：利用每个子集都具有μ-稀疏t-spanner的性质，递归地构造出跳数和拉伸比均为O(t)的全局spanner。

Result: 本文获得了两个主要结果：其一，简化并推广了Har-Peled等人的下界，证明即使允许引入Steiner顶点，ℓ₂范数下{0,1}ᵈ的2-hop t-spanner仍至少需要(2^d)^{1+Ω(1/t²)}条边；其二，建立了跳数受限与跳数不受限spanner之间的归约定理，即子集级稀疏性可保证全局O(t)-hop spanner的存在，从而将受限跳数的下界转化为某子集上一般spanner的下界。

Conclusion: 本文通过简洁的证明推广了高维ℓ_p空间中几何spanner的拉伸-规模下界，并建立了受限跳数spanner与一般spanner之间的重要联系。这一归约框架为后续研究提供了新工具：无需直接处理一般spanner，只需针对受限跳数情形建立下界即可隐式获得一般情形下的下界结果。该工作深化了对高维空间spanner结构本质的理解。

Abstract: We study the stretch--size tradeoff for geometric spanners in high-dimensional $\ell_p$ spaces. Our main contribution is a simple proof of a lower bound shown by Har-Peled, Indyk, and Sidiropoulos [SODA 2013]: Every $2$-hop $t$-spanner of the pointset $\{0,1\}^d$ under $\ell_2$ norm has at least $(2^d)^{1+Ω(1/t^2)}$ edges. Our proof further extends this result to spanners with Steiner vertices. In addition, we establish a connection between bounded-hop spanners and general spanners, as follows. If every subset $Y$ of an $n$-point metric has a $t$-spanner with at most $μ|Y|$ edges, then the metric has an $O(t)$-hop $O(t)$-spanner of size $O(n(μ+\log n))$. Consequently, hop-restricted spanner lower bounds for a metric imply lower bounds without hop restriction for one of its subsets.

</details>


### [108] [Two-point Approximate Shortest Path Queries among Convex Polygonal Obstacles in the Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09677)
*Siddharth Gaur,R. Inkulu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 针对由h个两两不相交的凸多边形障碍物（共n个顶点）构成的多边形域P，提出一种近似最短路径查询算法。该算法在O(n+h/ε(h+1/√ε)·lg(h/√ε))时间内预处理P，构建O(n+h/√ε·(h+1/ε))大小的数据结构，使得任意两点s和t之间可在O(1/√ε·lg(h/√ε)+h/ε^2.5·lg(lg(h/√ε)))时间内输出一条具有(1+ε)乘法拉伸和13ℓ加法拉伸的路径，其中ℓ的上界由障碍物直径和ε确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含多个凸多边形障碍物的自由空间中，如何在有限预计算成本下快速回答任意两点间的近似最短路径查询，同时保证路径质量（乘法和加法误差均受控），是计算几何中路径规划的核心问题之一。

Method: 提出一种预处理与查询相结合的算法：利用h和ε为参数的设计，构建紧凑的索引数据结构，在查询阶段输出同时具有(1+ε)乘法拉伸和13ℓ加法拉伸的近似最短路径。算法复杂度以h和ε显式表达，适合障碍物较少但顶点较多或对精度有特定要求的场景。

Result: 预处理时间为O(n+h/ε(h+1/√ε)·lg(h/√ε))，数据结构大小为O(n+h/√ε·(h+1/ε))，查询时间为O(1/√ε·lg(h/√ε)+h/ε^2.5·lg(lg(h/√ε)))。输出的路径满足(1+ε)乘法拉伸与13ℓ加法拉伸。

Conclusion: 该算法在凸多边形障碍物场景下提供了高效的近似最短路径查询方案，通过合理的预处理与存储开销，实现了查询效率与路径质量的平衡。

Abstract: Given a polygonal domain $\cal P$ consisting $h$ pairwise disjoint convex polygonal obstacles together defined with $n$ vertices and a positive real number $ε$ in $(0, 0.6)$, this paper presents an algorithm to preprocess $\cal P$ in $O(n+\frac{h}ε(h+\frac{1}{\sqrtε})\lg(\frac{h}{\sqrtε}))$ time to compute data structures of size $O(n+\frac{h}{\sqrtε} (h+\frac{1}ε))$ so that given any two points $s$ and $t$ in the free space defined by $\cal P$, a path between $s$ and $t$ with a $(1+ε)$ multiplicative stretch and $13\ell$ additive stretch is output in $O(\frac{1}{\sqrtε}(\lg{\frac{h}{\sqrtε}})+\frac{h}{ε^{2.5}}(\lg{\lg(\frac{h}{\sqrtε})}))$ time. Here, $\ell$ is upper bounded by $(\sqrt{2ε}) (\max_{P_i \in \cal P} \max_{p, q \in P_i} |pq|)$.

</details>


### [109] [On the Spanning Ratio of the Greedy Triangulation for Convex Point Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09854)
*Prosenjit Bose,Jean Lou de Carufel,Anil Maheshwari,Bobby Miraftab,Michiel Smid,Leonidas Theocharous*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限平面点集的贪婪三角剖分（greedy triangulation），证明了当点集处于凸位置（convex position）时，其跨越比（spanning ratio）具有远小于已知通用常数（约11739.1）的上界 κ < 17.814，即凸点集的贪婪三角剖分是一个18-spanner。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贪婪三角剖分是计算几何中的重要结构，已知其跨越比有通用的常数上界，但标准上界（约11739.1）过于粗略。作者希望针对凸位置点集这一特殊情形，证明一个显著更小的跨越比上界，从而更精确地刻画贪婪三角剖分的几何性能。

Method: 作者利用贪婪三角剖分的结构特性，结合凸位置点集的几何约束，通过构造和分析特定的路径来实现跨越比的估计。与通用情形中使用的菱形（diamond）和良好多边形（good-polygon）性质不同，本文针对凸位置点集设计了更精细的论证，将上界从约11739.1大幅压缩至小于17.814。

Result: 证明了对于任意有限凸位置点集 P⊂ℝ²，贪心三角剖分中任意两顶点 u,v 之间存在长度至多为 κ|uv| 的路径，其中 κ < 17.814。因此凸位置点集的贪婪三角剖分是18-spanner。

Conclusion: 凸位置点集的贪婪三角剖分具有远优于通用情形的跨越比上界（小于18），显著改进此前约11739.1的标准界。这一结果加深了对贪婪三角剖分在特殊点集配置下几何性质的理解。

Abstract: The greedy triangulation of a finite planar point set is obtained by considering all segments in nondecreasing order of length and inserting each segment that does not cross an earlier one. Its spanning ratio is known to be bounded by a universal constant, but the standard bound obtained from the diamond and good-polygon properties is about $11739.1$. We prove a substantially smaller bound for points in convex position. In particular, for every finite point set $P\subset\mathbb{R}^2$ in convex position and every pair $u,v\in P$, the greedy triangulation contains a $u$--$v$ path of length at most $κ|uv|$, where $κ<17.814$. Thus, the greedy triangulation of a convex point set is an $18$-spanner.

</details>

### [110] [SuperEM: A Sub-meV Threshold Detector Architecture for Cosmic Neutrino Background and Dark Matter Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08592)
*Zhenjie Li,Xilei Sun,Xiaoshan Jiang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying (SuperEM)探测器，通过超导吸收体与高密度半导体P-N结雪崩放大器的耦合架构，突破当前辐射探测技术中"不可能三角"（亚meV能量阈值、快速时间响应和大规模可扩展性三者不可兼得）的固有局限，为宇宙中微子背景（CνB）直接探测和亚GeV轻暗物质（LDM）搜索提供可行的新型探测器方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代粒子物理、天体物理和宇宙学对辐射探测提出了极致需求：探测宇宙中微子背景（CνB）、测定绝对中微子质量标度以及搜索亚GeV轻暗物质（LDM），这些都需要亚毫电子伏特（sub-meV）能量阈值、优异的绝对能量分辨率、快速时间响应以及大规模可扩展性。然而现有技术面临固有极限——"不可能三角"——即优化亚meV阈值必然牺牲宏观时间响应或空间可扩展性，亟需突破性的架构设计来绕开这一瓶颈。

Method: SuperEM探测器采用三层耦合架构：超导吸收体提供超低能量阈值，高密度半导体P-N结在强偏置下实现高增益数字化雪崩放大，二者之间通过超薄原子层沉积（ALD）绝缘势垒实现非平衡准粒子的量子隧穿提取。该设计基于先前深度低温雪崩力学的实验验证，并通过信号输运模拟确认了无掺杂界面配合强漂移场可实现高效、纳秒级瞬态电子漂移，整个信号在35 ns内完全解析完毕。

Result: 信号输运模拟表明，所提出的无掺杂界面与强漂移场设计能够支撑高效的纳秒级瞬态电子输运，全部信号可在35 ns内完成解析。该结果从理论上确立了SuperEM探测器完整器件的基础可行性与结构基础，同时满足亚meV能量阈值、高分辨率和快速时间响应的要求。

Conclusion: SuperEM架构代表了一种可规模化、高分辨率且具备快速时间响应的新型探测器框架，为下一代CνB和LDM观测站提供了切实可行的技术路线，有望突破现有辐射探测技术中"不可能三角"的限制，拓展辐射探测的运作战域边界。

Abstract: Expanding the operational boundaries of radiation detection is imperative for contemporary particle physics, astrophysics, and cosmology. At this frontier, the direct detection of the Cosmic Neutrino Background (C$ν$B), the determination of the absolute neutrino mass scale, and the search for sub-GeV Light Dark Matter (LDM) necessitate detector architectures capable of sub-millielectronvolt (sub-meV) energy thresholds, exceptional absolute energy resolution, fast time response, and massive scalability. Current technologies confront an intrinsic limit---the ``impossible triangle''---wherein optimizing for sub-meV thresholds inherently compromises either macroscopic timing response or spatial scalability. Here, we introduce the Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying (SuperEM) detector, a fundamentally novel structural paradigm designed to bypass this limitation. The architecture couples the ultra-low energy threshold of a superconducting absorber with the intrinsic high-gain digitization of a strongly biased, high-density semiconductor P-N junction. Incident energy yields a proliferation of non-equilibrium quasiparticles, which are subsequently extracted via quantum tunneling across an ultra-thin Atomic Layer Deposition (ALD) insulating barrier. Building upon our prior empirical validation of deep-cryogenic avalanche mechanics, this manuscript establishes the fundamental theoretical feasibility and structural foundation of the complete device. Signal transport simulations confirm that an undoped interface coupled with a strong drift field enables highly efficient, nanosecond-scale transient electron drift, resolving completely within 35 ns. The SuperEM architecture thus constitutes a scalable, high-resolution, and fast time-response framework for next-generation C$ν$B and LDM observatories.

</details>


### [111] [Silent coverage failures in rare-event searches and a degeneracy index that predicts them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09203)
*Davide Pagno*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了低本底实验中模型误设定对六种区间估计程序覆盖概率的影响，提出了Poisson-Fisher简并指数来量化这种影响，并论证了与名义模拟器校准无法保护免受模拟器误设定的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低本底实验中信号强度通常非负且事件数极少，常报告上限。名义频率学覆盖需要有效的区间构造和充足的数据模型，但实际中模型可能偏离真实数据生成过程。作者希望系统研究模型偏差如何影响置信区间端点的覆盖概率，并评估现有方法的稳健性。

Method: 在精确Poisson计数实验、暗物质反冲谱和无中微子双β衰变峰搜索三类场景中，对六种区间程序施加受控模型偏离，考察下端点和上端点覆盖。引入Poisson-Fisher简并指数I_PF(δν;ϑ₀)=(β,γ)：β为投影到拟合切空间后的有符号拟合信号偏移（以剖面标准误为单位），γ为未被吸收残差的Poisson-Fisher范数。通过饱和Poisson拟合优度检验（固定5%一类错误率）评估γ的可检测性。

Result: 正信号类偏差会降低发现侧覆盖并使上限保守；由高估信号效率导致的负信号偏差可使上端点欠覆盖。校准于名义模拟器不能防止模拟器相对数据生成过程的误设定。大|β|小γ的形变可能逃避诊断，需以带辅助信息约束的 nuisance 表示或纳入可辩护包络。在精确共线约束nuisance基准中，对形变建模可在评估网格点恢复覆盖，但以区间灵敏度为代价。

Conclusion: 模型偏差对区间端点覆盖有方向性影响，Poisson-Fisher简并指数可有效刻画偏差的可诊断性与威胁端点。研究者应主动建模不可忽略的模型形变，或使用辅助信息约束nuisance，而非仅依赖名义模拟器校准。

Abstract: Searches for new physics in low-background experiments infer a non-negative signal strength from few events and often report an upper limit. Nominal frequentist coverage requires both a valid interval construction and an adequate data model. We study how controlled model departures affect lower- and upper-endpoint coverage for six interval procedures in an exact Poisson counting experiment, dark-matter recoil spectra, and a neutrinoless-double-beta-decay peak search. We introduce the Poisson--Fisher degeneracy index $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{PF}}(δν;\vartheta_0)=(β,γ)$, which maps a specified expected-count deformation, after projection onto the complete fitted tangent space, to $β$, the signed fitted signal shift in profiled standard-error units, and $γ$, the Poisson--Fisher norm of the unabsorbed residual. Locally, the sign of $β$ identifies the threatened endpoint, while larger $γ$ implies greater detectability by the saturated-Poisson goodness-of-fit test used here at a fixed $5\%$ type-I error rate. Across the studied deformations, positive signal-like bias degrades discovery-side coverage while making upper limits conservative; negative signal bias from overestimated signal efficiency can make the upper endpoint undercover. Calibration under the nominal simulator does not protect against misspecification of that simulator relative to the data-generating process. A plausible deformation with large $|β|$ and small $γ$ may therefore evade diagnosis and should be represented by a nuisance constrained with auxiliary information or included in a defensible envelope. In the exactly collinear constrained-nuisance benchmark, modelling the deformation restores coverage at the evaluated grid points, at a quantifiable cost in interval sensitivity.

</details>


### [112] [Amplitude analysis for charmed meson decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09338)
*Han Zhang,Zhenxuan Li,Chunyi Guan,Zehui Lu,Hui Li,Liping Yang,Yechun Yu,Nan Zhang,Minggang Zhao,Yu Lu,Bai-Cian Ke,Liaoyuan Dong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文全面介绍了BESIII合作组在多变体粲介子衰变中采用的振幅分析方法，涵盖无分箱最大似然拟合的概率密度函数与似然函数构建、蒙特卡洛积分归一化、探测效率与分辨率修正、基于XGBoost的多维背景建模，以及强子与半轻子衰变的振幅形式体系和共振传播子参数化，并讨论了拟合分数、运动学投影和统计不确定度估计等关键分析环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振幅分析是连接多变体粲介子衰变实验测量与中间两体过程理论预言之间的桥梁，作者旨在提供一套完整、实用的振幅分析方法论综述，帮助研究者理解和实施这类复杂的分析工作。

Method: 本文系统地梳理并详述了BESIII合作组的振幅分析流程：包括无分箱最大似然拟合中概率密度函数与似然函数的构建、利用蒙特卡洛积分实现归一化、通过探测效率和分辨效应进行修正、借助XGBoost分类器进行多维背景建模，同时涵盖强子和半轻子衰变的振幅形式学及标准共振传播子参数化方法。

Result: 论文提供了一套完整可操作的振幅分析方法框架，对拟合分数的评估、运动学投影的生成以及统计不确定度的估计等关键分析环节均给出了具体的方法说明。

Conclusion: 该综述为粲介子多变体衰变的振幅分析提供了从理论形式到工程实践的全流程指导，兼具方法论的系统性和实施细节的可操作性，对相关领域的实验物理研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Amplitude analysis bridges the gap between experimental measurements of multibody charmed-meson decays and theoretical predictions of intermediate two-body processes. This work presents a comprehensive overview of the amplitude-analysis methodology employed by the BESIII Collaboration, emphasizing practical implementation. We detail the construction of the probability density function and likelihood function for unbinned maximum-likelihood fits. This encompasses Monte Carlo integration techniques for normalization, the incorporation of detection efficiency and resolution effects, and multidimensional background modeling utilizing XGBoost classifiers. Furthermore, we describe the amplitude formalism for both hadronic and semileptonic decays, incorporating standard resonance-propagator parametrizations. Key analytical aspects, including the evaluation of fit fractions, the generation of kinematic projections, and the estimation of statistical uncertainties, are also discussed.

</details>


### [113] [Automated Signal Integrity Analysis Framework for High-Speed Interconnects in the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 Probe Card](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09462)
*D M S Sultan,R. Plackett,A. E. McDougall,A. J. A. Knight,A. S. Rotelli,J. Vossebeld,D. Bortoletto*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复用的MATLAB信号完整性(SI)分析框架，将四端口S参数数据转换为可追踪的链路级证据而非单一损耗指标，并在PPCB-1347-MuPix11探针卡的四个1.25 Gbps差分通道上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SI分析往往仅依赖单一损耗指标（如插入损耗）来评估链路性能，但路径长度和单一指标不足以作为SI排序依据。需要一个将S参数数据系统转化为综合链路级证据（眼图、BER等）的可扩展框架，以支持互连设计的比较分析。

Method: 自动化流程包括：1) 文件结构预检；2) 功率归一化混合模式变换；3) 路由长度感知的损耗分解；4) 因果加载信道模型构建；5) 传播完整PRBS-31序列并进行眼图、条件BER和8b10b编码链路分析。数据来源于PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver的四端口S参数和虚拟时域求解器。

Result: 在1.25 Gbps速率下，DP1-DP4差分插入损耗(SDD21)为-0.350至-0.300 dB，差分转共模转换为-31.038至-28.236 dBc，FEB输入眼开度为0.586至0.588 V。发现路径长度不是充分的SI排序变量：DP3 Nyquist插入损耗最低，DP1差分共模隔离最强，DP4眼图最大。所有BER值均低于报告下限，无法支持BER排序。

Conclusion: 该框架通过区分路由相关波形行为、模型投影和有限记录观测，为高速互连SI比较分析提供了可扩展的基础，适用于从设计评审到校准VNA测量解释的全流程。

Abstract: A reusable MATLAB signal-integrity (SI) framework is presented that converts compatible four-port S-parameter data, measured by VNA or obtained from electromagnetic simulation, into traceable link-level evidence rather than a single loss metric. The framework is demonstrated on the four 1.25 Gbps differential routes (DP1-DP4) of the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 probe card using PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver-derived four-port S-parameters and a virtual time-domain solver. The automated pipeline preflights file structures, performs a power-normalized mixed-mode transformation, applies route-length-aware loss decomposition, constructs a causally loaded channel model, and propagates full PRBS-31 sequences into eye-diagram, conditional-BER, and 8b10b-coded-link analyses. At the 1.25 Gbps data rate (Nyquist 0.625 GHz), DP1-DP4 exhibit differential insertion loss (SDD21) from -0.350 to -0.300 dB, differential-to-common conversion from -31.038 to -28.236 dBc, and modeled FEB-input eye openings from 0.586 to 0.588 V. The comparison shows that path length alone is not an adequate SI ranking variable: DP3 has the lowest Nyquist insertion loss, DP1 the strongest differential-to-common isolation, and DP4 the largest modeled eye. All analytical BER values remain below the reporting floor and therefore do not support a BER ranking. By preserving the distinction between route-dependent waveform behavior, model projections, and finite-record observations, the framework provides an extensible basis for comparative high-speed-interconnect SI analysis from design review through calibrated VNA measurement interpretation.

</details>
