# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [The cosine measure of a function at a point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07716)
*Warren Hare,Gabriel Jarry-Bolduc,Chayne Planiden*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"函数的余弦测度"这一新概念，用于分析当目标函数不可微或梯度为零向量时，一个向量集合是否能保证包含该函数的下降方向。文章给出了相关数学理论、算法及算例，并揭示了关于无穷集合和非凸锥的余弦测度的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 余弦测度在多种优化算法的收敛性理论中具有重要作用，其关键性质是：当函数f在点x处梯度非零时，集合S的余弦测度大于零等价于S必然包含f在x处的下降方向。然而，当函数不可微或梯度为零向量时，这一性质不再成立，目前缺乏相应的理论工具对其进行分析。

Method: 本文引入"函数在一点处的余弦测度"这一新概念，将其定义为：为保证向量集合包含该函数在关注点处的下降方向，集合所需的余弦测度值的下确界。作者围绕这一概念建立了数学理论框架，包括证明光滑函数的余弦测度可取[-1,1]中的任意值；同时给出了计算函数余弦测度的算法，并在光滑与不可光滑函数上进行了算例演示。

Result: 建立了函数余弦测度的理论体系，证明了光滑函数余弦测度可在[-1,1]内取任意值；提出了计算函数余弦测度的具体算法，并在光滑和不可光滑函数上验证了其有效性；相关结果还深化了对无穷向量集合和非凸锥的余弦测度的理解。

Conclusion: 通过引入函数的余弦测度，本文成功地将余弦测度的下降方向保证性质推广到了不可微和驻点情形，丰富了余弦测度的理论体系，并为更广泛的优化算法收敛性分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The cosine measure of a set of vectors in $\mathbb{R}^n$ measures how well the set covers all directions in $\mathbb{R}^n$. It identifies the direction furthest, in angle, from the set. It is used in the convergence theory of various optimization algorithms, but also highlights interesting geometric properties of sets. For example, the cosine measure of a set $S$ is greater than zero if and only if given any $\mathcal{C}^1$ function $f$ at a point $\mathbf{x}$ where the gradient is nonzero, $S$ must contain a descent direction of $f$ at $\mathbf{x}$. In this paper, we examine the question of what can be said when the function $f$ is non-differentiable or if it has a gradient equal to the zero vector. To examine these cases, we introduce the novel concept of the {\em cosine measure of a function} at a point. This value provides an infimum on the value of the cosine measure that a set of vectors requires to guarantee it contains a descent direction of the function at the point of interest. We present mathematical theory around this concept, including examples showing that the cosine measure of a smooth function can have any value in $[-1,1]$. We further present algorithms to compute the cosine measure of a function, and examples demonstrating the algorithm on smooth and nonsmooth functions. These results also shed light on the the cosine measure of infinite sets and nonconvex cones.

</details>


### [115] [Universal Birkhoff Method for Computing Extremals in the Elliptic Restricted Three-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07815)
*Michael J. Dixon,Isaac M. Ross*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆三体轨道优化问题中的有限推力极值弧计算，结合Birkhoff理论与快速无猜谱算法生成并验证极值解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆限制性三体问题中的有限推力轨道优化计算困难，传统方法依赖动力学系统理论的初始化或猜测，缺乏可验证的极值解。

Method: 利用Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto采样点的快速傅里叶变换将平动点轨道近似至接近机器精度；应用Pontryagin原理推导最小时间和时间约束最小推进剂问题的最优性条件；联合普适Birkhoff轨迹优化理论与快速无猜谱算法生成候选最优解，并通过哈密顿量最小化条件和横截条件验证极值性。

Result: Birkhoff谱算法无需动力学系统理论的辅助或初始化即可生成可验证的极值解，候选最优解的极值性通过哈密顿量最小化条件与横截条件得到确认。

Conclusion: Birkhoff理论结合谱方法为椭圆三体问题中的有限推力最优轨迹提供了有效且可验证的计算框架。

Abstract: The computation of finite-thrust extremal arcs in the elliptic restricted three-body trajectory optimization problem is considered. Libration-point orbits are approximated to near-machine precision using a fast Fourier transform of the sampled values of the state vector at Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto points. Checkable optimality conditions are derived by applying Pontryagin's principle to minimum-time and time-constrained minimum-propellant problems. These necessary conditions include criteria for optimal departure and arrival points. For propellant consumption, a recently developed computational model is employed. This model is agnostic to the specific impulse of the propellant and varies as the inverse quadratic of a cosine term. Candidate optimal solutions are generated by combining the universal Birkhoff theory for trajectory optimization with the fast, guess-free spectral algorithm. The extremality of the Birkhoff-computed solution is validated against the Hamiltonian minimization condition and the transversality conditions. It is shown that the Birkhoff-theoretic spectral algorithm can generate verifiable extremals without any assistance or initialization from dynamical systems theory.

</details>


### [116] [The Rank-Collapse Principle for Quadratic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07828)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian,Ahmad Mousavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"秩坍缩原理"（rank-collapse principle），揭示正半定二次优化中，当矩阵Q=BB^T秩较低时，目标函数只通过低维影子y=B^T x依赖变量，最优影子在其自身方向上是唯一的线性泛函最大化器并满足二次边际条件。该原理本身不保证有限候选集，效率还取决于可行族的投影/活动几何，作者通过投影影子散射和活动结构坍缩来区分二者，并统一了区域多面体、凸组合、边骨架、投影正规扇形和固定秩稀疏PCA等方法，给出了二元/有限相位向量、基数约束、拟阵基底和稀疏PCA的tie安全结果及方向稳定性与近似低秩证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次优化在非凸或离散可行集下通常是困难的，但秩较低时存在固定秩的多项式时间精确算法。作者希望揭示这一现象背后的共同正半定几何机制，提供一个统一、严谨的框架来理解和推导这些已知的固定秩可处理性结果。

Method: 从Q=BB^T的正半定分解出发，将目标函数转化为关于低维影子y=B^T x的线性函数。证明任意最优影子y*在其自身方向上唯一最大化线性泛函，并满足定量二次边际。将这一原理与可行族的投影/活动几何相联系，通过"投影影子散射"和"活动结构坍缩"两个概念组织分析，覆盖二元向量、有限相位向量、基数约束、拟阵基底和稀疏PCA等场景，并提供方向稳定性和近似低秩证书。

Result: 建立了秩坍缩原理，给出了最优影子的方向唯一性和二次边际的定量刻画；将原理与多种既有方法（zonotope、凸组合、边骨架、投影正规扇形、固定秩稀疏PCA）建立显式联系；推导了二元和有限相位向量、基数约束、拟阵基底及稀疏PCA的tie安全（避免退化/平局）结果，并提供了方向稳定性与近似低秩证书及可复现实验。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提供一个统一的量化学术框架来理解固定秩二次优化的正半定几何本质，而非声称首创已知的固定秩可处理性结果。其框架为后续将秩坍缩原理推广到更广泛的问题类型奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Quadratic optimization becomes hard as soon as either the matrix in the quadratic form has an unfavorable curvature or the feasible set is discrete, combinatorial, or otherwise nonconvex. A complementary phenomenon is also well known in the signal-processing and optimization communities: when the matrix in the quadratic form has small rank, some hard-looking quadratic programs admit exact polynomial-time algorithms for fixed rank. We study the common positive-semidefinite geometry behind this phenomenon. If $Q=BB^\top$ is positive semidefinite, the objective depends on $x$ only through the rank-space shadow $y=B^\top x$. Every optimal shadow $y^*$ uniquely maximizes the linear functional defined by its own direction and satisfies a quantitative quadratic margin. Thus nonlinear optimality collapses to a low-dimensional, self-generated linear exposure direction. We call this the rank-collapse principle. The principle alone does not imply a finite candidate set: efficient exact optimization additionally depends on the projected or active geometry of the feasible family. We organize this distinction through projected-shadow scattering and active-structure collapse, relate it explicitly to established zonotope, convex-combinatorial, edge-skeleton, projected-normal-fan, and fixed-rank sparse-PCA methods, and derive tie-safe consequences for binary and finite-phase vectors, cardinality constraints, matroid bases, and sparse PCA. We also give directional-stability and approximately low-rank certificates, together with reproducible experiments. The paper's contribution is a unified, careful framework and a set of quantitative consequences, rather than a claim to originate the known fixed-rank tractability results that motivate it.

</details>


### [117] [Zeroth-Order Langevin Monte Carlo via SPSA under Noisy Function Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07837)
*Hongbo Li,James C. Spall*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出LMC-SPSA（带噪声的Langevin Monte Carlo与同时扰动随机逼近）方法，在梯度不可得且函数评估有噪声的采样场景中，利用每次迭代两次带噪函数评估近似目标对数密度的梯度，并在Wasserstein距离下证明了其收敛性，将维度依赖从O(p^4)改进到O(p^2)，给出了W_2精度ε下总噪声预言复杂度O(p/ε²+δ²p³/ε³)，优于ZO-LMC方法，并通过数值实验验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度类采样方法（如LMC）相比非梯度方法混合更快，但实际中许多场景（随机模拟器或黑盒模拟器）无法直接获得目标对数密度的梯度，且函数评估含有噪声，这限制了LMC类方法的适用性。因此需要一种无需显式梯度、仅依赖带噪函数评估就能实现高效采样的方法，并需严格证明其收敛性。

Method: 本文提出LMC-SPSA，利用同时扰动随机逼近（SPSA）的思想，通过每次迭代仅需两次带噪函数评估来近似目标对数密度的梯度。作者在带噪梯度估计下证明了LMC-SPSA在Wasserstein距离下的分布收敛；构造了使Wasserstein误差界收敛的递减步长调度，将收敛保证扩展到常数步长设置之外；通过精细分析将Wasserstein误差的维度依赖从O(p^4)锐化到O(p^2)。

Result: LMC-SPSA达到W_2精度ε的总噪声预言复杂度为O(p/ε²+δ²p³/ε³)，其中δ为成对噪声水平，改善了Roy等人ZO-LMC方法中噪声依赖的精度缩放。在递减步长和扰动序列下建立了迭代次数趋于无穷时Wasserstein误差渐近趋于零的结果，并为平衡调度下的带噪零阶反馈推导了显式收敛速率。数值实验表明，在与ZO-LMC匹配预言预算时，LMC-SPSA在相同的函数评估预算下取得了更小的经验采样误差。

Conclusion: LMC-SPSA在梯度和噪声环境受限的采样问题中提供了高效且理论上严谨的解决方案，不仅改善了收敛复杂度的维度依赖和噪声依赖，还扩展了步长调度的收敛保证范围，并通过实验验证了其在实际采样任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: In sampling problems, gradient-based schemes such as Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC) mix faster than non-gradient-based methods, but their applicability is limited by access to the gradient of the target log-density. In practice, gradients are often unavailable and function evaluations are noisy, e.g., stochastic simulators or black-box simulators, so we propose LMC-SPSA with noise, which approximates the gradient of the target log-density using two noisy function evaluations per iteration. We prove, under noisy gradient estimates, that LMC-SPSA converges in distribution by proving the convergence in Wasserstein distance. Furthermore, we construct a diminishing step-size schedule that still drives the Wasserstein error bound to convergence, extending convergence guarantees beyond the constant-step setting. Further, we sharpen the dominant dimension dependence of the Wasserstein error from $O(p^4)$ to $O(p^2)$ (with $p$ denoting the dimension), and support this analysis with numerical results. We show that LMC-SPSA achieves $W_2$-accuracy $\varepsilon$ with total noisy-oracle complexity of $O(p/\varepsilon^2+δ^2p^3/\varepsilon^3)$, where $δ$ is the paired-noise level. This improves the noise-dependent accuracy scaling relative to the ZO-LMC method of Roy et al. We further establish asymptotically vanishing Wasserstein error as the number of iterations $\to\infty$ under diminishing step-size and perturbation sequences and derive an explicit convergence rate for a balanced schedule under noisy zeroth-order feedback. Empirical experiments are conducted to verify the performance of LMC-SPSA with noise. We provide an oracle-budget-matched comparison with the ZO-LMC method, showing smaller empirical sampling errors under the same function-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [118] [Computing the Maximal Controlled Invariant Set for Neural Network Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07908)
*Tianxiao Ye,Hang Zhang,Xiangru Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络控制系统（NNCS）中最大受控不变集（MCIS）的计算问题。提出一种基于区间的方法，为非标称神经网络残差模型构造控制仿射包含函数，利用输入空间中的超平面排列将受控不变性的验证从无限控制搜索简化为有限代表性评估，并基于此有限特征开发了可并行化的验证引导算法，迭代构造MCIS的内外近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络控制系统在工业应用中日益广泛，但其安全性验证是一个核心挑战。最大受控不变集是形式化验证系统安全性质的关键工具，然而在神经网络控制系统（尤其是带有神经网络残差模型）中计算MCIS非常困难，因为神经网络带来的非线性使得传统方法无法直接应用，且受控不变性验证通常涉及无限控制搜索空间。

Method: 本文提出一种基于区间分析的方法：(1) 构造一个控制仿射包含函数来覆盖NNCS的动力学行为；(2) 利用输入空间中由该包含函数诱导的超平面排列，将受控不变性的验证从无限控制搜索转化为有限个代表性评估点的检验；(3) 基于这一有限特征，设计验证引导的迭代算法来构造MCIS的内近似和外近似，算法天然支持并行化。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，表明该方法能够准确且高效地计算神经网络控制系统最大受控不变集的内外近似。

Conclusion: 通过区间包含函数和超平面排列的有限特征化，本文成功将神经网络控制系统中MCIS的计算问题从无限搜索降维为有限评估，并提供了一系列可并行化的验证引导算法，为NNCS的安全性分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: This article studies the computation of the maximal controlled invariant set (MCIS) for neural network control systems (NNCSs) with a nominal plant model and a neural network residual model. An interval-based method is developed to construct a control-affine inclusion function for the NNCS dynamics and to exploit the induced hyperplane arrangement in the input space, which reduces the verification of controlled invariance from an infinite control search to a finite set of representative evaluations. Based on this finite characterization, verification-guided algorithms iteratively construct inner and outer approximations of the the MCIS, which are naturally parallelizable. Numerical examples demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [119] [Queueing Analysis and Cost Optimization in a Diagnostic--Treatment Hospital Queue with Heterogeneous Referred Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07967)
*Shobha Rani,Rohit Dehru,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对混合医院服务系统（包含首诊患者和转诊患者）建立了一种基于马尔可夫相位型队列的分析与决策框架。系统在两种治疗方案下用概率生成函数求解，任意n种治疗方案下用矩阵解析法求解。作者推导了关键性能指标，设计了负载触发控制以确定满足预设利用率的最小容量增量，并证明总成本问题在倒数服务时间变量下为严格凸函数且具有唯一全局最小解。同时使用粒子群优化、模拟退火和正弦余弦算法作为独立启发式求解器进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院服务系统常需同时处理两类患者：首诊患者（需诊断后治疗）和转诊患者（仅需既定治疗方案）。这两种患者的不同服务路径使得系统建模复杂化，现有文献缺乏对多治疗模式混合患者流的统一分析与决策框架。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提供可解析的性能评估工具和最优容量配置决策方法。

Method: 将系统建模为马尔可夫相位型（phase-type）队列：两种治疗方案情形下推导概率生成函数；任意n种治疗方案情形下开发矩阵解析方法（matrix-analytic method）。设计负载触发控制策略来确定达到预设利用率所需的最小容量增量。成本优化方面，将总成本问题重构为关于倒数服务时间变量的严格凸优化问题，并证明其存在唯一全局最小值；此外采用PSO、SA和SCA三种启发式算法作为独立求解器。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了分析结果的正确性。总成本问题在倒数服务时间变量下被证明是严格凸的且具有唯一全局最小解。三种启发式算法（PSO、SA、SCA）能够独立求解该问题并作为解析方法的对照验证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分析框架为混合医院服务系统提供了一套完整的性能评估与容量优化工具，涵盖从解析建模、性能指标推导、控制策略设计到成本优化的全链路决策支持。严格凸性保证了成本优化问题解的唯一性与全局最优性，启发式算法则提供了灵活的替代求解途径。

Abstract: This study develops an analytical and decision framework for a pooled hospital service comprising first-time patients who require diagnosis followed by treatment and referred patients who require only their prescribed treatment mode. The system is represented as a Markovian phase-type queue. probability generating functions are derived for two-treatment cases, while a matrix-analytic method is developed for an arbitrary number $n$ of treatment modes. The key performance measures are derived and interpreted. A load-triggered control is designed to determine the minimum capacity increment required to achieve a prescribed utilization level. Numerical experiments are conducted to validate the analytical results. Furthermore, the total-cost problem admits a strictly convex reformulation in reciprocal service-time variables and has a unique global minimum. Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Simulated Annealing (SA), and the Sine Cosine Algorithm (SCA) are additionally used as independent heuristic solvers.

</details>


### [120] [A Computable Stochastic Riccati Equations Framework for Mean--Variance Portfolio Selection with Multifactor Stochastic Volatility Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07977)
*Zhecheng Huang,Guojiang Shao,Lei Wang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可计算且可实证实施的连续时间均值-方差最优投资组合选择框架，基于多因子随机波动率市场模型，通过随机Riccati方程刻画最优控制，并设计了基于迭代BSDE的双边逼近算法结合Deep BSDE和DBDP神经网络求解器，在行业ETF数据上验证了其在下行风险控制和回撤管理方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间均值-方差组合选择问题在随机市场系数下缺乏可计算且可实证实施的有效解；随机Riccati方程（SRE）通常缺乏闭式解且数值求解存在奇异性和收敛困难，需要一种兼具理论严谨性与计算可行性的框架。

Method: 构建多因子随机波动率市场模型以捕捉状态依赖风险溢价、随机波动率和动态跨资产相关性；最优控制由随机Riccati方程刻画；设计迭代BSDE过程从上下两个方向逼近SRE，初始上下界通过求解两个线性BSDE获得，再通过对数变换消除SRE的奇异性，使用Deep BSDE和DBDP两种神经网络方法求解变换后的方程，线性BSDE界同时提供高效的初始值估计以加速收敛。

Result: 基于行业ETF数据的实证实验表明，所提多因子均值-方差策略产生平滑的目标收益财富动态，相较于基准策略在回撤控制和下行风险保护方面均有改进。

Conclusion: 研究证明将随机Riccati方程、神经BSDE求解器与多因子市场建模相结合用于动态资产配置具有实践潜力。

Abstract: We investigate a computable and empirically implementable framework for continuous-time mean--variance optimal portfolio selection with random market coefficients. The market model is built on a tractable multifactor stochastic volatility structure, which captures state-dependent risk premia, stochastic volatility, and dynamic cross-asset dependence. The optimal control is characterized by stochastic Riccati equations. On the computational side, we design an iterative BSDE-based procedure to approximate the stochastic Riccati equation from above and below, where the initial upper and lower bounds are obtained by solving two linear BSDEs. We then apply a logarithmic transformation to remove the singularity in the SRE, and solve the resulting transformed equation using both Deep BSDE and DBDP methods. The linear BSDE bounds also provide effective initial-value estimates, improving the convergence speed and training stability of the Deep BSDE solver. Empirical experiments based on sector ETF data show that the proposed multifactor mean--variance strategy produces smooth target-return wealth dynamics, with improved drawdown control and downside-risk protection relative to benchmark strategies. These results demonstrate the practical potential of combining stochastic Riccati equations, neural BSDE solvers, and multifactor market modeling for dynamic asset allocation.

</details>


### [121] [A Game-Theoretic Characterization of Feedback Capability for Fully Coupled Vector-Valued Nonparametric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08028)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究未知函数f和任意有界扰动下离散时间系统x_{t+1}=f(x_t)+u_t+w_{t+1}的反馈镇定问题。作者针对完全耦合的向量值系统，引入响应历史逃逸博弈，利用Borel决定性和斜率单调性定义独立博弈值Γ_d，证明植物问题存在有限严格反馈能力阈值且该阈值等于Γ_d。标量情况下阈值等于3/2+√2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量系统的反馈能力阈值在广义Lipschitz不确定性下已被确定为3/2+√2，但完全耦合的向量值系统无法利用标量序和区间递归，耦合也排除了坐标化约简，因此需要新的理论框架来刻画高维反馈镇定阈值。

Method: 引入响应历史逃逸博弈，其获胜条件要求有界包络以规定斜率增长且状态半径无界；利用Borel决定性和斜率单调性定义博弈值Γ_d；通过均匀次临界响应博弈控制器（下界方向）和基于Kirszbraun-Valentine延拓定理的Hilbert空间实现引理（上界方向）建立阈值等价性；利用最近邻反馈律给出有限维下界，并利用维数单调性和标量理论比较确定Γ_1=3/2+√2。

Result: 证明了植物问题存在有限严格反馈能力阈值并识别为Γ_d：当L<Γ_d时存在单一因果反馈律对所有该类植物和所有有界扰动序列镇定；当L>Γ_d时对任意因果反馈律都存在同类植物和有界扰动使闭环状态无界。显式最近邻反馈律给出任意有限维中严格大于1的下界，包括Γ_2≥2/√3；维数单调性给出Γ_d≤Γ_1，与标量理论比较得到Γ_1=3/2+√2。

Conclusion: 该工作将标量情形的精确反馈能力阈值推广到向量值系统，建立了响应历史逃逸博弈与植物反馈镇定问题之间的严格等价关系，为高维不确定性系统的反馈能力理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study feedback stabilization for the discrete-time system $x_{t+1}=f(x_t)+u_t+w_{t+1}$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with unknown $f$ and arbitrary bounded disturbances. For scalar plants, the sharp feedback capability threshold under generalized Lipschitz uncertainty is $3/2+\sqrt{2}$. We treat fully coupled vector-valued systems, where scalar order and interval recursion are unavailable and coupling precludes a coordinatewise reduction. We introduce a response-history escape game whose winning condition requires a finite envelope at the prescribed slope and an unbounded state radius. Borel determinacy and slope monotonicity give an independently defined game value $Γ_d$. We prove that the plant problem admits a finite strict feedback capability threshold and identify it with $Γ_d$. If $L<Γ_d$, one causal feedback law stabilizes every plant in the uncertainty class against every bounded disturbance sequence. If $L>Γ_d$, then for every causal feedback law there exist a plant in the same class and a bounded disturbance sequence such that the closed-loop state sequence is unbounded. The threshold equivalence is proved through a uniform subcritical response-game controller and a Hilbert-space realization lemma based on the Kirszbraun--Valentine extension theorem. An explicit nearest-neighbor feedback law yields a lower bound strictly above one in every finite dimension, including $Γ_2\ge 2/\sqrt{3}$. Dimension monotonicity gives $Γ_d\leΓ_1$, and comparison with the scalar theory yields $Γ_1=3/2+\sqrt{2}$.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal cost of fast boundary controls for the one-dimensional heat equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08041)
*Pierre Lissy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了热方程在短时间极限下（T→0⁺）的L²零控制最优成本满足精确渐近公式C_H(T,L)=exp((κ_*L²+o(1))/T)，其中κ_*=Γ(1/4)⁴/(8π³)≈0.696601964842838，统一了Lissy的下界与Dardé–Ervedoza的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程零控制成本在T→0时的渐近增长速率是控制理论中的核心问题。此前Lissy（2015）给出下界，Dardé和Ervedoza（2019）给出上界，但两者之间存在间隙，且上界常数以收敛级数形式表达，缺乏封闭形式。本文旨在精确确定该常数并闭合上下界之间的空隙。

Method: 论文考虑(0,L)区间上的热方程，一端为齐次Dirichlet条件，另一端为Dirichlet边界控制。通过精确定估计H^{-1}初始数据下最优L²零控制成本，推导出短时间极限下的精确渐近公式，并证明所得的精确常数κ_*与Dardé–Ervedoza上界中的常数完全一致。

Result: 证明了C_H(T,L)=exp((κ_*L²+o(1))/T)，其中κ_*=Γ(1/4)⁴/(8π³)≈0.696601964842838。该常数以闭式表达，与Dardé–Ervedoza通过收敛级数给出的上界常数相吻合。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底闭合了Lissy下界与Dardé–Ervedoza上界之间的间隙，给出了热方程短时间零控制成本的最优常数精确值，且该常数具有简洁的闭合表达式。

Abstract: We consider the heat equation on $(0,L)$ with homogeneous Dirichlet condition at one endpoint and a Dirichlet boundary control at the other. If \(C_{\mathrm H}(T,L)\) denotes the optimal \(L^2\) null-control cost for initial data in \(H^{-1}(0,L)\), we prove that \[
  C_{\mathrm H}(T,L)
  =
  \exp\left(\frac{κ_*L^2+o(1)}{T}\right),
  \qquad
  κ_*
  =
  \frac{Γ(\frac14)^4}{8π^3}
  \simeq
  0.696601964842838,
  \qquad T\to0^+. \] The constant \(κ_*\) coincides with the upper-bound constant obtained by Dardé and Ervedoza (2019, ANPDE), which was expressed there through a convergent series. This closes the gap between the lower bound obtained in by Lissy (2015, JDE) and the upper bound established by Dardé and Ervedoza.

</details>


### [123] [Learning under Opponent Unawareness in Linear-Quadratic Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08268)
*Dantong Chu,Xuefeng Gao,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究无限时域、非零和线性二次随机博弈中的去中心化学习问题。在极端非耦合信息结构下（玩家只观察公共状态和自身动作历史，对对手完全无知或战略性忽视），玩家各自独立运行单智能体ε-贪心迭代最小二乘算法，证明了该学习动力学几乎必然收敛到完全信息下的纳什均衡，并刻画了收敛速率。应用该框架于粘性价格动态古诺竞争，数值实验表明有限信息下的学习在低和高价格粘性下均降低企业利润，高粘性下总剩余下降、市场集中度上升；公开揭示市场总量产出可显著加速收敛并缓解福利损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着企业将机器学习应用于战略决策，理解算法之间的相互作用已成为运筹学和经济学的核心问题。现有研究大多假设玩家拥有对手的完整信息，但现实中玩家往往对对手的模型和策略一无所知。本文旨在探索在极端有限信息条件下，去中心化强化学习算法是否仍能收敛到完全信息纳什均衡，从而回答"无知玩家能否通过简单的单智能体学习算法达到最优战略结果"这一基础性理论问题。

Method: 本文在无限时域、非零和的线性二次随机博弈框架下，假设玩家仅能观察公共状态和自身动作历史（极端非耦合信息结构），各自独立运行ε-贪心迭代最小二乘（iterated least-squares）单智能体算法进行异步去中心化学习。理论上通过随机逼近和鞅论等工具证明收敛性并刻画收敛速率，随后将该框架应用于粘性价格下的动态古诺竞争模型，并通过数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了在无法辨识系统参数的情况下，玩家的学习动力学几乎必然收敛到完全信息纳什均衡，并给出了收敛速率刻画。数值实验显示：有限信息下的学习在低和高价格粘性下均降低企业利润；高价格粘性时总剩余下降且市场集中度上升。公开揭示市场总量产出信息（一种有限信息增强手段）能显著加速收敛并缓解福利损失。

Conclusion: 本文表明，即使在极端缺乏对手信息的条件下，简单的单智能体ε-贪心迭代最小二乘学习算法也能使多智能体系统收敛到完全信息的纳什均衡，这为去中心化算法博弈论提供了理论基础。同时，研究揭示了信息结构对企业利润、社会福利和市场结构的影响，并指出公共信息的透明披露有助于改善学习效率和福利效果，对平台和市场设计具有监管启示。

Abstract: As firms increasingly deploy machine learning for strategic decision-making, understanding algorithmic interactions has become central to operations research and economics. This paper studies learning in infinite-horizon, nonzero-sum linear-quadratic stochastic games under a radically uncoupled information structure, where players are either unaware of opponents or strategically oblivious, observing only a common state and their own action history. Under this minimal information, we analyze an asynchronous decentralized learning process in which each player independently runs a single-agent $ε$-greedy iterated least-squares algorithm. We prove that, despite being unable to identify the system parameters, players' learning dynamics converge almost surely to the complete-information Nash equilibrium and characterize the convergence rate. We then apply the framework to a dynamic Cournot competition with sticky prices. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical results and show that learning under limited information reduces firm profits under both low and high price stickiness, while total surplus declines and market concentration increases when price stickiness is high. Publicly revealing aggregate market output substantially accelerates convergence and mitigates these welfare losses.

</details>


### [124] [Stability of MIMO PID With Backward Differences Under Fast Sampling: An Exact Spectral Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08318)
*Zhaobo Liu,Yanyi Wu,Peng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在多变量状态空间系统中，即使采样周期任意小，使用后向差分实现PID控制器的微分项也可能导致不稳定。精确的提升模型揭示了由存储的先前输出采样所创建的快速特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后向差分是PID控制中微分作用的标淮数字实现方式，但现有理论并未充分分析在多变量状态空间系统中该实现方式在极小采样周期下的稳定性边界，需要澄清这一潜在的不稳定问题。

Method: 通过构造精确的提升（lifted）模型来分析数字PID控制器中后向差分实现的稳定性质，识别出由存储的先前输出采样产生的快速特征值，并建立稳定性等价条件。

Result: 证明了无论采样周期多小，后向差分实现都可能导致不稳定。稳定性条件等价于：在远离边界谱的情况下，理想回路稳定性加上输出矩阵、输入矩阵和微分增益矩阵乘积的Schur稳定性。

Conclusion: 后向差分在多变量PID控制中的稳定性不能仅靠缩小采样周期来保证；需要额外的代数条件（输出、输入和微分增益矩阵乘积的Schur稳定性）来确保整体稳定性。

Abstract: Backward differences are a standard digital realization of derivative action in proportional-integral-derivative control. This note proves that, for multivariable state-space plants, this implementation can be unstable no matter how small the sampling period is. The exact lifted model reveals fast eigenvalues created by the stored previous output sample. Away from boundary spectra, stability is equivalent to ideal loop stability plus Schur stability of the product of the output, input, and derivative gain matrices.

</details>


### [125] [Output tracking: an application for irrational SISO transfer functions and a survey for MIMO systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08342)
*Lucas Davron*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维线性时不变（LTI）MIMO系统与无理SISO传递函数的输入-输出映射的值域刻画问题。在有限维情形下系统性地收集了主要结果，并针对无理SISO传递函数的跟踪问题改进了已有结论，使得能够完整描述L^2(0,∞)输入下的系统输出，最后将其应用于系数为多项式的热方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维MIMO系统输入-输出映射值域的理论虽然令人满意但文献较为分散，需要系统性整理；同时无理SISO传递函数的跟踪问题已有结果尚不够完善，无法完全描述L^2输入下的输出空间。

Method: 在有限维情形下，系统收集并综述输入-输出映射值域的主要理论成果；对于无理SISO传递函数，改进已有的跟踪问题结果，从而实现对L^2(0,∞)输入下输出的完整刻画，并将其应用于带多项式系数的热方程。

Result: 给出了有限维MIMO系统输入-输出映射值域的全面综述，并改进了无理SISO传递函数的跟踪问题结果，实现了对L^2输入下输出的完整描述；该理论成功应用于多项式系数热方程。

Conclusion: 论文一方面整合了有限维系统的值域理论，另一方面扩展了无理SISO系统的输出刻画能力，并通过热方程实例验证了方法的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper we consider (MIMO) finite dimensional linear and time-invariant systems and irrational single-input single-output (SISO) transfer functions. In both cases our aim is to describe as best as possible the range of the input-output map $u(\cdot) \mapsto y(\cdot)$. The theory in finite dimension is quite satisfying but rather scarce, the first aim of this paper is to collect the main results in this direction in a comprehensive way. Our second aim is to improve a recent result on the tracking problem for irrational SISO transfer functions, allowing one to completely describe the outputs of such system for inputs $u \in L^2(0,\infty)$. An application is given for a heat equation with polynomial coefficients.

</details>


### [126] [Halpern Iteration Achieves $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1/p})$ $p$th-Order Oracle Complexity for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08463)
*Lesi Chen,Xinliang Zhang,Hengyu Wang,Chengchang Liu,Yongchao Chen,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的大步长非精确Halpern迭代方法（Halpern-NPE），用于求解光滑单调变分不等式，实现了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$的收敛速率，并进一步推广到$p$阶方法（ATM结合Halpern迭代），达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$的速率，全面改进了已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调变分不等式（MVI）的高阶方法收敛速率是一个开放问题。已有工作中，Monteiro和Svaiter提出的NPE方法达到$\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$，而凸-凹极小极大优化（MVI的子集）最近被改进至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1.75})$。作者希望突破这一瓶颈，探索是否存在更快的收敛速率。

Method: 提出了一种大歩长非精确Halpern迭代（large-step inexact Halpern iteration）并据此设计了Halpern-NPE方法。首先引入锚定张量方法（Anchored Tensor Method, ATM）实现$\mathcal{O}(T^{-(p-1)})$的速率，再将其与Halpern迭代结合，通过加速机制将速率提升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$。

Result: Halpern-NPE方法在MVI上达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$的收敛速率，优于之前的$\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$和$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1.75})$结果。$p$阶推广方法达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$的速率，对所有$p \ge 2$均优于此前所有结果，且在$p=1$时与经典的额外梯度法（extragradient method）的速率匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Halpern迭代与大歩长非精确策略的结合，首次将MVI的收敛速率提升至接近最优的理论水平，为单调变分不等式的高阶加速方法提供了新的理论框架和通用范式的系统性改进。

Abstract: We study second- and higher-order methods for solving smooth monotone variational inequalities (MVI). Monteiro and Svaiter (SIAM J. Optim., 2012) showed that a second-order method, NPE, converges at the rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$. For convex-concave minimax optimization, a subset of MVI problems, Chen, Liu, Luo, and Zhang (COLT 2025) recently improved the complexity to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( T^{-1.75})$ . However, it is open whether the conjectured complexity for MVI can be improved. In this paper, by using a large-step inexact Halpern iteration, we propose a novel Halpern-NPE method that achieves an even faster rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$ for solving MVIs. We also provide the $p$th-order generalization of our method. We first introduce an Anchored Tensor Method (ATM) that achieves the rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-(p-1)})$, and then combine it with the Halpern iteration to achieve a faster convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$. This improves all prior results for $p \ge 2$ and matches the classical extragradient method for $p=1$.

</details>


### [127] [Max-$k$-Cut via Node Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08499)
*Avinash Bhardwaj,Hritiz Gogoi,Vishnu Narayanan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从节点特征视角研究Max-k-Cut问题，提出了SDP松弛精确性的充分条件，并将问题等价转化为向量平衡问题，进而证明贪心特征平衡算法保持1-1/k近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Max-k-Cut问题研究主要从图的结构（如边权）出发，忽略了节点可能携带的特征向量信息。当边权由节点特征的成对内积决定时，这一特殊结构可能带来更强的理论保证和更高效的算法。

Method: 1) 利用法锥（normal-cone）论证，推导Frieze-Jerrum SDP松弛精确性的一般充分条件；2) 识别两种特征结构域（特征平衡与特征主导）满足该条件；3) 将Max-k-Cut目标等价转化为最小化k个部分聚合特征向量范数平方之和；4) 提出贪心特征平衡算法，并借助Chandra-Wong经典界对秩1非负特征图给出后验最优性间隙证书。

Result: 贪心特征平衡算法保持了经典的1-1/k最坏情况近似比，并在特征主导条件下恢复最优划分；对秩1非负特征图，可获得仅依赖返回划分的、可计算的后验最优性间隙证书。

Conclusion: 从节点特征视角研究Max-k-Cut问题不仅揭示了SDP松弛精确性的新结构条件，还通过向量平衡联系提供了具有理论保证的高效贪心算法，为特征图上的图划分问题开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We study the Max-$k$-Cut problem from a node-feature perspective, where each vertex is associated with a feature vector and edge weights are given by pairwise inner products. We first examine the semidefinite relaxation of Max-$k$-Cut from this perspective. Using a normal-cone argument, we derive a general sufficient condition for exactness of the Frieze--Jerrum relaxation and show that it is satisfied in two feature-structural regimes: perfect feature balance, where the aggregate feature vectors of the parts are equal, and feature dominance, where a small set of large nonnegative feature vectors determines the structure of an optimal partition. We then show that the Max-$k$-Cut objective is equivalent to minimizing the sum of squared norms of the aggregate feature vectors assigned to the $k$ parts, thereby connecting the problem to vector balancing. Motivated by this observation, we show that a greedy feature-balancing algorithm retains the classical $1-1/k$ worst-case approximation guarantee and recovers an optimal partition under feature dominance. For rank-$1$ feature graphs with nonnegative features, classical bounds of Chandra and Wong for greedy load balancing yield a computable \emph{a posteriori} optimality-gap certificate that depends only on the returned partition and requires no knowledge of the optimum.

</details>


### [128] [A Unified Maximum-Margin Framework for Data Envelopment Analysis: Theoretical Foundations and Numerical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08530)
*Teruki Akahoshi,Hiroki Ueda,Tomonari Kitahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的最大间隔DEA模型（MM-DEA），将机器学习中的结构风险最小化思想引入效率评估框架，通过排除目标DMU并对其分数设置明确上界，解决了经典CCR模型在高维数据下区分能力不足的问题，确保所有得分保持在[0,1]范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CCR-DEA模型在输入输出变量数量相对DMU数量较大时，缺乏区分能力，导致大量DMU被评价为效率值为1的"CCR有效"，难以对最佳执行者进行排序或区分。这一局限性严重影响了DEA方法在高维场景下的实际应用价值。

Method: MM-DEA模型借鉴机器学习中的"间隔最大化"概念（源于结构风险最小化），将其直接整合到效率测度框架中。与超效率模型类似，MM-DEA从用于评估目标DMU的参考集中排除该DMU；但不同于超效率模型，MM-DEA对目标DMU自身的得分施加了明确的上界，确保所有得分保持在标准[0,1]范围内。论文还展示了MM-DEA的分数规划表达式可以严格转化为线性规划问题。

Result: 理论上证明了MM-DEA是CCR模型的广义扩展，当权衡参数α为零时退化为CCR模型。数值实验中，MM-DEA在高维合成数据集上产生了唯一排名，而CCR模型在该数据集上无法区分任何DMU。

Conclusion: MM-DEA有效解决了高维条件下CCR模型的区分力不足问题，产生的"间隔"可作为管理韧性和竞争优势的关键指标，具有良好的理论性质和计算效率。

Abstract: Data Envelopment Analysis (DEA) is a widely used non-parametric method for evaluating the relative efficiency of decision-making units (DMUs). However, the classic Charnes-Cooper-Rhodes (CCR) model often suffers from a lack of discriminatory power, particularly when the number of input and output variables is large relative to the number of DMUs. Under such conditions, many DMUs are evaluated as "CCR-efficient" with a score of unity, making it difficult to rank or distinguish between top performers. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Maximum-Margin DEA (MM-DEA) model that integrates the concept of "margin maximization" -- inspired by structural risk minimization in machine learning -- directly into the efficiency measurement framework. Like the traditional super-efficiency model, the proposed MM-DEA model excludes the target DMU from the reference set used to evaluate it; unlike the super-efficiency model, however, MM-DEA imposes an explicit upper bound on the target DMU's own score, ensuring that every score remains within the standard $[0,1]$ range rather than potentially exceeding one. We theoretically demonstrate that the MM-DEA model is a generalized extension of the CCR model, reducing to the latter when the trade-off parameter $α$ is zero. Furthermore, we show that the fractional programming formulation of MM-DEA can be rigorously transformed into a linear programming (LP) problem, ensuring computational efficiency. In our numerical experiments, MM-DEA produced a unique ranking for a high-dimensional synthetic dataset on which the CCR model failed to discriminate among any of the DMUs. Our results suggest that the "margin" serves as a critical metric for managerial resilience and competitive advantage.

</details>


### [129] [Integrated Learning and Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08718)
*Cheng Tan,Yuchen Mao,Shuming Wang,Huan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成学习与鲁棒优化的框架(ILRO)，将鲁棒决策问题同时用作训练损失函数和部署决策，实现鲁棒性与学习-决策对齐的双重目标，并证明了其理论性质与计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多运营决策需要在线性规划中求解未知成本向量，需从上下文信息中预测。传统方法将预测与决策分离，导致两者仅弱对齐。虽然集成学习与优化(ILO)范式通过下游问题训练预测器，但预测不可避免地存在不精确性，决策阶段的鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 提出ILRO框架，采用鲁棒决策问题同时定义训练问题(RSPO损失)和部署决策。开发凸替代损失RSPO+并刻画其Fisher一致性条件；利用RSPO损失的梯度信息设计一阶计算方法；推导RSPO和RSPO+预测器的有限样本超额风险界。

Result: 在交通和投资组合问题上的数值实验表明，相比多个基准方法，所提框架在决策质量上具有优势，尤其在样本有限、高维决策以及模型误设场景下增益更为显著。

Conclusion: ILRO框架通过将鲁棒优化嵌入集成学习流程，同时实现了预测-决策对齐与鲁棒性，在理论上具备一致性和样本复杂度保证，在计算上可通过一阶方法高效求解，为不确定性下的数据驱动决策提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Many operational decisions require solving a linear program whose cost vector is unknown at decision time and must be predicted from contextual information. Because prediction and decision are only weakly aligned, the emerging integrated learning and optimization (ILO) paradigm trains the predictor through the downstream problem, judging a prediction by the decision it induces. However, predictions are inevitably imprecise, so robustness often enters the decision stage. To address this issue, we propose an integrated learning and robust optimization (ILRO) framework, where a robust decision problem is used both to define the training problem (termed the RSPO loss problem), and to produce the deployed decision. Thus, this framework simultaneously achieves both robustness and learning-decision alignment. To tackle its computational challenges, we develop a convex surrogate, RSPO+, and characterize when it is Fisher consistent. Moreover, the RSPO loss possesses informative gradients, allowing us to develop first-order computational methods. We also derive finite-sample excess risk bounds for both RSPO and RSPO+ predictors. Numerical experiments on transportation and portfolio problems, in comparison with multiple benchmarks, show the advantage in decision quality of the proposed framework. The gain is more pronounced for scenarios with limited samples, high-dimensional decisions, and model misspecification.

</details>


### [130] [Simultaneous Group-Envelope Bounds for $Γ$-Robust Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08861)
*Zi Yuan Eric Shao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多选择背包松弛问题的批量求解方法，通过消去阈值特定基线并将每组贡献简化为两个包络，实现对整个阈值族的统一有界计算，在不损失精度的前提下大幅提升鲁棒规划问题的求解速度，平均加速2.37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多鲁棒规划问题需要对每个不确定性阈值重复构建和求解普通优化问题（多选择背包松弛），这一过程在计算上非常昂贵，主导了整体运行时间。现有方法缺乏一种能够同时处理整个阈值族的高效手段。

Method: 提出一种批量有界技术：当决策从每组中恰好选择一个选项时，利用消去法去除阈值特定的基线项，将每组贡献约简为两个简单包络。排序后，一个乘子可在所有阈值上以阈值数量的线性时间和选项数量的近似线性时间完成求值。生成的区间界有效，在单一可行阈值上与普通松弛匹配，且至少与匹配的组-团比较器一样强。采用有认证的一维搜索控制乘子误差，并用精确检查解决数值上模糊的可行性与比较情形。

Result: 在完全相同的自适应搜索框架下，该方法在所有60个实例级别的中位数对比中全部胜出，几何平均加速达2.37倍。额外的消融实验、独立线性规划校验以及从公开存档转换的九个实例验证了机制的有效性。另有整数审计表明其边界：当整数搜索占主导时，更快的松弛界并不一定有用。

Conclusion: 提出的批量区间界方法能够在不牺牲有效性和强度的前提下显著加速基于阈值族的鲁棒规划求解，但需注意其优势在整数搜索占主导的场景下会受到限制。

Abstract: Many robust planning problems are solved by checking a family of ordinary optimization problems, one for each uncertainty threshold. Repeatedly building and solving those relaxations can dominate runtime. We show that, when a decision chooses exactly one option from each group, the entire threshold family of multiple-choice knapsack relaxations can instead be bounded together. A cancellation removes threshold-specific baselines and reduces each group's contribution to two simple envelopes. After sorting, one multiplier is evaluated across all thresholds in time linear in their number and nearly linear in the number of options. The resulting interval bound is valid, matches the ordinary relaxation on a single feasible threshold, and is at least as strong as the matched group-clique comparator. A certified one-dimensional search controls multiplier error, while exact checks resolve numerically ambiguous feasibility and comparison cases. In an otherwise identical adaptive search, the method wins all 60 instance-level median comparisons and achieves a 2.37-fold geometric-mean speedup. Additional ablations, independent linear-program checks, and nine instances transformed from a published archive test the mechanism beyond the internal generator. A separate integer audit shows the boundary: faster relaxation bounds need not help when integer search dominates.

</details>


### [131] [Regional factors determine the feasibility of an elimination strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08956)
*George Adu-Boahen,Troy Day,Michael J Plank,Amy Hurford*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过显式的数学模型刻画地区特征与流行病学结果之间的关系，评估在公共卫生资源约束下，通过病例隔离和接触者追踪等定向措施实现消除或抑制策略的可行性，并证明最优控制是立即以最大力度实施公共卫生措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情管理中不同地区采用不同策略（消除、抑制、缓解），但缺乏对地区特征如何决定策略可行性的数学刻画。本文旨在明确回答：哪些地区特征组合使得消除或抑制策略可行，从而为政策制定提供理论依据。

Method: 构建了一个感染者可来自旅行者或社区成员传播的流行病学模型，并在公共卫生资源（隔离到达旅行者和感染社区成员的能力）有限约束下，通过最优控制理论推导最优策略，并分析不同地区特征（公共卫生容量、传播率、旅行者到达率）组合下的可行性条件。

Result: 证明了最优控制为立即以最大力度实施公共卫生措施。消除或抑制策略在公共卫生容量高、传播率低或感染者到达率低时可行。旅行措施直接预防的社区感染数量有限，但通过保护接触者追踪容量可间接防止疫情复活，大幅减少疫苗或疗法开发前的总感染人数。

Conclusion: 地区特征组合（公共卫生容量、传播率、旅行者到达率）共同决定消除、抑制或无复发的缓解策略是否可行。低旅行者到达率且有隔离/检疫执行能力的地区更不易超出接触追踪与隔离能力上限。实施缓解策略的国家未必能通过改为消除策略取得更好结果，因为消除策略在低容量、高传播或高旅行者到达率的地区可能根本不可行。

Abstract: We provide an explicit mathematical characterization of regional characteristics and the resulting epidemiology to evaluate whether elimination or suppression is feasible using targeted measures, such as case isolation and contact tracing. We derive an epidemic model where community members can be infected by either travellers or other community members, and with constraints on the public health resources available to support isolation of arriving travellers and infected community members. We prove that the optimal controls are to immediately implement public health measures at their maximum levels. We find that elimination or suppression strategies are feasible with targeted measures if public health capacity is high, the transmission rate is low, or if the arrival rate of infected travellers is low. Our results illustrate that a particular country that implemented a mitigation strategy during the pandemic may not necessarily have achieved better outcomes if it had instead implemented elimination because elimination may not have been feasible in countries with low public health capacity to support control measures, high transmission rates, or high traveller arrival rates. Combinations of regional characteristics determine whether an elimination, suppression or mitigation strategy without resurgence is feasible. We find that regions with the low traveller arrival rates and the capacity to implement and enforce isolation or quarantine of arriving travellers are less likely to exceed their capacity to contact trace and isolate community members. Travel measures prevent only a small number of community infections via their direct effect, but indirectly, by protecting contact tracing capacity, travel measures can prevent epidemic resurgence and substantially reduce the number of people infected during a pandemic before a vaccine or therapy is developed.

</details>


### [132] [Direct Search Methods for Online Nonconvex Optimization Under Inexact Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08979)
*Gaspar Robert,Gianluca Bianchin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非凸时变优化的随机两点直接搜索算法，在常数和递减探测比例下推导了迭代复杂度界，并将直接搜索方法从静态设置扩展到动态环境，同时展示了其在一类最优控制问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶（bandit）反馈优化对许多工程问题至关重要，但现有方法大多局限于针对时不变或一阶优化方法的直接改编，依赖的梯度替代品未能充分利用零阶反馈的结构信息。现代应用（如在线控制和在线学习）中的优化问题随时间演化，需要自适应优化方法，因此需要专门为时变零阶优化设计的方法。

Method: 提出一种随机两点直接搜索算法，专门针对非凸时变优化问题。算法在常数和递减两种探测比例下运行，进行理论分析推导出迭代复杂度界，并显式地以问题的时间变化程度和可能的预言机误差来刻画驻点界。复杂度分析表明在时不变设置下能恢复现有零阶方法的复杂度，同时将直接搜索方法推广到时变场景。

Result: 推导出在常数和递减探测比例下的迭代复杂度界，给出了以时间变化性和预言机误差表示的显式驻点界。在时不变情况下恢复现有零阶方法的复杂度结果，并扩展了直接搜索方法的应用范围。在一类最优（均衡选择）控制问题中，展示了该方法能自然地求解动态系统控制问题，并得到了以植物动态和外部扰动变化效应衡量的显式驻点界。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机两点直接搜索算法为时变非凸零阶优化提供了首个专门设计的直接搜索方法，其理论与应用分析证明该方法在动态环境下具有明确的理论保证，并能有效解决最优控制中的均衡选择问题。

Abstract: Optimization under zeroth-order (i.e., bandit) feedback is central to many engineering problems where the analytic forms of objectives and/or constraints are unavailable. In modern applications, such as online control and online learning, optimization problems often evolve with time, requiring adaptive optimization methodologies. Yet, existing methods in this seting are largely confined to adaptations of methodologies developed for time-invariant or first-order optimization, and thus often rely on gradient surrogates that fail to fully exploit the zeroth-order structure of the available information. In this paper, we propose a randomized two-point direct-search algorithm for nonconvex time-varying optimization and derive iteration-complexity bounds under both constant and diminishing probing ratios. The resulting analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem and possible oracle errors. Our complexity bounds recover the complexity of existing zeroth-order methods in the time-invariant setting, while extending direct- search methods beyond static settings. As an illustrative application, we show that the methodology is naturally suited to solve optimal (equilibrium-selection) control problems for dynamical systems. In this setting, the analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem, measured through the effects of plant dynamics and exogenous disturbance variations.

</details>


### [133] [A Tight Lower Bound for Smooth Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization with Bounded Gradient Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09004)
*Jikai Jin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在均匀有界梯度噪声条件下，光滑非凸随机优化问题的一个严格下界。在K=1新鲜样本模型中，任何随机自适应算法找到期望梯度范数不超过ε的点，至少需要Ω(ΔL/ε² + ΔLσ²/ε⁴)次查询。该结果匹配标准上界，解决了Arjevani等人2023年提出的问题：几乎必然有界的oracle误差是否比有界方差允许更好的速率。答案是否定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arjevani等人2023年提出了一个重要问题：在随机非凸优化中，如果oracle误差是几乎必然有界的（而非仅方差有界），是否可能获得比现有有界方差框架下更好的收敛速率。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 采用信息论/复杂度理论方法，在均匀有界梯度噪声的假设下，构造对抗性实例并证明任意随机自适应算法的查询复杂度下界。证明分为两个下界项分别处理：ΔL/ε²项与ΔLσ²/ε⁴项。

Result: 证明了对于均匀有界梯度噪声的平滑非凸随机优化，在K=1新鲜样本模型下，任何随机自适应算法都需要Ω(ΔL/ε² + ΔLσ²/ε⁴)次查询，这匹配标准上界。结果是锐利（sharp）的。

Conclusion: 该结果回答了Arjevani等人2023年提出的开放问题：即使在更强的几乎必然有界噪声假设下，也不能获得比有界方差假设更好的速率。下界与上界匹配，表明现有算法在这类问题上已经是最优的。此外，论文披露证明由GPT-5.6 Sol辅助生成，人类作者仅负责提示、验证和润色。

Abstract: We prove a sharp lower bound for smooth nonconvex stochastic optimization with uniformly bounded gradient noise. In the \(K=1\) fresh-sample model, every randomized adaptive algorithm requires $$Ω\left( \frac{ΔL}{ε^2} + \frac{ΔLσ^2}{ε^4} \right)$$ queries to find a point with expected gradient norm at most \(ε\). This matches the standard upper bound and, to the best of our knowledge, resolves the question raised by [Arjevani et al. 2023] of whether almost-surely bounded oracle error permits a better rate than bounded variance. The proof was independently generated with GPT-5.6 Sol in Codex's Ultra mode during a two-hour session. The human author supplied the prompt and was responsible only forchecking the proof and revising and polishing the manuscript.

</details>


### [134] [Radial-type error bounds for semidefinite feasibility problems without strict feasibility: qualitative estimates and asymptotic tightness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09013)
*Mitsuhiro Nishijima,Guoyin Li,Bruno F. Lourenço*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性的框架，用于在无需Slater条件（严格可行性）的情况下推导半定可行性问题的显式误差界。通过引入径向型Hölder误差界，结合面对约简（facial reduction）和面对残差函数（facial residual functions），获得了无约束规格的定性径向Hölder误差界，并补充了Sturm的经典工作，提供了常数显式估计。文中还分析了维度渐近行为，并将结果应用于半定规划最优性系统的误差界推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半定可行性问题误差界结果大多依赖于Slater条件（严格可行性），而在许多实际应用中该条件不成立。在缺乏严格可行性的背景下，已有的误差界理论与方法非常有限，亟需建立不依赖约束规格的显式误差界框架。

Method: 核心方法是引入径向型Hölder误差界，其中误差界常数通过径向模函数（radial modulus functions）显式依赖于参考矩阵的范数。具体步骤包括：(1) 将面对约简与近年来发展的面对残差函数相结合，获得模函数的显式描述；(2) 推导无约束规格的定性径向型Hölder误差界；(3) 分析误差界在矩阵空间维度增长时的渐近行为；(4) 将半定规划的最优性系统重新表述为可行性问题，并在严格互补条件下建立显式误差界。

Result: 获得了不依赖任何约束规格的径向型Hölder误差界，补充了Sturm的局部Hölder误差界结果，提供了有界集上常数的显式估计。在维度渐近分析中，识别了误差界可渐近紧（直至维度无关常数）的区间。在应用层面，基于严格互补条件推导了半定规划最优性系统的径向误差界，无需通常的解唯一性假设，并通过显式例子证明了渐近紧性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架从根本上拓展了半定可行性问题误差界的适用范围，消除了对Slater条件和解唯一性的依赖。这些结果不仅在理论上完善了Hölder误差界理论，也为半定规划的数值分析和灵敏度分析提供了实用的误差估计工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a systematic framework for deriving explicit error bounds for semidefinite feasibility problems without assuming strict feasibility (Slater's condition), a setting in which existing results are limited. Our main technical contribution is the introduction of radial-type Hölder error bounds, where the error bound constant depends explicitly on the norm of the reference matrix through radial modulus functions. By combining facial reduction with recently developed facial residual functions, we obtain explicit descriptions of these modulus functions, yielding qualitative radial-type Hölder error bounds without imposing any constraint qualifications. Our results complement the classical work of Sturm by providing explicit estimates for the constants involved in Sturm's local Hölder error bounds over bounded sets with a given size. We further analyze the asymptotic behavior of these bounds as the dimension of the underlying matrix space grows, identifying regimes in which they can be asymptotically tight up to a dimensional-free constant. As an application, we establish explicit error bounds for the optimality system of semidefinite programs by reformulating them as feasibility problems, a setting where Slater's condition typically fails. Under the generically satisfied strict complementarity condition, we derive radial-type error bounds without assuming the usual solution uniqueness requirement, and demonstrate their asymptotic tightness through an explicit example.

</details>


### [135] [Provable Parameter-Free Fixed-Point Algorithms with Linear Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09022)
*Quoc Tran-Dinh,Pham Ngoc Anh,Ha Manh Tien*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对压缩映射的可证明的参数无关和自适应不动点算法，包括完全无参数的Halpern迭代变体、仅需压缩因子上界的自适应Halpern方法，以及结合Tikhonov正则化和Nesterov加速的扩展版本，均具备显式线性收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不动点算法通常需要预先知道压缩因子或通过线搜索、二分法等辅助手段来保证收敛，这限制了算法的实用性和效率。本文旨在设计无需任何先验参数估计的自适应算法，自动利用问题中潜在的压缩性，同时保持与经典方法相当的计算成本。

Method: 本文设计了两种核心算法：(1) 完全参数无关的Halpern不动点迭代变体，无需线搜索、二分法或压缩因子的先验估计；(2) 自适应Halpern方法，仅需压缩因子的上界，并在非扩张情形下退化为现有自适应Halpern方案。此外，通过将不动点方案与Tikhonov正则化结合处理co-coercive方程，并利用Halpern迭代与Nesterov加速方案的关系推导出参数无关的加速变体。

Result: 两种核心算法均建立了固定点残差和到唯一不动点距离的显式线性收敛速率。参数无关的Tikhonov-正则化方法在求解co-coercive方程时达到O(ε^{-1} ln(ε^{-1}))的迭代复杂度。数值实验表明，所提算法与现有自适应不动点方法相比具有竞争力，且通常在压缩问题中表现更好，同时在不扩张问题上依然有效。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了在无需压缩因子先验知识的情况下自动利用隐藏压缩性的参数无关不动点算法，并在理论上保证了线性收敛速率，实践上优于或持平现有方法，为自适应不动点迭代提供了新的理论和方法框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop provable parameter-free and adaptive fixed-point algorithms for contractive mappings, with an emphasis on automatically exploiting hidden contractivity without requiring prior knowledge of the contraction factor. Our first method is a completely parameter-free variant of the Halpern fixed-point iteration. It requires no line search, bisection, or prior estimate of the contraction factor, while retaining essentially the same per-iteration computational cost as classical fixed-point schemes. We establish explicit linear convergence rates for both the fixed-point residual and the distance to the unique fixed point. The second algorithm is an adaptive Halpern method that requires only an upper bound on the contraction factor and reduces to an existing adaptive Halpern scheme in the nonexpansive case. This method also enjoys explicit linear convergence guarantees. We further extend these ideas in two directions. First, by combining the proposed fixed-point schemes with Tikhonov regularization, we obtain a parameter-free method for solving co-coercive equations and establish an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}({ε^{-1}\ln(ε^{-1})})$ for computing an $ε$-solution. Second, using the relation between Halpern iterations and Nesterov's accelerated fixed-point schemes, we derive parameter-free Nesterov's accelerated variants that inherit linear convergence in the contractive setting. Numerical experiments on several examples demonstrate that the proposed algorithms are competitive with, and often outperform, existing adaptive fixed-point methods. In particular, the methods successfully exploit contractive behavior when it is present while remaining effective on nonexpansive problems.

</details>


### [136] [GPU-Accelerated Conic Quadratic Programming with Local Linear Convergence under Strict Complementarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09159)
*Hongpei Li,Yicheng Huang,Huikang Liu,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出PDHCG-CQP，一个GPU加速的一阶求解器，用于大规模锥凸二次规划。它支持多种锥约束，基于重启的均值PDHG方法，采用不精确原始更新和投影梯度迭代，在8个GPU上可扩展处理多达4.4×10^8个原始坐标的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模锥凸二次规划在实际应用中广泛存在（如QCQP、SOCP、Fisher均衡等），但现有的一阶求解器在鲁棒性和可扩展性方面存在瓶颈。需要一种既高效（利用GPU并行）又鲁棒（收敛性保证）的求解器来应对超大规模问题。

Method: 核心是重启的均值原始-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法，通过投影梯度迭代不精确求解锥二次近端子问题。提出了在平滑原始-对偶间隙上的均匀局部二次增长条件下，精确和不精确原始近端评估的重启均值方案具有局部线性收敛。通过旋转二阶锥提升结合局部原始-对偶正则性条件，证明该条件在严格互补性下成立。实现上结合了矩阵自由线性代数、批处理锥投影、自适应内求解、反射-Halpern加速和全设备驻留KKT残差计算，并通过二维数据划分支持多GPU执行。

Result: 在标准和大规模QP、QCQP、SOCP以及拟线性Fisher均衡基准上的广泛实验表明，PDHCG-CQP在一阶求解器中达到最先进的鲁棒性，同时高效扩展到8个GPU和高达4.4×10^8个存储原始坐标的实例。

Conclusion: PDHCG-CQP作为开源工具（https://github.com/Lhongpei/PDHCG），展示了一阶方法在GPU大规模并行环境下处理锥凸二次规划的潜力，在鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均达到先进水平。

Abstract: We present PDHCG-CQP, a GPU-accelerated first-order solver for large-scale conic convex quadratic programming. PDHCG-CQP supports affine constraints and Cartesian products of nonnegative, second-order, rotated second-order, exponential, and three-dimensional power cones. At its core is a restarted averaged primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method, whose primal update is computed inexactly by solving a conic quadratic proximal subproblem with projected gradient iterations. We establish local linear convergence of the restarted averaged scheme with both exact and inexact primal proximal evaluations under a uniform local quadratic-growth condition on the smoothed primal-dual gap. We further show that this condition holds under strict complementarity by exploiting a rotated second-order-cone lifting together with local primal and dual regularity conditions. Our C/CUDA implementation combines matrix-free linear algebra, batched cone projections, adaptive inner solves, reflected-Halpern acceleration, and fully device-resident KKT residual computations. It also supports multi-GPU execution through a two-dimensional partitioning of the problem data. Extensive experiments on standard and large-scale quadratic programming (QP), convex quadratically constrained quadratic programming (QCQP), second-order cone programming (SOCP), and quasilinear Fisher equilibrium benchmarks demonstrate that PDHCG-CQP achieves state-of-the-art robustness among first-order solvers while scaling efficiently to 8 GPUs and instances with up to $4.4\times10^8$ stored primal coordinates. PDHCG-CQP is open source and available at https://github.com/Lhongpei/PDHCG.

</details>


### [137] [Plant-Wide Hierarchical Electricity-Heat Coordination for Large-Scale Cold-Region ReP2H Plants via Bidirectional Thermal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09170)
*Yiwei Qiu,Baiping Zhu,Tao Wu,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou,Kaigui Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向寒冷地区大规模可再生能源制氢（ReP2H）工厂的全厂热拓扑结构和分层电-热管理框架，通过堆栈集群与工厂热力公用系统（PTUS）之间的双向热耦合，实现预热、热备用和废热回收，并将分钟级生产调度与秒级热调控协调起来。基于中国北方80 MW工厂的案例研究表明，该框架全年消除冷启动，氢气产量提高1.50%，能量效率和㶲效率分别提高0.99和4.33个百分点，平准化制氢成本降低3.22%，同时减少热疲劳损伤和启停引起的电压退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寒冷地区的大规模可再生能源制氢工厂在频繁启停运行中面临启动时间过长和反复热应力问题，根本原因在于碱性电解堆、辅助设备（BoP）、工厂热力公用系统（PTUS）和厂房建筑之间缺乏协调的热管理，导致热损失严重、冷启动频繁且设备寿命受损。

Method: 提出全厂热拓扑结构（即在堆栈集群与PTUS之间建立双向热耦合，支持预热、热备用和废热回收）以及分层电-热管理框架（将分钟级生产调度与秒级热调控进行协调，实现多时间尺度的一体化管理）。

Result: 基于中国北方80 MW工厂的案例验证表明：全年消除冷启动；氢气产量提升1.50%；能量效率和㶲效率分别提升0.99和4.33个百分点；平准化制氢成本降低3.22%；热疲劳损伤和启停诱导的电压退化均显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的全厂热拓扑与分层电-热管理框架能有效解决寒冷地区ReP2H工厂频繁启停导致的启动慢、热应力大等问题，在提升产氢量、能效和经济效益的同时延长设备寿命，为寒冷地区大规模制氢厂提供了一种实用的整体热管理解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale renewable power-to-hydrogen (ReP2H) plants in cold regions suffer from prolonged startup and repeated thermal stress during frequent startup-shutdown operation. The situation becomes worse due to the lack of coordinated heat management among the alkaline electrolysis stacks, balance of plant (BoP), plant thermal utility system (PTUS), and plant building. This paper presents a plant-wide thermal topology and a hierarchical electricity-heat management framework to address the issues. Bidirectional thermal coupling between the stack cluster and PTUS enables preheating, thermal standby, and waste heat recovery, while minute-scale production scheduling is coordinated with second-scale thermal regulation. Case studies based on an 80 MW plant in Northern China show that the proposed framework eliminates cold startups in year round, increases hydrogen yield by 1.50, improves energy and exergy efficiencies by 0.99 and 4.33 percentage points, respectively, and reduces the levelized cost of hydrogen by 3.22%. It also reduces thermal fatigue damage and startup-shutdown-induced voltage degradation.

</details>


### [138] [A primal--dual interior-point method for nonsymmetric conic optimization with conjugate-free scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09206)
*Rui-Jin Zhang,Wenhao Fu,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共轭无关（conjugate-free）缩放矩阵的原始-对偶内点法，用于非对称锥优化。缩放矩阵通过原始障碍Hessian的单割线BFGS更新获得，无需共轭障碍导数，适用于高维非对称锥。方法嵌入齐次自对偶预测-校正框架，并通过分裂中心路径邻域证明缩放矩阵与原始障碍Hessian一致可比，从而获得O(√ν log(1/ε))的迭代界，优于此前O(ν log(1/ε))的结果。数值实验在算子透视谱图和量子相对熵锥上验证了与专用求解器QICS的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称锥优化中，传统的共轭障碍（conjugate barrier）导数在高维情形下可能无法以闭式形式获得或计算代价高昂，这限制了多割线BFGS缩放方法的应用。本文旨在开发一种不依赖共轭障碍导数的缩放方法，以扩展内点法在高维非对称锥优化中的适用性，同时保持最优的迭代复杂度。

Method: 提出基于单割线BFGS更新原始障碍Hessian的共轭无关缩放矩阵，将其嵌入齐次自对偶预测-校正内点法框架。采用分裂中心路径邻域分别控制锥变量和标量齐次变量，从而证明缩放矩阵与原始障碍Hessian的一致可比性，并利用该比较界建立邻域保持性质及互补性度量和线性残差的一致收敛速率。

Result: 方法达到O(√ν log(1/ε))的迭代复杂度界，改进了Badenbroek和Dahl的O(ν log(1/ε))结果，与内点法最佳已知复杂度阶数一致。在算子透视谱图锥和量子相对熵锥的数值实验中，该方法与专用求解器QICS具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文证明共轭无关的单割线BFGS缩放结合分裂中心路径邻域可以获得最优迭代复杂度，为高维非对称锥优化提供了一种无需共轭障碍导数的实用且理论完备的内点法方案。

Abstract: We develop a primal--dual interior-point method for nonsymmetric conic optimization based on a conjugate-free scaling matrix. The scaling is obtained from a single-secant BFGS update of the primal barrier Hessian. In contrast to multi-secant BFGS scalings, it does not require conjugate-barrier derivatives. This feature is important for high-dimensional nonsymmetric cones, where conjugate-barrier derivatives may be unavailable in closed form or expensive to compute. We embed the conjugate-free scaling in a homogeneous self-dual predictor--corrector framework. Using a split central-path neighborhood that separately controls the conic variables and the scalar homogeneous variables, we prove that the scaling matrix remains uniformly comparable to the primal barrier Hessian. This comparison bound is used to prove neighborhood preservation and to show that the complementarity measure and the linear residual decrease at a uniform rate. Consequently, the method attains an iteration bound of $\mathcal{O}(\sqrtν\log(1/\varepsilon))$, improving the $\mathcal{O}(ν\log(1/\varepsilon))$ bound of Badenbroek and Dahl [Optim. Methods Softw., 37 (2022), pp. 1027--1064] and matching the best-known complexity order for interior-point methods. Numerical experiments on instances involving the operator perspective epigraph cone and the quantum relative entropy cone show that the method is competitive with QICS, a specialized solver for conic models arising in quantum information.

</details>


### [139] [Entropic Partial Optimal Transport and Partial Gromov--Wasserstein Distance between Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09265)
*Toshiaki Yachimura,Xiaocheng Zou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对高斯混合模型发展了带熵正则的部分最优传输理论，定义了部分混合Gromov-Wasserstein距离，证明了解的存在唯一性、建立大惩罚定量估计，并通过数值实验验证了其对离群点的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输和Gromov-Wasserstein距离的平衡公式强制要求所有质量都被匹配，这在对含有离群点、缺失部分或仅有部分重叠的数据建模时约束过强，需要发展部分匹配框架。

Method: 论文将部分最优传输与熵正则化结合应用于高斯混合模型：对有限熵部分最优传输问题证明最小化解的存在唯一性并建立大惩罚定量估计；通过高斯最优映射，熵部分分量耦合诱导连续部分传输计划，分析其大惩罚和零熵极限，构造位移插值和重心投影映射；将每个高斯混合模型视为有限度量测度空间，建立部分混合Gromov-Wasserstein距离的度量性质和大惩罚极限。

Result: 证明了有限熵部分最优传输问题最小解的存在唯一性和大惩罚定量估计；建立了部分混合Gromov-Wasserstein距离的度量性质与大惩罚极限；在合成高斯混合模型和点云上的数值实验展示了惩罚和熵正则化的效果以及部分匹配对离群点的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 部分匹配框架结合熵正则化能够有效处理含离群点或部分重叠的数据，为高斯混合模型之间的比较提供了理论完备且数值稳健的工具。

Abstract: Optimal transport and Gromov--Wasserstein distances are useful tools for comparing probability measures and metric measure spaces, but their balanced formulations force all mass to be matched. This constraint is often too strong for data with outliers, missing parts, or only partial overlap. In this paper, we develop entropic partial optimal transport for Gaussian mixture models and define a partial mixture Gromov--Wasserstein distance. For the finite entropic partial optimal transport problem, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the minimizer and establish quantitative large-penalty estimates. Moreover, the resulting entropic partial component couplings induce continuous partial transport plans through Gaussian optimal maps. We analyze their large-penalty and subsequent zero-entropy limits and construct the associated displacement interpolations and barycentric projection maps. In addition, by identifying each Gaussian mixture with a finite metric measure space of Gaussian components, we establish the metric property and large-penalty limit of the partial mixture Gromov--Wasserstein distance. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic Gaussian mixtures and point clouds illustrate the effects of the penalty and entropic regularization and the robustness of partial matching to outliers.

</details>


### [140] [On the Hautus test for exact observability of normal semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09407)
*Jannik Daun,Birgit Jacob*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文否证了修正后的Russell-Weiss猜想：即使T相似于压缩半群且A是正规算子（因此T为压缩半群），满足无限维Hautus检验的(A,C)也不一定精确可观测。但证明了对于自伴算子A，Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性；对于正规算子A且C为紧算子，Hautus检验蕴含A有紧预解式，从而在有限维观测空间Y下Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Russell和Weiss原先猜想：若(A,C)满足无限维Hautus检验，则(A,C)精确可观测。该猜想在反例出现后被修正，增加了"T相似于压缩半群"这一假设。本文旨在检验这个修正后的猜想是否成立，并探索在更一般的条件下（如A为自伴或正规算子）Hautus检验与精确可观测性之间的关系。

Method: 构造反例：选取正规算子A，X具有A的特征向量的标准正交基，C:X_1→Y为Hilbert-Schmidt算子，且T指数稳定（因A正规，故T为压缩半群）。该反例满足修正后猜想的所有假设但不满足精确可观测性。同时，通过谱分析和紧性论证分别证明自伴随情形下Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性，以及正规算子情形下Hautus检验蕴含A有紧预解式。

Result: (1) 构造了反例，否证了修正后的Russell-Weiss猜想——即使A正规且T为压缩半群，(A,C)满足Hautus检验也不一定精确可观测。(2) 证明了对于自伴算子A，Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性。(3) 证明了若A正规且C:X_1→Y紧，则Hautus检验蕴含A有紧预解式，进而当Y有限维时Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性。

Conclusion: 修正后的Russell-Weiss猜想仍不成立；但通过限定A为自伴算子或正规且C为紧算子的情形，可以恢复Hautus检验蕴含精确可观测性的结论。这表明精确可观测性与算子A的谱结构密切相关，压缩性假设本身不足以保证Hautus检验与精确可观测性的等价。

Abstract: Let $T$ be an exponentially stable strongly continuous semigroup on a Hilbert space $X$, $A$ its generator, $X_1$ the domain of $A$ with the norm $\|v\|_1 := \|Av\|$, and $C$ an admissible observation operator taking values in a Hilbert space $Y$. Russell and Weiss conjectured that if $(A,C)$ satisfies the infinite-dimensional Hautus test, then $(A,C)$ is exactly observable. After a counterexample was found their conjecture was modified to include the additional assumption that $T$ is similar to a contraction semigroup. We disprove this modified conjecture by constructing a counterexample in which $A$ is normal, $X$ has an orthonormal basis of eigenvectors of $A$ and $C: X_1 \to Y$ is Hilbert-Schmidt. Since $A$ is normal and $T$ is exponentially stable, it follows that $T$ is a contraction semigroup. In contrast, we prove that the Hautus test implies exact observability for self-adjoint $A$. Finally, we show that if $A$ is normal and $C:X_1 \to Y$ is compact, then the Hautus test implies that $A$ has compact resolvent. Consequently, for normal $A$ and finite-dimensional $Y$ the Hautus test implies exact observability.

</details>


### [141] [Linear Convergence of a Frank-Wolfe-type Method over the Spectrahedron without Strict Complementarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09569)
*Dan Garber*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在谱面体上进行光滑凸最小化的Frank-Wolfe类方法，仅依赖极端特征向量计算。作者在先前工作的基础上，去除了严格互补假设、随机性、预热阶段和需要光滑度常数等限制，在二次增长和所有最优解同秩的假设下，提出了一种确定性且无参数的Frank-Wolfe类方法，实现了全局的、与环境维度无关的线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作虽然首次实现了环境维度无关的线性收敛率，但该方法存在多个局限性：需要强严格互补假设、是随机方法、线性收敛率仅在预热阶段后以期望形式成立，且需要知道目标函数的平滑度常数。消除这些限制是提升方法实用性和理论完备性的关键。

Method: 作者提出了一种确定性的、无参数的Frank-Wolfe类方法。该方法的前提假设为：目标函数满足二次增长条件，且所有最优解具有相同秩。在这些假设下，不再需要严格互补性假设，也不需要预先知道目标函数的平滑度常数，方法依然能实现全局的、与环境维度无关的线性收敛速率。

Result: 所提出的方法在仅依赖极端特征向量计算的前提下，实现了确定性的、全局的、与环境维度无关的线性收敛速率，同时消除了先前方法中严格互补假设、随机性、预热阶段和参数依赖等所有局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在二次增长和最优解同秩的温和假设下，Frank-Wolfe类方法可以在不需要额外强假设的条件下实现理想的线性收敛性能，显著推广了先前工作的适用场景。

Abstract: We consider smooth convex minimization over the spectrahedron using Frank-Wolfe-type methods based only on extreme-eigenvector computations. In our recent work \cite{garber2026randomized} we presented the first ambient-dimension-independent linear convergence rate under quadratic growth. However, the method makes an additional strong strict complementarity assumption, it is randomized, its linear rate holds only after a burn-in phase and in expectation, and it requires the objective smoothness constant. We show that these limitations can be removed. Assuming quadratic growth and that all optimal solutions have the same rank, but without assuming strict complementarity, we give a deterministic and parameter-free Frank-Wolfe-type method with a global ambient-dimension-independent linear convergence rate.

</details>


### [142] [Input convex neural networks as surrogates in mathematical optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09707)
*Yu Liu,Jan Kronqvist,Fabricio Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文主张在优化问题中使用输入凸神经网络（ICNN）替代传统的ReLU前馈神经网络（FNN）作为代理模型。ICNN在函数近似凸或凹的场景下具有计算优势，包括更紧的LP松弛、无整数间隙、基于epigraph的LP重表述，以及凸包构造的可行性。作者开发了分支定界算法在输入变量上直接分支，并在人道主义粮食援助、油井路由和葡萄酒调配等案例中验证了ICNN在保持精度同时提升求解速度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ReLU前馈神经网络嵌入优化问题时，其混合整数规划（MIP）重构虽然精确但随着网络规模增大会变得计算密集。作者希望找到结构上更优的代理模型架构，在保持近似精度的同时获得更好的计算性能。

Method: 1) 提出ICNN作为代理模型的框架，利用输入凸架构获得更紧的LP松弛。2) 利用epigraph表示的LP重表述嵌入ReLU激活，但指出该嵌入并非总是精确。3) 非精确时利用ICNN性质构造凸包（下界为epigraph，上界为凹包络），这是box域上最强的连续松弛。4) 开发分支定界算法，在每个节点构建该松弛，直接在输入变量而非MIP中的中间变量上分支，当epigraph嵌入有效时在根节点终止。

Result: 在人道主义粮食援助、油井路由和葡萄酒调配三个案例研究中，ICNN代理模型在精度上与FNN相当，同时显著提升了求解时间和可扩展性，验证了ICNN在凸、凹或可近似的场景中作为默认代理模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 当底层函数是凸、凹或能被良好近似为凸凹函数时，ICNN应作为优化中代理模型的默认选择，其在松弛质量、求解速度和可扩展性方面优于传统FNN-MIP方法。

Abstract: Embedding trained neural networks as surrogates within optimisation problems is an established practice in operations research. The prevailing approach uses feedforward neural networks (FNNs) with ReLU activations, whose piecewise-linear structure admits an exact but computationally intensive mixed-integer programming (MIP) reformulation as the networks grow. We advocate input convex neural networks (ICNNs) as structurally superior surrogates when the underlying response is approximately convex or concave. The convex architecture offers two computational advantages. First, the ICNN-MIP formulation tends to yield a tighter linear programming (LP) relaxation than its FNN-MIP counterpart, with no integrality gap in favourable instances. Second, ICNNs uniquely admit an LP-based reformulation via epigraph representations of ReLU activations, though this embedding is not always exact. When it is not, we exploit the properties of ICNNs to construct the strongest continuous relaxation over box domains, namely, the convex hull of the ICNN's graph, bounded below by the epigraph and above by the concave envelope; this construction is tractable under input convexity but hard for general ReLU networks. On this basis, we develop a branch-and-bound algorithm that builds this relaxation at each node, branches directly on input variables rather than intermediate variables as in MIP reformulations, and terminates at the root node whenever the epigraph embedding is valid. Case studies on humanitarian food aid, oil well routing, and wine blending show that ICNN surrogates match FNN accuracy and deliver gains in solve time and scalability, supporting ICNN as the default surrogate when the underlying function is convex, concave, or well-approximated as such.

</details>


### [143] [Stochastic Multistage Constellation Reconfiguration Problem: Stochastic Dual Dynamic Integer Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09738)
*Brycen D. Pearl,Hang Woon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机多阶段星座重构问题（Stochastic MCRP），用于卫星观测任务调度，并采用随机对偶动态整数规划（SDDiP）进行求解，以应对观测目标的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统星座重构问题的研究假设任务环境是确定性的，即在先验已知环境信息下进行规划；然而实际观测目标在不同时间点本质上存在不确定性。为提升卫星运行效率并确保观测任务的充分执行，调度必须将不确定性纳入考虑。

Method: 将多阶段星座重构问题（MCRP）扩展为随机变体，采用随机对偶动态整数规划（SDDiP）求解，并与多种其他随机问题求解技术进行对比。

Result: 通过两组计算实验（随机轨道目标与模拟飓风情景）验证了SDDiP方法在解决该问题上的有效性，且优于其他随机问题求解方法。

Conclusion: 随机MCRP能够在满足可见时间窗口和机动可行性的约束下，有效应对目标随机性，为卫星观测任务调度提供了可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: Observation tasking is critical to satellite operations, enabling observation of planetary phenomena, orbital debris monitoring, and space domain awareness. To improve the effectiveness of satellite operations, orbital maneuverability is introduced as a leading-edge concept of operations within constellation reconfigurability for response to dynamic events. Previous investigations regarding constellation reconfigurability consist of deterministic mission environments, in which the formulations consider a priori knowledge of the environment; however, observation objectives inherently involve uncertainty at any given time. As such, scheduling satellite operations must account for uncertainties to ensure adequate observation tasking. In response, we present a stochastic variant of the Multistage Constellation Reconfiguration Problem (MCRP) which is solved using Stochastic Dual Dynamic Integer Programming (SDDiP). We additionally explore other stochastic problem-solving techniques for solution quality comparison. To demonstrate each solution method, two computational experiments with stochastic target properties are conducted. The first concerns random orbital targets, and the second concerns simulated hurricanes. The results of the experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the SDDiP solution approach over other stochastic problem-solving methods. Overall, the stochastic MCRP accounts for target stochasticity while obeying visible time windows and maneuver feasibility.

</details>


### [144] [A globally and superlinearly convergent QO-free method for nonlinear optimization on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09755)
*Chunming Tang,Hao He,Wen Huang,Jinbao Jian,Ruyun Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流形上带等式和不等式约束优化问题的无二次规划（QO-free）方法。每轮迭代通过解决三个共享线性算子的线性系统确定主搜索方向，并通过求解降维线性最小二乘子问题获得高阶校正方向以规避Maratos效应，随后在切空间内执行Riemannian弧搜索生成新迭代点。在适当假设下，证明了算法的全局收敛性和强收敛性，并证明了单位步长最终会被弧搜索接受，从而建立超线性收敛。数值实验表明该方法比其他现有方法更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QO-free方法在欧氏空间中是非线性约束优化问题的强大高效算法，但尚未被推广到流形上的优化问题。现有的流形相关文献假设Maratos效应不会出现，这在实际问题中往往不成立。因此需要将QO-free方法扩展到流形设定，并通过高阶校正处理Maratos效应。

Method: 每轮迭代求解三个共享线性算子的线性系统来获得主搜索方向；再通过求解一个降维线性最小二乘子问题得到高阶校正方向，用于规避Maratos效应；在当前迭代点的切空间内执行Riemannian弧搜索以生成新迭代点。

Result: 在适当假设下建立了算法的全局收敛性和强收敛性；证明了弧搜索最终会接受单位步长，从而建立超线性收敛；数值实验表明所提方法与现有方法相比具有很强的竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将QO-free方法从欧氏空间扩展到流形设定，解决了带等式和不等式约束的流形优化问题，并通过高阶校正方向克服了Maratos效应，实现了全局、强以及超线性收敛的完整理论保证。

Abstract: The quadratic optimization-free (QO-free) method is a class of powerful and effective algorithms for solving nonlinearly constrained optimization problems in Euclidean spaces. The aim of the present work is to extend this method to solve optimization problems on manifolds with additional equality and inequality constraints. We first present a specific algorithm in the manifold setting. At each iteration, three linear systems sharing a common linear operator are solved to determine the master search direction. In addition, a higher-order correction direction is obtained by solving a reduced linear least squares subproblem to circumvent the Maratos effect which is assumed not to arise in existing related literature. A Riemannian arc search is then performed within the tangent space of the current iterate to generate the new iterate. Under appropriate assumptions, we establish the global and strong convergence of the proposed method. Moreover, we prove that the unit step size will eventually be accepted by the arc search, upon which the superlinear convergence of the algorithm is established. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method is very competitive compared with other existing approaches.

</details>


### [145] [ADPSO-ERLS: A Hybrid Discrete PSO with Enhanced Local Search for the Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09786)
*Anier Soria-Lorente,Jean-Marie Vilaire,Junior Michel,Lázaro Alvarado-Barrios*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADPSO-ERLS，一种用于旅行商问题（TSP）的离散群智能算法，将探索与局部强化之间的搜索预算分配作为显式可调的设计变量。算法结合记忆引导的交换变异、异构初始化、进化过程中的候选受限2-opt局部搜索，以及最终对最优解的候选受限/全量2-opt加双桥扰动的精化。在统一的Rust实现、相同硬件和严格10万次候选解评估预算下，ADPSO-ERLS在五个对称TSPLIB实例上均取得最低最优值和均值成本，Friedman检验排名第一，25个Wilcoxon对比全部显著优于基线。消融实验表明初始化、进化中局部搜索和最终精化提升质量，候选限制大幅降低运行时间（最高约38倍）。在最多16,862个城市的扩展实验中，最优tour偏差保持在6.7%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSP是研究基于种群的算法如何在固定搜索预算下分配探索与逐步增强的局部强化（intensification）之间权衡的典型场景。现有方法往往未将此分配作为显式可调的设计变量，作者希望提出一个将这一分配显式化、可调节的离散群算法，以便系统研究该权衡。

Method: ADPSO-ERLS采用PSO启发的框架（保留个体记忆与全局记忆），但摒弃了速度、惯性和加速度系数等传统PSO组件。核心组件包括：(1) 记忆引导的交换变异；(2) 异构初始化；(3) 进化过程中的候选受限（candidate-restricted）2-opt局部搜索；(4) 仅对最优解（incumbent-only）的最终精化，结合候选受限或全量2-opt加double-bridge扰动。所有算法统一用Rust实现，在同一硬件上运行，并以严格的10万次候选解评估为一致预算，墙钟时间单独报告。

Result: 在5个对称TSPLIB实例（EUC_2D整数约定）上各运行50次，ADPSO-ERLS在所有实例上取得最低best和mean成本，best-tour Gap为1.93%-4.17%，相对误差为3.04%-5.50%。Friedman检验排名第一，25个经多重比较控制的Wilcoxon检验全部支持其优势，且分布分离程度接近完全。配对消融实验（共用随机种子）表明初始化、进化中局部搜索和最终精化均贡献质量提升；候选限制主要节省运行时间，最高约38倍。在最多16,862个城市的扩展实验中，best-tour Gap保持在6.7%以下，最大实例在11分钟内求解完成。

Conclusion: ADPSO-ERLS通过将探索与强化之间的预算分配显式建模为可调设计变量，在固定评估预算下取得了优于对比算法的解质量，同时候选限制机制在几乎不影响解质量的前提下大幅降低运行时间。实验结果验证了该设计思路的有效性，并为群智能方法在组合优化中的预算分配问题提供了有价值的参照。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem is a canonical setting for studying how a
  population-based method should allocate a fixed search budget between
  exploration and progressively stronger local intensification. We propose
  ADPSO-ERLS, a discrete swarm algorithm that treats this allocation as an
  explicit, tunable design variable. It couples memory-guided swap mutation,
  heterogeneous initialization, selective candidate-restricted 2-opt during
  evolution, and an incumbent-only final refinement combining candidate-restricted
  and optional full 2-opt with double-bridge perturbations. The method is
  PSO-inspired, using personal and global memories yet dispensing with velocity,
  inertia, and acceleration coefficients. All six algorithms are implemented in
  Rust, run on identical hardware, and stopped at a strict, recorded limit of
  100,000 candidate-solution assessments, so that programming language, hardware,
  and evaluation budget are held common across methods; wall-clock time is
  reported separately because equal assessment counts need not correspond to
  equal arithmetic work. Over 50 runs on five symmetric TSPLIB instances under the integer
  \texttt{EUC\_2D} convention, ADPSO-ERLS attains the lowest best and mean cost on
  every instance, with best-tour Gap of $1.93$--$4.17\%$ and relative error of
  $3.04$--$5.50\%$. It ranks first under the Friedman test, and all twenty-five
  multiplicity-controlled Wilcoxon comparisons favor it with large, near-complete
  distributional separation. A paired ablation with common seeds links
  initialization, in-run local search, and final refinement to quality gains,
  while candidate restriction chiefly cuts runtime, by up to a factor of roughly
  $38$. Further experiments up to $16{,}862$ cities keep best-tour Gaps below
  $6.7\%$, solving the largest case in under eleven minutes.

</details>


### [146] [A Singular Control Problem for Data Center Electricity Cost Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09794)
*Rene Carmona,Xiaoyu Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对一个包含运行最大值（running maximum）成本项的随机控制问题进行了完整分析，该问题源于数据中心面临峰值电费并参与需求响应计划的场景。作者将问题转化为闭楔形区域上带边界反射的奇异随机控制问题，证明了值函数的局部Lipschitz连续性，推导了动态规划原理与HJB方程的粘性解理论，并提供了数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心在实际运营中面临两类冲突的经济激励：峰值电费（基于运行最大用电负荷）和需求响应计划（鼓励降低峰值负荷）。这一实际问题需要数学上严谨的随机控制框架来处理成本函数中包含运行最大值的复杂情形，而现有文献对此类问题的完整分析尚属空白。

Method: 将运行最大值纳入系统状态，将原问题转化为平面闭楔形区域上的奇异随机控制问题，边界部分带有反射。利用间隙单调性估计（gap-monotonicity estimate）和确定性的总变差界建立值函数的局部Lipschitz连续性；对普通渐进可测控制推导动态规划原理与粘性公式；HJB方程包含负载方向的Neumann反射条件和对角边界上的斜边界条件；通过粘性意义下解的存在性和比较原理论证唯一性。

Result: 证明了值函数在空间上局部Lipschitz连续；建立了HJB方程的粘性解存在性以及比较定理（给出对应粘性类中的条件唯一性）；数值结果展示了数据中心预期成本中两种冲突激励之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为带运行最大值的随机控制问题提供了完整的理论分析框架，包括正则性、动态规划原理、粘性解理论与数值验证，为数据中心能源管理中的峰值负荷优化问题提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: The goal of the paper is to provide a complete analysis of a stochastic control problem when the cost to minimize involves the running maximum of the underlying controlled state process. The model is motivated by the electricity cost minimization of a data center facing coincident peak charges and possibly benefiting from participation in a demand-response program. From a mathematical standpoint, the main challenge comes from the inclusion of the running maximum in the state of the system forcing the original problem into a singular stochastic control problem on a closed wedge in the plane with reflection on parts of the boundary. We prove that the value function is locally Lipschitz continuous in space using a gap-monotonicity estimate for coupled reflected loads and a deterministic total-variation bound for the difference of two running maxima. The dynamic programming principle and viscosity formulation are then derived for ordinary progressively measurable controls. The corresponding HJB equation includes a reflecting Neumann condition on the lower boundary for the load direction as well as an oblique boundary condition on the diagonal part of the boundary of the domain due to the effect of the running-maximum process. We prove existence of a solution in the viscosity sense as well as a comparison theorem providing conditional uniqueness in the corresponding viscosity class. Finally we provide numerical results illustrating the trade-off between the two conflicting incentives comprising the expected cost of the data center.

</details>


### [147] [Vehicle Platooning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09864)
*Zhi-Long Chen,Nicholas G. Hall*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究道路网络上不同路段长度条件下的车辆编队排序与重排序问题，以最小化总能耗和最小化最大能耗为两个基本目标，提供了完整的计算复杂性分类，并为若干难解情况设计了全多项式时间近似方案和具有可证明性能保证的启发式算法，计算实验表明启发式解平均在最优解的1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆编队驾驶可显著降低能耗、减少排放、提升道路利用率和安全性。随着智能驾驶技术进步，编队规模将大幅增加，使得高效的车队排序和重排序变得日益重要，但该问题在多变的路段长度和能耗目标下的计算复杂性尚不清楚。

Method: 对车辆编队排序与重排序问题在不同车辆和道路特征组合下进行计算复杂性分析，开发多项式时间算法和不可判定性证明；对若干难解情形设计全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）和具有可证明性能保证的多项式时间启发式算法；针对位置相关能量节省信息有限的情况，设计具有有界最坏情况性能的启发式；对仅允许有限位置变化的道路重排序问题提出高效算法。

Result: 提供了完整的计算复杂性分类；设计的启发式算法在计算实验中平均达到最优解的1%以内；在有限信息条件下提出了具有有界最坏情况性能的启发式；为有限位置变化的道路重排序问题设计了高效算法。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同构成了一个全面的能源高效车辆编队排序与重排序的算法框架，为实际应用中的车队调度和道路重排序提供了系统的理论支撑和实用的求解方法。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning offers significant benefits, including reduced energy consumption, lower emissions, improved road utilization, enhanced safety, and reduced driver fatigue. As intelligent driving technologies continue to advance, platoon sizes are expected to increase substantially, making the efficient sequencing and resequencing of vehicles increasingly important. We study the vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing problem on road networks with varying segment lengths under two fundamental objectives: minimizing total energy consumption and minimizing the maximum energy consumption of any vehicle. For the typically encountered combinations of vehicle and road characteristics, we provide a complete computational complexity classification, either developing polynomial-time algorithms or proving computational intractability. For several intractable cases, we design fully polynomial-time approximation schemes and polynomial-time heuristics with provable performance guarantees. A computational study demonstrates that the proposed heuristics achieve average solutions within 1\% of optimal. We also consider settings in which only limited information about position-dependent energy savings is available and develop a heuristic with bounded worst-case performance. In addition, we present an efficient algorithm for on-road vehicle resequencing when only limited position changes are permitted. Together, these results provide a comprehensive algorithmic framework for energy-efficient vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing.

</details>


### [148] [Safe Start: Configuring Optimization Algorithms for Decision-Making under Extreme Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09872)
*Henry Lam,Wasin Meesena*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机优化问题，目标不仅是优化平均情况目标，还要缓解罕见灾难事件的发生。作者指出在存在模拟模型时，将方差缩减集成到优化的自然方法（即使以合理的自适应方式执行）会遇到根本性挑战，难以保证使用常见随机梯度下降算法时的实际运行时间。这一挑战源于尾部分布目标对决策变量的极端敏感性，导致无论选择何种步长都会出现收敛的二分失败。作者提出基于"安全起点"（safe start）新概念的补救方法，实现高效的有限时间误差控制，并展示安全起点与方差缩减结合时采样复杂度的有利扩展。方法在投资组合优化和神经网络鲁棒分类等例子中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机优化仅关注平均情况目标，但在安全感知决策和AI训练等场景中，罕见灾难事件的发生同样至关重要。作者希望同时优化平均情况目标并缓解尾部极端事件风险，然而现有的方差缩减技术与随机梯度下降结合时，在处理尾部分布目标的敏感性上面临根本性困难。

Method: 作者首先分析了方差缩减集成到随机梯度下降中的挑战，指出尾部分布目标对决策变量的极端敏感性导致步长选择的二分困境（收敛失败）。随后提出"安全起点"（safe start）这一新概念，作为算法初始化的指导原则，使得算法能够实现高效的有限时间误差控制。核心思想是将安全起点与方差缩减技术相结合，以缓解尾部目标的敏感性并改善采样复杂度。

Result: 证明了在"安全起点"与方差缩减结合的组合下，采样复杂度能够获得有利的扩展（favorable scaling）。在投资组合优化和基于神经网络的鲁棒分类两个应用实例中验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地揭示了将方差缩减方法应用于尾部风险约束的随机优化时所面临的根本性障碍，并通过"安全起点"这一创新概念提供了可行的解决方案，为安全感知决策和AI训练中的风险缓解提供了理论保障和实用算法框架。

Abstract: We consider stochastic optimization where the goal is not only to optimize an average-case objective, but also to mitigate the occurrence of rare catastrophic events. This problem is motivated by safety-aware decision-making and AI training. We first argue that, in the presence of a simulation model, natural attempts to integrate variance reduction into optimization, even executed in a reasonable adaptive fashion, encounter fundamental challenges in guaranteeing realistic runtime when using common stochastic gradient descent algorithms. This challenge arises from the extreme sensitivity of tail-based objectives with respect to the decision variables, which renders a dichotomic failure of convergence regardless of what step size we select. We offer remedies based on a new notion of safe start that allows for efficient finite-time error control, and show how the sampling complexity scales favorably under the combination of safe start and variance reduction. We illustrate our methodologies on examples in portfolio optimization and robust classification with neural networks.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>
