The problem is discretized by a stochastic Galerkin method and written in an all-at-once (monolithic) form where the time steps are informed by a sequential solve of the mean problem. The solution vector is represented in Tensor Train (TT) format, while the system matrices are represented in CANDECOMP/PARAFAC (CP) format as needed. We propose preconditioners based on the low-rank CP approximations that retain additional stochastic and nonlinear information compared to mean-based preconditioners. Numerical experiments for a benchmark channel-flow problem are presented to show the effectiveness of the low-rank approximation, the relative tolerance strategy, and the proposed preconditioners.

</details>


### [185] [A splitting mixed finite element method for a stochastic Keller-Segel system with multiplicative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07798)
*Thoa Thieu,Liet Vo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于带乘性噪声和logistic增长的随机Keller-Segel系统的分裂混合有限元方法，通过引入辅助变量和时滞分裂策略解耦原耦合系统，消除了LBB稳定性约束，允许所有未知量使用连续分片线性有限元空间，并避免每步求解全耦合非线性系统，从而大幅降低计算成本。结合隐式Euler时间离散，建立了最优强误差估计并证明了带显式收敛速度的概率收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Keller-Segel系统模拟了趋化聚集与logistic生长的相互作用，但现有的数值方法面临耦合非线性系统求解成本高、满足LBB稳定性条件受限等挑战。需要一个既能降低计算复杂度又能保持最优收敛精度的数值方案。

Method: 该方法引入代表化学梯度的辅助变量，并结合时间滞后分裂策略，将原耦合系统解耦为一系列更简单的子问题。使用隐式Euler时间离散，并通过局部化技巧和合适的随机稳定性论证来建立误差估计。所有未知量采用连续分片线性有限元空间，无需满足LBB条件。

Result: 建立了全离散近似的最优强误差估计，证明了带显式收敛速度的概率收敛性。数值实验验证了理论收敛速度，并展示了方法能成功捕捉logistic生长项引起的全局有界性以及乘性噪声对趋化聚集的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的分裂混合有限元方法在保持最优收敛精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，是一种有效的随机Keller-Segel系统数值求解方案，能够准确反映logistic生长与乘性噪声对系统动力学行为的影响。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a splitting mixed finite element method for a stochastic Keller--Segel system with logistic growth driven by multiplicative noise. By introducing an auxiliary variable representing the chemical gradient together with a time-lagged splitting strategy, the proposed method decouples the original coupled system into a sequence of simpler subproblems. Consequently, it eliminates the Ladyzhenskaya--Babuška--Brezzi stability constraint, permits the use of continuous piecewise linear finite element spaces for all unknowns, and avoids solving a fully coupled nonlinear system at each time step, thereby significantly reducing the computational cost. Combined with an implicit Euler time discretization, the proposed approach yields a fully discrete numerical scheme for the stochastic Keller--Segel system. Using a localization technique together with suitable stochastic stability arguments, we establish optimal strong error estimates for the fully discrete approximations and prove convergence in probability with explicit convergence rates. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate that the proposed method successfully captures the global boundedness induced by the logistic growth term as well as the influence of multiplicative noise on chemotactic aggregation.

</details>


### [186] [A non-conforming finite difference discrete fracture model based on an energy principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07807)
*Ziyao Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于裂缝性多孔介质中单相流的有限差分离散裂缝模型，基于统一能量原理在非保形笛卡尔网格上实现，无需额外自由度，仅局部修改有限差分格式，可同时处理高导裂缝和低渗屏障，保持离散系统的对称正定性，并通过低成本局部后处理恢复界面效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂缝性多孔介质中的流动模拟对油气开采、地下水等领域至关重要，但现有离散裂缝模型通常需要网格与裂缝保形或引入额外自由度，计算成本高且实现复杂。需要一个在非保形网格上无需额外自由度的高效、灵活且鲁棒的数值方法。

Method: 从统一能量原理出发推导有限差分离散裂缝模型；在无需与裂缝保形的笛卡尔网格上实现；不引入额外自由度，仅对底层有限差分格式进行局部修改；可同时处理高导裂缝和低渗屏障；通过廉价的局部后处理恢复屏障引起的压力跳跃和裂缝引起的通量。

Result: 通过解析解构造的数值实验以及已发表的二维和三维基准问题验证，该方法对孤立裂缝、复杂裂缝网络和各向异性多孔介质均表现出良好的精度、有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种在非保形笛卡尔网格上高效模拟裂缝性多孔介质流动的替代途径，兼具无额外自由度、局部修改、对称正定性和对裂缝特征（高导/低渗）普适处理等优点。

Abstract: We propose a finite difference discrete fracture model for single-phase flow in fractured porous media. The method is derived from a unified energy principle and is implemented on Cartesian grids that need not conform to the fractures. It introduces no additional degrees of freedom, modifies the underlying finite difference scheme only locally, handles both highly conductive fractures and low-permeability barriers, and naturally preserves symmetry and positive definiteness of the discrete system. Barrier-induced pressure jumps and fracture-induced fluxes can be recovered through inexpensive local post-processing, yielding a sharper representation of the interface effects. Numerical experiments based on manufactured solutions and published $2$D and $3$D benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy, effectiveness, and flexibility of the method for isolated fractures, complex networks, and anisotropic porous media.

</details>


### [187] [The Sharp Worst-Case Asymptotic Rate of the Barzilai--Borwein Method in $\mathbb R^d$ and Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07839)
*Shutai Yang,Ya-Xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了Barzilai-Borwein（BB）方法在一致正定二次型及局部非线性问题上的精确渐近收敛速率。在有限维情形下，梯度根因子被(b0-a0)/(b0+a0)界定，其中[a0,b0]为初始活跃谱区间；在Hilbert空间中，标量谱测度给出了相应的活跃支撑界及最优匹配一致包络阈值；对于具有严格Fréchet可微导数且导数一致正定的局部非线性问题，建立了(BB)方法的局部包络速率与误差/梯度根因子界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barzilai-Borwein方法因其无需线搜索的梯度类方法特性而广泛应用，但其收敛速率的精确刻画长期缺乏严谨的理论结果。本文旨在建立BB规则在二次型与非线性问题上的尖锐（sharp）渐近速率，明确最坏情况下轨迹因子的精确常数，并推广到无穷维Hilbert空间设定。

Method: 本文通过分析BB方法的两个规则（长步与短步），引入"初始活跃谱区间"[a0,b0]概念，利用谱分解与标量谱测度工具在有限维和Hilbert空间中推导梯度根因子的上界；通过对匹配初始化与端点轨迹的构造性分析证明最优性；并利用严格Fréchet可微性与导数的一致正定性，将二次型结果推广到局部非线性问题，建立统一包络速率。

Result: （1）有限维中，任意固定BB规则且首步为正时，梯度根因子≤(b0-a0)/(b0+a0)，最坏轨迹因子为c_H=(κ(H)-1)/(κ(H)+1)；（2）H至少有两个不同特征值时，匹配初始化与平衡端点轨迹可达到该值，且匹配初始化下同一常数是最优一致包络阈值；（3）Hilbert空间中，标量谱测度给出活跃支撑界和最优匹配一致包络阈值（含连续端点谱）；（4）局部非线性情形下，每个γ∈(c_*,1)都是任一纯BB规则的一致局部包络速率，所有收敛到驻点的良定义轨迹的误差/梯度根因子≤c_*、目标间隙根因子≤c_*^2；（5）在R²中构造二次与C^∞真非二次匹配端点轨迹可达这些因子。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了BB方法在一致正定二次型及局部非线性问题上的精确最坏收敛速率，证明因子(κ-1)/(κ+1)（及其平方形式）是最优常数，且在有限维、Hilbert空间和局部非线性三种设定下均建立了统一的理论框架。这一结果不仅统一并改进了已有的BB方法收敛性分析，还为实际应用中步长选取和收敛性预测提供了量化的理论保障。

Abstract: We establish sharp asymptotic rates for the two Barzilai--Borwein (BB) rules on uniformly positive quadratics and local nonlinear problems. In finite dimensions, for either fixed rule and an arbitrary positive first step, the gradient root factor is bounded by $(b_0-a_0)/(b_0+a_0)$, where $[a_0,b_0]$ is the initially active spectral interval. Hence the worst trajectory factor is $c_H=(κ(H)-1)/(κ(H)+1)$. When $H$ has at least two distinct eigenvalues, matched initialization and a balanced endpoint trajectory attain this value. Under matched initialization, the same constant is the optimal uniform-envelope threshold. For bounded, self-adjoint, uniformly positive operators on Hilbert space, scalar spectral measures yield the corresponding active-support bound and optimal matched uniform-envelope threshold, including continuous endpoint spectrum. Finally, if the gradient is strictly Fréchet differentiable at a stationary point and its derivative is self-adjoint and uniformly positive, every $γ\in(c_*,1)$, where $c_*=(κ(A_*)-1)/(κ(A_*)+1)$, is a uniform local envelope rate for either pure BB rule. Every well-defined trajectory converging to the stationary point has error and gradient root factors at most $c_*$ and objective-gap root factor at most $c_*^2$. Over the class of objectives with prescribed distinct derivative endpoints $m_*<M_*$, matched endpoint trajectories for quadratic and $C^\infty$ genuinely nonquadratic examples in $\mathbb R^2$ attain these factors.

</details>


### [188] [High-Resolution Weighted Essentially Non-Oscillatory Compact Least-Squares Schemes with Implicit Time Integration for Compressible Navier-Stokes Equations on Curvilinear Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08117)
*Yongzhi Luo,Huiheng Fan,Wei-Gang Zeng,Yu-Xin Ren,Jianhua Pan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族用于可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的高分辨率加权本质无振荡（WENO）紧致最小二乘格式族，结合隐式时间积分和曲线网格。相比原始格式，该方法通过精度保持的加权策略仅在光滑重构线上构造重构矩阵以抑制高频振荡，并简化激波捕捉过程（仅用单一多项式集），同时通过谱优化获得优于传统WENO格式的谱特性。数值实验验证了其在可压缩流动中的鲁棒激波捕捉能力和高分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始紧致最小二乘格式在间断区域强制执行精度约束，导致持续的高频振荡，且需要同时使用无限制和限制多项式，增加了激波捕捉的复杂性和计算成本。本文旨在解决这些问题，同时保持高分辨率。

Method: 提出算法族包含两个核心改进：(1) 引入精度保持的加权策略，仅在光滑重构线上构造重构矩阵，抑制高频振荡并获得更锐利的间断剖面；(2) 使用单一多项式集替代原有的双多项式集（无限制+限制），简化激波捕捉流程并提升效率。结合谱优化、光滑性指示器和罚矩阵的高效迭代构造，实现低附加成本的鲁棒重构。

Result: 数值结果表明：（1）该方法能有效抑制原始格式中持续的高频振荡；（2）在间断附近获得更锐利的剖面；（3）相比传统WENO格式具有更优的谱特性；（4）在无粘和有粘的一维、二维和三维流动中均展现出鲁棒的激波捕捉能力，同时在光滑区域和接触间断处保持高分辨率。

Conclusion: 所提出的高分辨率WENO紧致最小二乘格式族在保持高分辨率的同时显著改善了激波捕捉的鲁棒性和计算效率，可广泛应用于曲线网格上的可压缩Navier-Stokes方程数值模拟。

Abstract: This paper presents a family of high-resolution weighted essentially non-oscillatory compact least-squares schemes with implicit time integration for the compressible Navier-Stokes equations on curvilinear grids. Compared with the original compact least-squares schemes, the proposed method introduces two main improvements. First, instead of enforcing the accuracy constraints over the entire computational domain, including discontinuous regions, it constructs the reconstruction matrix only along smooth reconstruction lines using an accuracy-preserving weighting strategy. This treatment effectively suppresses the persistent high-frequency oscillations observed in the original compact least-squares schemes and yields sharper profiles near discontinuities. Second, the method simplifies the shock-capturing procedure and improves efficiency by using a single set of polynomials, whereas the original compact least-squares schemes require both unlimited and limited polynomials. Combined with spectral optimization, the proposed method exhibits more favorable spectral properties than conventional weighted essentially non-oscillatory schemes. The smoothness indicators and penalty matrices are constructed through an efficient iterative procedure with modest additional cost. Numerical results for inviscid and viscous one-, two-, and three-dimensional flows demonstrate that the proposed method provides robust shock-capturing capability while maintaining high resolution in smooth regions and across contact discontinuities.

</details>


### [189] [Rational neural networks for tracking complex singularities of nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08134)
*Nadiia Derevianko,Hans-Joachim Bungartz,Felix Dietrich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的方法，用于非线性偏微分方程（PDE）数值解析延拓及复奇异性检测。该方法采用"不安全"Padé激活单元（PAUs）作为激活函数，并结合一种新颖的无反向传播权重/偏置计算方法。与传统固定隐层参数的方法不同，该方法的权重和偏置被视作时间相关函数，从而能适应复奇异性的演化。通过对非线性热方程（有限时间爆破）、Burgers方程（激波形成）和非线性Schrödinger方程（怪波形成）三个经典案例的验证，展示了该方法在定位复奇异性、跟踪其轨迹及推断特征现象形成方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性PDE解的解析延拓和复奇异性检测长期面临数值挑战。传统方法（如固定隐层参数的神经网络或纯数值延拓）难以高效追踪随时间演化的奇异性轨迹，且无法从奇异动力学中推断解的特征现象（如爆破、激波、怪波）的形成机制。

Method: 1) 使用"不安全"Padé激活单元（PAUs）作为激活函数，使网络能够表示具有极点型奇异性的亚纯函数；2) 提出一种无反向传播的权重和偏置计算新方法，专门针对学习含极点奇异的亚纯函数设计；3) 将隐藏层权重和偏置视为时间相关函数，使网络能够适应复奇异性的时空演化。

Result: 在三个经典非线性PDE案例中验证了方法性能：非线性热方程能够定位有限时间爆破对应的复奇异性；Burgers方程能够检测激波形成相关的奇异性；非线性Schrödinger方程能够追踪怪波形成过程中的奇异性轨迹。方法能够高效定位解析延拓解的复奇异性并跟踪其随时间的变化轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于PAU激活函数和时变参数的无反向传播神经网络方法，为非线性PDE的解析延拓与复奇异性检测提供了一种高效、自适应的新工具，基于奇异动力学可以推断非线性PDE解中特征现象的物理机理。

Abstract: We present a neural network-based method for numerical analytic continuation of solutions of nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) and detection of their complex singularities. The proposed framework employs "unsafe" Padé activation units (PAUs) as activation functions, together with a novel backpropagation-free method for computing the weights and biases of the hidden layers. The training procedure is designed specifically for learning meromorphic functions with pole-type singularities. Unlike existing methods with fixed hidden-layer parameters, the proposed approach treats the weights and biases as time-dependent functions, allowing the network to adapt to the evolution of the complex singularities. Using this method, we can efficiently locate the complex singularities of the extended solution, track their trajectories over time, and, based on this dynamics, infer the formation of characteristic phenomena in the solutions of nonlinear PDEs. To demonstrate the performance of our method, we analyze three well-known cases: (1) the nonlinear heat equation, which exhibits finite-time blow up; (2) the nonlinear Burgers equation, which develops a shock; and (3) the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, in which rogue waves form.

</details>


### [190] [Second order unfitted ghost-FEM for elliptic interface problems with applications to low-dimensional semiconductor devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09466)
*Clarissa Astuto,Giovanni Nastasi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于椭圆界面问题的非拟合幽灵有限元方法，并应用于低维半导体器件的静电模拟。方法基于固定笛卡尔网格和水平集函数表示几何，通过 snapping-back-to-grid 策略控制小网格问题，并用对称 Nitsche 公式弱施加界面条件。在基准问题中验证了二阶精度（解）和一阶精度（梯度）。应用中模拟石墨烯场效应晶体管的漂移-扩散-Poisson 耦合模型，数值结果再现了 OFF 到 ON 态的转移特性，并揭示了栅压对二维静电势的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维半导体器件（如石墨烯场效应晶体管）的静电模拟涉及具有不连续扩散系数的椭圆界面问题。传统的有限元方法需要随界面移动进行网格生成和重剖分，计算成本高且复杂。作者旨在开发一种基于固定笛卡尔网格的非拟合方法，避免网格生成和重剖分，同时有效处理小切割单元和界面条件问题。

Method: 采用非拟合幽灵有限元方法：(1) 使用固定笛卡尔网格，以水平集函数表示几何界面；(2) 采用 snapping-back-to-grid 策略控制小切割单元问题；(3) 通过对称 Nitsche 公式弱施加界面条件；(4) 数值验证采用不同几何和系数跳变的标准椭圆界面基准问题；(5) 在实际应用中采用二维氧化物/石墨烯/氧化物结构的静电势计算，结合一维双极性漂移-扩散方程描述石墨烯层电荷输运，通过阻尼不动点迭代配合区域分解方法求解耦合非线性系统。

Result: 基准数值实验表明该方法在解的 L2 误差上达到二阶精度，梯度误差上达到一阶精度。在石墨烯场效应晶体管模拟中，再现了从 OFF 态到 ON 态的清晰转移特性，展示了栅压对二维静电势的显著影响。此外，结果显示石墨烯层的有效厚度和离散化会影响横向电势分布，支持采用全二维静电描述并显式施加氧化物/石墨烯界面条件的必要性。

Conclusion: 非拟合幽灵有限元方法能够高效、准确地求解具有不连续扩散系数的椭圆界面问题，避免了网格重剖分带来的计算负担。该方法成功应用于石墨烯场效应晶体管的静电模拟，验证了其在真实器件建模中的有效性。研究还表明，对于石墨烯这类超薄低维材料，必须采用全二维静电描述并显式处理界面条件，而不能简单地将石墨烯层视为零厚度界面。

Abstract: We develop an unfitted ghost finite element method for elliptic interface problems with discontinuous diffusion coefficients and apply it to the electrostatic simulation of low-dimensional semiconductor devices. The proposed approach is based on a fixed Cartesian grid and represents the geometry by level-set functions, avoiding mesh generation and remeshing even in the presence of interfaces. A snapping-back-to-grid strategy is used to control the small-cut-cell issue, while interface conditions are weakly enforced by a symmetric Nitsche formulation. The method is first validated on benchmark elliptic interface problems with different geometries and coefficient jumps, showing second-order accuracy for the solution and first-order accuracy for its gradient.
  As an application, we consider a graphene field-effect transistor described by a self-consistent drift-diffusion-Poisson model. The electrostatic potential is computed in a two-dimensional oxide/graphene/oxide structure, while charge transport in the graphene layer is modeled by one-dimensional bipolar drift-diffusion equations in the degenerate case and by including a field-dependent mobility model. The coupled nonlinear system is solved by a damped fixed-point iteration combined with a domain-decomposition treatment of the three-layer geometry. Numerical simulations reproduce transfer characteristics with a clear transition from an OFF state to an ON state and show the influence of the gate voltage on the two-dimensional electrostatic potential. The results also indicate that the effective thickness and discretization of the graphene layer affect the transverse potential profile, supporting the use of a full two-dimensional electrostatic description with explicit oxide/graphene interface conditions.

</details>


### [191] [Convergence of a CutFEM for fluid--structure interaction with a deforming interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08140)
*Miguel A. Fernández,Buyang Li,Maxim A. Olshanskii*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对一种用于流固耦合问题的半离散浸入式CutFEM方法进行了严格的误差分析，证明了在足够正则的FSI解和k≥3次有限元下，流体速度与固体位移具有k阶收敛，流体压力具有k-1阶收敛。这是首次对具有变形界面的全耦合FSI系统进行非贴体有限元方法的严格收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流固耦合问题中，固体变形会使流体域发生改变，传统的贴体网格方法需要不断重新生成网格，计算成本高昂。非贴体（unfitted）有限元方法如CutFEM可以避免网格重构，但此前缺乏对全耦合FSI系统（含变形界面）的非贴体有限元方法的严格收敛性分析，因此需要建立该方法的理论误差界。

Method: 采用半离散的浸入式CutFEM方法，通过拉格朗日乘子施加流固耦合条件，并完整考虑结构变形对流体域的影响。对流体速度、固体位移和流体压力分别在FSI问题的能量范数下进行误差估计，利用k≥3次的有限元进行离散。

Result: 对于足够正则的FSI解和k≥3次的有限元，证明了流体速度和固体位移在能量范数下具有k阶收敛，流体压力具有k-1阶收敛。

Conclusion: 这是首次对具有变形界面的全耦合FSI系统建立非贴体有限元方法的严格收敛性分析，填补了该领域的理论空白，为浸入式CutFEM在FSI问题中的应用提供了收敛性理论保障。

Abstract: We present a rigorous error analysis for a semidiscrete immersed CutFEM applied to fluid--structure interaction (FSI) problems involving an incompressible viscous fluid and an elastic solid. The method employs a Lagrange multiplier to enforce the fluid-solid coupling conditions while fully accounting for the effect of the structural deformation on the fluid domain. For sufficiently regular FSI solutions and finite elements of degree $k\geq3$, we prove convergence of order $k$ for the fluid velocity and solid displacement and convergence of order $k-1$ for the fluid pressure in the energy norms associated with the FSI problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first rigorous convergence analysis of an unfitted finite element method for a fully coupled FSI system with a deforming interface.

</details>


### [192] [A frequency-stable Nyström method for two-dimensional diffraction by periodic gratings at and near Wood anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08172)
*Kei Matsushima,Hiroshi Isakari*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于二维周期衍射光栅标量波散射的频率稳定边界积分方程方法。通过改进的准周期格林函数消除Wood异常处的奇异性，并构建了频率均匀的全离散Nyström格式，在数值和理论上均展现了跨Wood异常频率的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准周期格林函数在Wood异常频率处作为频率的函数具有奇异性，这阻碍了对传统积分方程离散化进行频率均匀误差分析。作者旨在开发一种在Wood异常频率处仍然稳定且可进行频率均匀误差估计的方法。

Method: 该方法基于修正的准周期格林函数：通过减去奇异的Rayleigh模式并引入有限维修正项来恢复辐射条件。然后利用Ewald表示格林函数，并采用适应核函数对数奇异性的求积规则，构建全离散Nyström格式。对于可能包含Wood异常频率的紧致频率区间，证明了离散系统在足够大的离散化与截断参数下唯一可解，并建立了频率均匀误差估计和条件数界。

Result: 证明了修正后的组合场边界积分方程在底层衍射问题唯一可解时总是唯一可解的，且对应的散射场在包括Wood异常在内的频率上连续依赖于频率。数值实验验证了该格式在Wood异常频率处的收敛性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种频率稳定的边界积分方程方法，克服了Wood异常频率对传统格林函数方法的限制，为周期衍射光栅的散射问题提供了严格的频率均匀误差分析框架。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a frequency-stable boundary integral equation method for scalar wave scattering by periodic diffraction gratings in two dimensions. The standard quasi-periodic Green function is singular as a function of the frequency at Wood anomaly frequencies, which prevents a direct frequency-uniform error analysis of conventional integral equation discretizations. Our method is based on a modified quasi-periodic Green function obtained by subtracting the singular Rayleigh modes and introducing a finite-dimensional correction that restores the radiation condition. The resulting combined-field boundary integral equation is shown to be uniquely solvable whenever the underlying diffraction problem is uniquely solvable, and the corresponding scattered field depends continuously on the frequency, including at Wood anomalies. We then construct a fully discrete Nyström scheme using an Ewald representation of the Green function and a quadrature rule adapted to the logarithmic singularity of the kernel. For compact frequency intervals that may contain Wood anomaly frequencies, we prove unique solvability of the discrete system for sufficiently large discretization and truncation parameters and establish a frequency-uniform error estimate and bound on the condition number. Numerical examples illustrate the convergence of the scheme and its stability across Wood anomaly frequencies.

</details>


### [193] [Suppressing Low-Frequency Cancellation Errors in Far-Field Computation with the MFIE, Müller, and Multitrace Müller Integral Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08314)
*Chien V. Le,Kristof Cools*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了鲁棒的数值公式，用于抑制理想导体磁场积分方程（MFIE）以及均匀和复合介质物体的Müller积分方程中的低频抵消误差。该方法结合准Helmholtz投影器与定制的重新缩放程序，在保持混合MFIE和Müller离散化在密集网格和低频区域的条件数性质的同时，避免了有限精度抵消导致的远场评估误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低频区域，MFIE和Müller积分方程中Helmholtz分量的数值抵消会导致显著精度损失，影响远场计算的准确性，需要开发鲁棒的数值公式来解决这一问题。

Method: 将准Helmholtz投影器（用于分离表面电流的Helmholtz分量）与定制的重新缩放程序相结合，在有限精度条件下保留对远场评估至关重要的分量，从而抑制低频抵消误差。

Result: 数值实验涵盖简单和多重连通散射体、复合介质结构、多尺度和高对比度配置，结果表明所提方法在从全波频段到极低频率的广泛频率范围内均能提供准确的远场预测，同时保持混合MFIE和Müller离散化的良好条件性质。

Conclusion: 所提出的准Helmholtz投影器与重新缩放策略相结合的方法，能够有效抑制MFIE和Müller积分方程在低频下的抵消误差，在宽带频率范围内实现鲁棒且准确的远场计算，适用于各类复杂散射配置。

Abstract: This paper presents robust numerical formulations for suppressing low-frequency cancellation errors in the magnetic field integral equation (MFIE) for perfect electric conductors and the Müller integral equations for homogeneous and composite dielectric objects. The proposed approach combines quasi-Helmholtz projectors, which separate the Helmholtz components of the surface currents, with a tailored rescaling procedure that preserves the components essential for accurate far-field evaluation from finite-precision cancellation. The resulting formulations retain the conditioning properties of the mixed MFIE and Müller discretizations in the dense-mesh and low-frequency regimes. Numerical experiments involving simply and multiply connected scatterers, composite dielectric structures, multiscale and high-contrast configurations demonstrate that the proposed methods yield accurate far-field predictions across a broad frequency range, from the full-wave regime to extremely low frequencies.

</details>


### [194] [Kernel Localization and Whole-Trajectory Generalization for Linear Multistep Methods in Deep Learning-Based Discovery of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08435)
*Yaru Liu,Yiqi Gu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性多步法（LMMs）与神经网络结合用于从离散轨迹数据学习动力系统向量场时两个尚未解决的问题：一是非辅助Adams-Bashforth和Adams-Moulton发现系统中零残差网格解的非唯一性及边界层局部化现象；二是辅助与非辅助公式的整条轨迹泛化估计，后者包含指数衰减的边界层项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网格级收敛理论无法解释LMM发现系统中的两个关键问题：非辅助A-B和A-M系统中零残差网格解的非唯一性及其边界层行为，以及整条轨迹上的泛化误差估计缺失。这两个问题对理解和保证方法在实际应用中的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 通过发现矩阵的核（kernel）分析来解释非辅助A-B和A-M系统中零残差解的非唯一性；在发现的稳定性条件下，证明A-B方案中解的差异在初始索引附近指数局部化，而A-M方案在初始和终端索引形成两侧边界层。随后推导辅助与非辅助公式的整条轨迹泛化估计，将误差分解为标量时间函数进行分析。

Result: 辅助公式的轨迹误差在网格精度、近似和迹正则性假设下为O(h^p)；非辅助公式的全局估计包含额外边界层项，但在固定内部子区间上这些项指数衰减。数值实验验证了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了LMM发现系统在零残差解非唯一性和整条轨迹泛化误差方面的理论空白，表明非辅助公式虽然在全局上受边界层影响，但其内部子区间上的误差呈指数衰减，不影响长期轨迹学习的精度。

Abstract: Linear multistep methods (LMMs) combined with neural-network approximation provide a high-order framework for learning governing vector fields of dynamical systems from discrete trajectory data. This paper studies two issues in LMM-based discovery that are not resolved by existing grid-level convergence theory. First, in non-auxiliary Adams--Bashforth (A-B) and Adams--Moulton (A-M) discovery systems, we observe that zero-residual grid solutions are nonunique but consistent along the trajectory, with differences limited to the boundary layer. We explain this phenomenon through a kernel analysis of the non-auxiliary discovery matrices. Under the corresponding discovery-stability conditions, the differences between zero-residual grid solutions are exponentially localized near the initial indices for A-B schemes, whereas they form two-sided boundary layers near the initial and terminal indices for A-M schemes. Second, we derive whole-trajectory generalization estimates for both auxiliary and non-auxiliary formulations. Once the learned vector field is restricted to a fixed observed trajectory, each component of the error becomes a scalar function of time. For the auxiliary formulation, the trajectory error is $O(h^p)$ under the corresponding grid accuracy, approximation, and trace regularity assumptions. For non-auxiliary formulations, the global estimates contain additional boundary-layer terms. On fixed interior subintervals, these terms are exponentially damped. Numerical experiments illustrate the convergence behavior.

</details>


### [195] [Coarse space preconditioning for Generalized Optimized Schwarz Methods. Part I: continuous case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08505)
*Xavier Claeys*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对广义优化Schwarz方法（GOSM）提出了一种粗空间构造策略，用于该方法的预条件处理，并在连续无穷维框架下给出了GMRes求解预条件方程的收敛性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GOSM是Deprés算法的一种变体，用于求解谐波波动传播问题的区域分解策略，通过非局部交换算子施加传输条件。尽管该方法已有应用，但其预条件处理和收敛性分析尚不完善，特别是缺乏连续层面的粗空间构造方案。

Method: 在连续层面、无穷维设置下进行分析，为GOSM公式的预条件处理构造粗空间，并推导GMRes迭代应用于预条件方程后的收敛性估计。

Result: 成功构造了适用于GOSM预条件处理的粗空间，并给出了GMRes求解预条件系统时的收敛性理论估计。

Conclusion: 本文为GOSM提供了理论上的预条件策略和收敛性保证，为谐波波动问题的域分解求解提供了更坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: The Generalized Optimized Schwarz Method (GOSM) originally proposed in [Claeys, 2021] is a variant of Deprés algorithm, a domain decomposition strategy for the solution of harmonic wave propagation problems. It imposes transmission conditions through interfaces by means of a non-local exchange operator. Conducting our analysis at the continuous level, in an infinite dimensional setting, we propose a coarse space construction for the preconditioning of the GOSM formulation, and provide estimates for the convergence of GMRes applied to the preconditioned equation.

</details>


### [196] [ADEx-FNO: A Unified Ambient-Domain Framework for Fourier Neural Operators on Varying Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08608)
*Roberto Nuca,Giovanni Testa,Luca Galimberti,Matteo Parsani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出ADEx-FNO，一种在不修改傅里叶算子层的前提下，通过将物理域嵌入固定环境超立方体并用符号距离函数表示，实现几何感知的确定性框架；输入和解场被确定性扩展到环境域，在共同的非均匀矩形隐网格上处理，然后插值到独立选择的目标离散网格。无需可训练图、点云、变形或几何解码模块，所有几何传输操作均位于优化过程之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fourier神经算子（FNO）提供高效的非局部谱学习，但难以适应变化的几何形状和独立选择的离散化方案。现有方法在处理不同几何和网格时面临挑战，需要一个能够在不改变核心FNO结构的前提下处理任意几何和离散化的确定性框架。

Method: 每个物理域嵌入固定的环境超立方体，通过符号距离函数表示；输入和解场确定性扩展到环境域，转移到共同的非均匀矩形隐网格，由FNO处理，然后插值到独立选择的目标离散网格并限制回物理域。所有几何传输操作在优化过程外部执行，无需可训练的结构模块。

Result: 在2D和3D光滑域非线性Poisson和advection-reaction-diffusion问题中，held-out相对l2误差为0.32%-0.77%；在未见过的非光滑几何上也进行了评估。用于初始化CFD求解器时，29个收敛的2D和3D RANS案例中伪时间迭代平均减少44.17%和43.03%，三种网格分辨率下均有类似收益。URANS案例减少物理时间推进18.52%-27.51%。在从2D URANS训练数据到不同Mach和Reynolds数的DNS迁移中，bootstrap区间减少23.47%-48.21%。

Conclusion: ADEx-FNO在不修改FNO核心架构的前提下，通过环境域扩展和符号距离函数实现了对任意几何和独立离散化的适应，在求解精度和CFD求解器初始化加速方面均表现出显著效果，同时保持了物理一致性。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide efficient nonlocal spectral learning, but varying geometries and independently chosen discretizations remain difficult to accommodate. We introduce the ambient-domain extension Fourier neural operator (ADEx-FNO), a deterministic framework that incorporates geometry without modifying the defining Fourier-operator layers. Each physical domain is embedded in a fixed ambient hypercube and represented by a signed distance function. Inputs and solution fields are deterministically extended to the ambient domain, transferred to a common, potentially nonuniform rectilinear latent grid, processed by the FNO, then interpolated to an independently chosen target discretization and restricted to the physical domain. All geometry-transfer operations lie outside the optimization procedure and require no trainable graph, point-cloud, deformation, or geometry-decoding modules.
  ADEx-FNO achieves relative l2 errors of 0.32%-0.77% on held-out smooth-domain nonlinear Poisson and advection-reaction-diffusion problems in 2D and 3D, and is also evaluated on unseen nonsmooth geometries. A single ADEx-FNO inference is then used to initialize conventional CFD solvers. For all 29 converged 2D and 3D RANS cases, pseudo-time iterations decrease, with mean reductions of 44.17% and 43.03%, respectively, with comparable gains across three mesh resolutions. URANS cases reduce post-window physical-time advances by 18.52%-27.51%. In transfer from 2D URANS training data to DNS at different Mach and Reynolds numbers, the bootstrap interval decreases by 23.47%-48.21%, depending on the target statistic. In all CFD tests, ADEx-FNO provides only the initial field; the governing-equation solver controls the subsequent solution, while physical or statistical consistency is assessed separately from computational savings.

</details>


### [197] [Exact Finite-Horizon Memory, Conditioning, and Dissipative Decay in Coarse Upwind Finite-Volume Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08633)
*Antonis Polemitis,Nicholas Christakis,Dimitris Drikakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了一阶迎风有限体积法离散周期标量平流方程时，粗粒化有限体积均值不能构成预测性状态的原因与补救方法。作者推导出有限时域观测秩定律（每增加一个观测暴露一层子网格并贡献比父网格数少一个的独立方向），提出了锚定通量散度队列来达到集中式预测下界并形成最小自主预测状态，同时区分了精确可观测性与稳定可恢复性，并证明了Courant数在(0,1)区间时非恒定分量在有限精度扰动下的收缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒度的有限体积平均值通常无法构成预测性状态——不同细网格态若具有相同的父网格平均值，可能演化出不同的未来粗粒度历史。这给粗粒化、降阶、多尺度和学习型科学模型的态充分性评估带来了根本性挑战，因此需要建立可解析求解的基准来分析这一失败机制并设计恢复预测性的方案。

Method: 以周期标量平流方程、一阶迎风有限体积离散和forward Euler时间积分为对象，通过渐近层分析推导有限时域观测秩定律；构建锚定通量散度队列（anchored flux-divergence queue）以实现集中式预测；对collar-to-queue映射进行条件数分析区分精确可观测性与稳定可恢复性；并利用有界算术扰动下的收缩性分析证明非恒定分量的指数衰减。

Result: 证明了观测秩定律：每增加一个观测暴露一层未解析子网格并贡献比父网格单元数少一个的独立方向；集中式预测每个暴露层需要一个比父单元数更少的补充坐标，而局部预测每层每父单元需要一个坐标。锚定通量散度队列达到集中式下界，能识别周期通量规范，并在饱和后与父平均值构成最小自主预测状态。还发现collar-to-queue映射随Courant数减小而快速病态化，并证明Courant数在(0,1)区间内非恒定分量在有界扰动下收缩（含均值漂移控制、显式扰动邻域和网格依赖衰减时间）。

Conclusion: 该工作为粗粒化/降阶/多尺度/学习型科学模型中的态充分性评估提供了一个可解析求解的基准问题，揭示了粗粒度平均值不足的根本原因（未解析子网格层的记忆效应），并给出了精确可观测性与数值稳定可恢复性之间的本质区别。

Abstract: Coarse finite-volume averages do not generally form a predictive state: realized interface fluxes close a conservative update, but distinct fine-grid states with identical parent averages can generate different future coarse histories. We analyze this failure for periodic scalar advection discretized by a first-order upwind finite-volume method with forward Euler time integration. We derive a finite-horizon observation-rank law: each additional observation exposes one new child-cell layer and contributes one fewer independent direction than the number of parent cells, until the unresolved layers are exhausted. Centralized prediction therefore requires one fewer additional coordinate than the number of parent cells per exposed layer, whereas product-local prediction requires one coordinate per layer in each parent. An anchored flux-divergence queue attains the centralized bound, identifies the periodic flux gauge, and, at saturation, forms a minimal autonomous predictive state with the parent averages. We then distinguish exact observability from stable recoverability. The collar-to-queue map becomes rapidly ill-conditioned as the Courant number decreases, so algebraically visible delayed information may fall below a prescribed numerical tolerance. For Courant numbers strictly between zero and one, we prove contraction of the nonconstant component under bounded arithmetic perturbations, with separate control of mean drift, an explicit perturbation neighborhood, and a grid-dependent decay time. Numerical experiments illustrate the rank ladder, effective-rank loss, queue conditioning, step-function flattening, and delayed coarse separation followed by dissipative decay. The results provide a solvable benchmark for assessing state sufficiency in coarse, reduced, multiscale, and learned scientific models.

</details>


### [198] [Mass Lumping and Numerical Quadrature for Approximation of Fractional Elliptic Differential Equations Driven by Gaussian White Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08658)
*Kelvin J. R. Almeida-Sousa,David Bolin,Alexandre B. Simas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数阶椭圆随机偏微分方程（SPDE）在数值近似中，由质量集中（mass lumping）和数值积分（numerical quadrature）引起的误差，并推导了协方差函数均方误差的显式收敛速率，覆盖了有界欧几里得域、黎曼流形和度量图等主要应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶SPDE方法在统计和机器学习中被广泛用于高斯随机场的高效灵活建模，其计算效率依赖于有限元近似结合数值积分和质量集中以实现稀疏矩阵推断。尽管已有大量工作研究分数阶SPDE的有限元近似，但实际中使用的质量集中和积分近似的影响尚未被充分分析，因此需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者在一般框架下推导了基于有限元离散、结合数值积分和质量集中的分数阶SPDE数值近似的显式收敛速率。具体而言，给出了协方差函数均方误差的收敛速率；同时分析了非平稳方差控制因子L^β(τu)=W的形式，其中τ为空间变化的函数，并推导了协方差误差估计以展示τ的正则性如何影响收敛速率。

Result: 获得了在一般框架下覆盖主要质量集中应用场景的协方差函数均方误差的显式收敛速率；针对非平稳方差控制因子，推导了反映空间变化系数τ正则性对收敛速率影响的协方差误差估计；并针对有界欧几里得域、黎曼流形和度量图上的随机场提供了具体结果，且数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文填补了分数阶SPDE方法中质量集中和数值积分近似误差分析的理论空白，为实践中SPDE方法中这些数值技巧的使用提供了严格的收敛性保证。

Abstract: Fractional elliptic stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) are widely used in statistics and machine learning for computationally efficient and flexible modeling of Gaussian random fields. The computational efficiency of the SPDE approach relies on finite element approximations combined with numerical quadrature and mass lumping, which enable sparse matrix methods during inference. Although many works have studied finite element approximations of fractional SPDEs, the effect of the mass lumping and quadrature approximations used in practice has not been fully analyzed. To fill this gap, we derive convergence rates for numerical approximations of fractional SPDEs based on finite element discretizations combined with numerical quadrature and mass lumping. Specifically, we obtain explicit convergence rates for the mean-squared error of the covariance function in a general framework that covers the main settings where mass lumping is used in the SPDE approach. We also analyze non-stationary variance-control factors of the form $L^β(τu)=\mathcal{W}$, where $τ$ is spatially varying, and derive covariance error estimates showing how the regularity of $τ$ affects the convergence rate. As specific examples, we provide results for random fields on bounded Euclidean domains, Riemannian manifolds, and metric graphs. Numerical experiments are presented that confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [199] [Discovering PDEs equivariant under rigid motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08913)
*Francesco Ballerin,Erlend Grong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用刚体运动对称性来构建PDE发现候选库的方法，用刚体运动等变的候选项替代通用导数单项式库，并结合稀疏回归在五个不同方程上进行了基准测试。该方法在噪声破坏刚体对称性（如径向或轴向变化的噪声）以及空间维度增加时优势最明显，且资源消耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PDE发现方法使用通用的导数单项式库，未利用系统本身的物理对称性（刚体运动对称性），导致在噪声条件下（尤其是噪声本身破坏对称性时）候选库臃肿、效率低下且精度不佳。

Method: 假设底层动力学在所有刚体运动下具有对称性，构建由刚体运动等变候选项组成的库（而非通用导数单项式库），并与稀疏回归方法结合，在五个不同方程上进行基准测试比较。

Result: 该方法在噪声破坏刚体运动对称性（如径向或轴向变化噪声）时优势最为显著；在高维空间中同样表现更优，且更为节省计算资源。

Conclusion: 将刚体运动对称性先验嵌入候选库构建中，是比通用库更有效且更高效的PDE发现策略，尤其适用于对称动力学系统和高维/结构化噪声场景。

Abstract: We consider the problem of PDE discovery from possibly noisy observations under the hypothesis that the underlying dynamic is symmetric in all rigid motions. Rather than using a generic library of derivative monomials, we leverage this assumption to construct libraries whose candidate terms are themselves rigid-motion-equivariant, and combine them with sparse regression to benchmark such libraries over five different equations. The advantage is most pronounced when the noise itself breaks rigid-motion symmetry (e.g., radially or axially varying noise), and when the ambient spatial dimension increases, in which case they are also less resource intensive.

</details>


### [200] [LU Factorization of Discrete Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08998)
*Samuel Orellana Mateo,John Urschel,Nicholas West*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究离散随机矩阵的强非奇异性（所有前导主子矩阵均非奇异，等价于存在LU分解）。证明了对任意有限支撑且|ξ|∞<1的离散随机变量ξ，Mn(ξ)以常概率强非奇异且增长因子有界于n^{5/2+δ}；并给出了|ξ|∞→0时该概率的渐近下界的紧确估计；最后提供了直到n=9的强非奇异二元矩阵的精确计数，并据此推导了Bernoulli情形下的改进上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散随机矩阵的强非奇异性（即所有前导主子矩阵非奇异），这一性质与LU分解的存在性直接相关。此前的随机矩阵研究大多关注整体非奇异性，而对强非奇异性的概率和增长因子边界缺乏系统分析，尤其是对有限支撑的离散随机矩阵。

Method: 采用概率论方法对离散随机矩阵的强非奇异性进行分析。利用LU分解的结构性质建立强非奇异与增长因子之间的联系，通过概率估计推导常概率下界；通过分析有限支撑离散分布的特殊结构，研究|ξ|∞→0的渐近行为；对于Bernoulli情形，通过枚举和精确计数（直到n=9）来推导改进的上界。

Result: 证明了对于任意有限支撑且|ξ|∞<1的离散随机变量ξ，矩阵Mn(ξ)以常数概率强非奇异，且此时增长因子以n^{5/2+δ}为界；给出了|ξ|∞→0时概率的紧渐近下界；提供了n≤9的强非奇异二元矩阵的精确计数，并据此改进了Bernoulli情形的上界。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了离散随机矩阵强非奇异性的概率行为：其在有限支撑离散分布下以常数概率成立，且增长因子的多项式阶上界可以保证。同时，精确计数结果为进一步优化Bernoulli情形下的概率上界提供了新工具。

Abstract: We consider the probability that a discrete random matrix $M_n(ξ)$ is \emph{strongly non-singular}, meaning all its leading principal submatrices are non-singular. This property is equivalent to the existence of an LU factorization. We show that for any discrete random variable $ξ$ with finite support and $|ξ|_\infty < 1$, there is a constant probability that $M_n(ξ)$ is strongly non-singular with a growth factor bounded by $n^{5/2+δ}$. Furthermore, we provide a tight asymptotic lower bound for this probability as $|ξ|_\infty \to 0$. Finally, we provide exact counts for strongly non-singular binary matrices up to $n=9$ and use these to derive improved upper bounds for the Bernoulli case.

</details>


### [201] [Two-Step MV-DeepONet: Probabilistic Operator Learning for Uncertainty Propagation Driven by Random Input Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09071)
*Yupei Nie,Lei Wang,Jiasen Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两步均值-方差DeepONet（two-step MV-DeepONet），通过在低维旋转系数空间进行高斯概率建模，实现了物理输出空间中非对角条件预测协方差的轻量级恢复，同时保持单次前向推理的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂物理系统中的前向不确定性传播会在场值输出上产生结构化协方差。现有Prob-DeepONet虽能提供轻量级不确定性量化，但其条件预测协方差被限制为对角形式，无法刻画跨位置的依赖关系；而直接参数化完整高维协方差矩阵又过于昂贵。

Method: 两步MV-DeepONet通过两项关键修改实现：(1) 两步训练解耦输出基学习与输入到系数映射，并引入基正交化与子空间旋转；(2) 将高斯概率建模从高维物理输出空间转移到低维旋转系数空间，再通过共享基映射回物理空间，从而自然产生非对角的条件预测协方差，且保持单次前向推理。同时给出Frobenius范数误差分解与上界，识别低秩协方差可压缩性、主干子空间逼近、有限样本统计误差和系数空间协方差估计为影响协方差恢复的主要因素。

Result: 在三个代表性PDE控制问题及高超声速钝体气动热问题上，该方法相比Prob-DeepONet展现了更好的泛化能力、更结构化的不确定带以及更准确的非对角相关模式恢复。

Conclusion: 通过将概率建模转移到低维系数空间，该方法实现了在单次推理下对跨位置条件协方差的非对角结构恢复，为高维场输出的结构化不确定性量化提供了一种高效且准确的方案。

Abstract: Forward uncertainty propagation in complex physical systems can induce structured covariance across field-valued outputs. For a probabilistic surrogate, the total predictive covariance comprises the covariance of conditional means across input realizations and the average conditional predictive covariance. Probabilistic DeepONet (Prob-DeepONet) provides lightweight uncertainty quantification by predicting pointwise Gaussian means and variances in a single forward pass, but its conditional predictive covariance is restricted to a diagonal form. To represent cross-location conditional dependence without explicitly parameterizing a full high-dimensional covariance matrix, we develop a two-step mean-variance DeepONet (two-step MV-DeepONet) through two principal modifications. First, two-step training is used to decouple output-basis learning from the input-to-coefficient mapping, together with basis orthogonalization and subspace rotation. Second, Gaussian probabilistic modeling is transferred from the high-dimensional physical output space to the low-dimensional rotated coefficient space. Mapping these probabilistic coefficients through the shared basis induces a generally non-diagonal conditional predictive covariance in the physical output space while retaining single-pass inference. A Frobenius-norm error decomposition and corresponding upper bound identify low-rank covariance compressibility, trunk-subspace approximation, finite-sample statistical error, and coefficient-space covariance estimation as the principal factors governing covariance recovery. Numerical experiments on three representative problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) and a hypersonic blunt-body aerothermal problem show improved generalization, more structured uncertainty bands, and accurate recovery of off-diagonal correlation patterns compared with Prob-DeepONet.

</details>


### [202] [Joint Lyapunov Certificates for K-Agent Generative AI Governance: Stochastic Stability, Emergent Ensemble Risk, and Zero-Knowledge Governance Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09087)
*Sriram Nagaraj*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个严格的数学框架，用于在模型风险管理（MRM）原则下治理由K个自适应生成式AI模型组成的系统。核心发现是：当多个模型通过交互矩阵形成元学习耦合时，标准的单代理Lyapunov分析不足以保证系统稳定性，并提出了联合Lyapunov证明（JLP）协议来在不泄露专有权重的情况下验证系统的聚合动态满足MRM持续监控标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模型共享元学习耦合（通过交互矩阵）的场景下，标准MRM依赖的逐代理Lyapunov分析被证明是不充分的——每个智能体可以各自满足其声明的稳定性界限，但整个联合系统可能处于涌现性的系统级漂移状态。这一安全缺口需要新的形式化框架来弥合。

Method: 提出了联合Lyapunov证明（JLP）协议，这是一个结合密码学与随机分析的协议，能够在不暴露专有权重的前提下证明聚合动态在每个验证周期满足MRM持续监控标准。具体包括：完整刻画联合二次Lyapunov函数的无穷小生成元；推导临界耦合阈值的精确值；证明噪声底限定理；基于实时权重构建逐周期的简洁非交互式知识论证（SNARK）。

Result: 理论贡献包括：联合二次Lyapunov函数无穷小生成元的完整刻画、系统失去均方稳定性的精确临界耦合阈值、噪声底限定理以及零知识证明的正确目标识别。所有理论声明均通过五个多智能体softmax系统的数值研究得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了多模型耦合场景下标准MRM分析与真实系统行为之间的形式化鸿沟，为在保护专有模型权重的约束下实现系统级稳定性监管提供了可行的密码学-随机组合验证框架。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the governance of systems of K self-adapting generative AI models under the principles of
  Model Risk Management (MRM). When multiple models share a
  meta-learning coupling through an interaction matrix, the
  per-agent Lyapunov analysis that underpins standard MRM is provably insufficient: individual agents can each satisfy their
  declared stability bounds while the joint system is in a
  regime of emergent ensemble-level drift. We formalize this gap through the
  Joint Lyapunov Proof (JLP)---a cryptographic and stochastic
  protocol that attests, without revealing proprietary weights, that the
  aggregate dynamics satisfy MRM Ongoing Monitoring standard at every validation epoch.
  Our main contributions are the following. We give a complete
  characterization of the infinitesimal generator of the joint
  quadratic Lyapunov function. We derive the exact critical coupling threshold
  above which the system loses mean-square stability. We prove a
  Noise-Floor Theorem and identify the correct target for zero-knowledge attestation.
  A per-epoch Succinct Non-Interactive Argument of Knowledge (SNARK) on the live weights is derived. All
  theoretical claims are validated against five numerical studies using a multi-agent softmax system.

</details>


### [203] [Efficient Multigrid Methods for Semi-implicit Landau-Lifshitz Schemes in Micromagnetic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09102)
*Changjian Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的基于聚合的多重网格方法，用于求解微磁学中Landau-Lifshitz方程离散化后产生的线性代数系统，该方法通过定制平滑算子和粗网格校正算子，实现了网格无关收敛并大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微磁学模拟中Landau-Lifshitz方程离散化产生的线性系统具有稀疏谱特性和非对称矩阵结构，标准迭代方法和面向对称问题的传统多重网格求解器在网格细化下收敛性能退化、计算效率低下，现有算法难以处理这些固有特征。

Method: 采用一阶向后差分公式结合一阶外推进行时间离散化，针对目标线性系统定制了多重网格框架中的平滑算子和粗网格校正算子，构建基于聚合（aggregation-based）的多重网格求解器。

Result: 数值实验证实了该方法的鲁棒性和网格无关收敛性。与成熟的Krylov子空间求解器和传统多重网格技术相比，该聚合型多重网格求解器显著减少了迭代次数和总体计算开销，同时能够忠实再现磁化动力学行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的聚合型多重网格方法是求解微磁学中Landau-Lifshitz方程离散线性系统的有效求解器，在计算效率和收敛性方面优于现有方法，适用于大规模微磁学模拟。

Abstract: An efficient aggregation-based multigrid approach is developed in this work to solve linear algebraic systems generated by discretizing the Landau-Lifshitz equation in micromagnetics. The discretization couples the first-order backward differentiation formula with first-order extrapolation, and the underlying equation describes magnetization dynamics within micromagnetic simulations. For these linear systems, standard iterative methods and conventional multigrid solvers intended for symmetric problems suffer from deteriorated convergence under mesh refinement and poor computational performance. Existing algorithms struggle to handle the system's intrinsic features, namely its sparse spectral properties and non-symmetric matrix structure. To overcome these limitations, a multigrid framework is constructed, where smoothing and coarse-grid correction operators are customized for the target linear system. Numerical tests confirm the robustness and mesh-independent convergence of the resulting method. Compared with well-established Krylov-subspace solvers and conventional multigrid techniques, the aggregation-based multigrid solver cuts down iteration counts and overall computational cost considerably, while producing faithful representations of magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [204] [A simple second-order nonstandard numerical method for a general class of dynamical systems and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09141)
*Manh Tuan Hoang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一类连续时间自治动力系统，构造了一个二阶非标准有限差分（NSFD）方法，在任意有限步长下同时保持系统解的正性、平衡点集合及其渐近稳定性。通过合适的非标准分母函数和加权离散化实现，并应用于具有补充的两阶段结构化物种模型，数值实验验证了其优于显式梯形法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NSFD方法大多为一阶精度，而高精度数值方法（如显式梯形法）往往无法在所有步长下保持动力系统的关键性质（正性、平衡点稳定性等）。因此需要构造一种既能保持动态一致性（dynamic consistency）又具有二阶收敛精度的数值方法，以兼顾精度与结构保持能力。

Method: 该方法基于两类关键技术构建：（1）选择合适的非标准分母函数，在容易验证的条件下保证二阶收敛；（2）对右端函数采用加权离散化方式，以确保动态一致性。与传统非局部离散化方法不同，本文利用右端函数的特殊结构采用简单离散化策略，从而简化构造过程并使渐近稳定性分析更易进行。

Result: 将该二阶NSFD方法应用于具有补充的两阶段结构化物种模型，推导出简单的二阶NSFD格式，优于此前工作构造的一阶NSFD格式。数值实验表明该二阶NSFD方法显著优于标准二阶数值方法（显式梯形法）。

Conclusion: 所提出的NSFD方法简单通用，适用于理论和应用中出现的广泛动力系统模型，并且可以方便地与Richardson外推技术结合以进一步提高精度。

Abstract: In this work, we consider a class of continuous-time autonomous dynamical systems that model various important phenomena and processes encountered in real-world situations. We construct a second-order nonstandard finite difference (NSFD) method that simultaneously preserves two essential properties of the dynamical systems for all finite step sizes, namely the positivity of the solutions, the set of equilibrium points and their asymptotic stability. This NSFD method is constructed based on an appropriate choice of nonstandard denominator functions and a weighted discretization of the right-hand side functions. Under easily-verified conditions, the denominator functions guarantee second-order convergence, whereas the weights ensure the dynamic consistency. By taking advantage of the specific structure of the right-hand side functions, a simple discretization is utilized instead of the nonlocal discretization approaches commonly used in previous works. This simplifies the construction of the proposed NSFD method and, in particular, makes its asymptotic stability analysis easier.
  As an illustration and an important application, we apply the constructed second-order NSFD method to a well-known two-stage structured species model with recruitment. Consequently, a simple second-order NSFD scheme for the considered two-stage structured species model is derived, improving upon a first-order NSFD scheme constructed in a previous work. Numerical experiments demonstrate the advantages of the second-order NSFD scheme over a standard second-order numerical method, namely, the explicit trapezoidal method.
  The proposed NSFD method is simple and can be applied to a broad class of dynamical system models arising in both theory and applications. Moreover, it can be readily combined with the Richardson extrapolation technique to improve its accuracy.

</details>


### [205] [On Energy Laws and Stability of First-Subdiagonal Pade Approximants for Linear Seminegative Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09239)
*Miaosen Jiao,Zheng Sun,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性半负问题，推导了由指数函数的第一子对角Padé逼近生成的有理时间离散格式的显式离散能量恒等式，将先前对角Padé能量律推广至第一子对角族，并通过显式Cholesky型因子分解实现了无条件收缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作[Sun, Wei, Wu, SIAM J. Numer. Anal., 60 (2022)]建立了对角Padé有理时间离散格式的离散能量律，但第一子对角Padé族尚缺乏相应的能量恒等式。该族在时间离散中具有重要应用价值，亟需将其能量耗散理论拓展至这一格式族。

Method: 核心新工具是能量系数矩阵的显式Cholesky型因子分解，该矩阵关联离散能量恒等式中的半内积项。由于矩阵元素是Padé系数的交错和且三角因子具有依赖于奇偶性的阶乘结构，因子分解的构造与证明均非平凡。证明通过将因子分解归结为标量有理恒等式，并利用有限乘积约化和望远镜求和实现。

Result: 结合β系数消去，因子分解产生了精确的离散能量律，恢复了对线性半负问题的经典无条件收缩性。数值实验验证了预测的收敛阶并确认了离散耗散恒等式成立。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了第一子对角Padé格式在能量耗散理论上的空白，为更广泛的有理时间离散化格式提供了统一的能量分析方法论。

Abstract: We derive an explicit discrete energy identity for rational time discretizations generated by the first-subdiagonal Padé approximants of the exponential for solving linear seminegative problems. This work extends the diagonal Padé energy laws in [Z. Sun, Y. Wei, and K. Wu, SIAM J. Numer. Anal., 60 (2022)] to the first-subdiagonal family. The main new ingredient is an explicit Cholesky-type factorization of the energy coefficient matrix associated with the semi-inner-product terms in the discrete energy identity. The construction and proof of this factorization are nontrivial, since the matrix entries are alternating sums of Padé coefficients and the triangular factor has a parity-dependent factorial structure. We prove the factorization by reducing it to scalar rational identities and establishing them through finite product reductions and telescoping summations. Together with a β-coefficient cancellation, the factorization yields an exact discrete energy law that recovers the classical unconditional contractivity for linear seminegative problems. Numerical experiments adapted from the diagonal Padé energy-law setting illustrate the predicted order and verify the discrete dissipation identity.

</details>


### [206] [High-order fully discrete multi-entropy-stable and bound-preserving schemes for relativistic Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09327)
*Linfeng Xu,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对特殊相对论欧拉方程，首次构建了具有"多熵稳定性"的高阶间断伽辽金和有限体积格式——即在完全离散意义上对任意有限族凸熵对均熵稳定，且在任何熵被评估处可证明保持解的物理容许性（密度和压力的正性）。这些性质仅通过一个逐单元的投影即可实现，不损失守恒性或设计精度，其构建依赖于相对论因果性（特征速度以光速为界）以及容许集的凸性。数值实验涵盖四种状态方程，在高Lorentz因子（>70）的强激波、近真空激波-涡旋相互作用和射流算例中均展现高阶精度、无不合法状态，并确认所有强制的离散熵单调衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在守恒律系统中，离散熵不等式是主要的非线性稳定性估计，但其评估需要物理容许的状态。然而此前的熵稳定格式和保界限制器是两个分离的研究方向：熵稳定格式几乎都是半离散的、围绕单一熵对构建，并把密度和压力的正性视为前提；保界限制器虽保持解的可容许性却不提供熵估计。对于特殊相对论欧拉方程，保守-原始变量映射是隐式的，不可容许的状态根本没有熵可修正，因此二者无法分离。本文的核心动机是首次将"完全离散熵稳定性"与"可容许性"统一到单一框架中。

Method: 该方法的核心思想是：利用相对论因果性保证所有特征速度以光速为界，从而数值粘性可以对所有状态和所有状态方程统一固定，且一个两点构建块即可服务整个熵家族。通过仅使用容许集的凸性和速度上界构建一个逐单元的投影算子，该投影同时实现：(1) 对任意预设有限族凸熵对在完全离散意义上的熵稳定（多熵稳定性）；(2) 在任何熵被评估处解保持物理可容许性。该方法统一适用于高阶间断伽辽金格式和有限体积格式，且不损失守恒性和设计精度。

Result: 在计算中测试了四种状态方程，方案在接近真空处保持高阶精度，在强激波、近真空激波-涡旋相互作用以及Lorentz因子超过70的射流中不产生任何不可容许状态，并确认每个强制的离散熵均单调衰减。这些数值结果验证了多熵稳定性和可容许性在同一方案中的同时实现。

Conclusion: 通过利用相对论因果性（所有特征速度以光速为界）和容许集的凸性，本文成功地将"完全离散的熵稳定性对任意有限族凸熵对（多熵稳定）"与"可证明的可容许性"统一到单个逐单元投影中，且不损失守恒性或设计精度。由于仅依赖这两个一般性质，该构造方法对相关的双曲系统具有可推广性。

Abstract: A discrete entropy inequality is the principal nonlinear stability estimate available for systems of conservation laws, and evaluating it presupposes a physically admissible state. So far, however, the two have been secured separately. Entropy-stable schemes are almost always semi-discrete, are built around one selected entropy pair, and take for granted the positivity of density and pressure that makes the entropy well defined in the first place, whereas bound-preserving limiters keep the solution admissible but deliver no entropy estimate. For the special relativistic Euler equations, the two cannot be separated at all, since the conservative-to-primitive map is implicit, and an inadmissible state therefore has no entropy to correct. Here we construct high-order discontinuous Galerkin and finite volume schemes that, to our knowledge, for the first time, are entropy stable in the fully discrete sense for an arbitrary prescribed finite family of convex entropy pairs, a property we call multi-entropy stability, and are provably admissible wherever an entropy is evaluated. All of this is achieved by a single cellwise projection, and neither conservation nor the design order is lost. The construction rests on relativistic causality, which bounds every characteristic speed by the speed of light. Consequently, the numerical viscosity can be fixed once for all states and all equations of state, and one two-point building block then serves the whole entropy family. Since only the convexity of the admissible set and this speed bound are used, the same route remains open for related systems. Finally, in computations with four equations of state, the schemes retain high-order accuracy close to vacuum, produce no inadmissible state in strong shocks, near-vacuum shock--vortex interaction or jets with Lorentz factor above $70$, and confirm the monotone decay of every enforced discrete entropy.

</details>


### [207] [A nonlocal nonlinear Schr{ö}dinger model:well-posedness, local limit, and structure-preservingasymptotically compatible Fourier approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09372)
*Jiashu Lu,Yufeng Nie,Xinning Xie,Pingrui Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了周期域上的非局部非线性薛定谔（NLS）模型，建立了其适定性、守恒律、局部极限和色散性质，并开发了一种保结构的渐近相容Fourier配点方法。理论分析和数值实验表明该方法在非局部参数、时间和空间离散上均具有最优收敛阶且无耦合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部模型在描述长程相互作用和尺度效应方面优于传统局部模型，但非局部NLS方程的数值方法和理论分析尚不完善，特别是缺乏可同时处理非局部参数趋零（局部极限）与离散参数加密的、具有渐近相容性的保结构数值格式。

Method: 本文建立非局部NLS模型的理论框架，包括全局适定性、质量和非局部能量守恒、O(δ²)局部极限以及色散关系与群速度分析。在数值方法上，采用Crank-Nicolson时间推进结合Fourier配点空间离散，该格式保持网格质量和原始网格能量，并在无δ-τ-N耦合条件下实现渐近相容性。

Result: 非局部解的H^s误差为O(τ²+N^{s-r})，且关于非局部水平δ一致成立；相对于局部NLS解的总H^s误差为O(δ²+τ²+N^{s-r})，无需任何耦合条件。一维、二维和三维数值实验验证了理论精度、离散守恒及独立收敛性。

Conclusion: 该非局部NLS模型及所提出的渐近相容保结构Fourier配点方法在理论和数值上均表现优异，且非局部参数、时间步长和空间截断可独立选取，为非局部色散波传播的数值模拟提供了可靠且高效的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) model on periodic domains, and establish its well-posedness, conservation laws, local limit, dispersion properties, and develop a structure-preserving asymptotically compatible Fourier collocation method. We prove that for a fixed nonlocal horizon $δ$, the model is globally well posed and conserves mass and nonlocal energy, and the nonlocal NLS solution converges to the local NLS solution with order $O(δ^2)$ under suitable regularity assumptions. We also derive the dispersion relation and group velocity for plane waves, establish their $O(δ^2)$ local limits, and characterize their behavior at high frequencies. The numerical method combines Crank--Nicolson time stepping with Fourier collocation and preserves the grid mass and the original grid energy. With time step $τ$, Fourier cutoff $N$, and the assumed $H^r$ regularity of exact solution, the $H^s$-error for the nonlocal solution is $O(τ^2+N^{s-r})$, uniformly with respect to the horizon. Moreover, its total $H^s$-error relative to the local NLS solution is $O(δ^2+τ^2+N^{s-r})$ without any coupling condition among $δ$, $τ$, and $N$, which proves asymptotic compatibility of the proposed method. Numerical experiments in one, two, and three dimensions are presented to verify the theoretical accuracy and discrete conservation, confirm convergence under independent variation of horizon and discretization parameters, and show how the horizon and kernel affect dispersive wave propagation.

</details>


### [208] [poly_rust: a domain-specific language for polytopal methods with a Rust interpreter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09414)
*Daniele A. Di Pietro*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了poly_rust，一个实现多面体方法领域特定语言的软件库，用户可以用接近数学公式的语法表达各种多面体方法，并通过局部变分问题重构算子替代形状函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法依赖于全局函数空间和形状函数，多面体方法通过重构相关算子支持更通用的网格，但目前缺乏一个易于使用、语法贴近数学表述的实现库。

Method: poly_rust库提供一种领域特定语言，放弃全局函数空间，直接从自由度出发通过求解局部变分问题重构相关算子，并将重构结果替代形状函数用于全局变分公式中。

Result: 文章从基础用户视角回顾了poly_rust库的主要方面，并提供了涵盖多种问题和方法的数值示例。

Conclusion: poly_rust为多面体方法的表达和实现提供了便捷的工具，其语法与数学表述高度接近，并通过数值实验验证了其适用性。

Abstract: In this article we introduce poly_rust, a new software library that implements a domain-specific language for polytopal methods. Polytopal methods support meshes much more general than those on which traditional finite element methods are built. This can be achieved by dropping global function spaces and reconstructing the relevant operators directly from the degrees of freedom through the solution of local variational problems. Reconstructions are then used in place of shape functions in the global variational formulation. poly_rust provides facilities for expressing a wide variety of polytopal methods using a syntax that closely resembles their mathematical formulation. We review here the main aspects of the library from the perspective of a basic user and provide numerical examples spanning several problems and methods.

</details>


### [209] [Automatic differentiation in finite element stress updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09442)
*Mao Ouyang,Alexandros Petalas,William M. Coombs,Charles E. Augarde*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文探讨在非线性有限元分析中引入自动微分（AD）技术，以替代传统解析微分方法实现本构模型的应力更新。通过超塑性临界状态模型在排水三轴压缩试验中的对比，证明AD消除了手动推导导数的需求，同时保持与解析方法相当的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算固体力学中，非线性有限元分析中的应力更新需要反复计算本构模型的导数，无论是隐式还是显式算法，解析推导这些导数往往复杂、易错且耗时。这成为开发复杂材料模型的瓶颈，亟需一种简化导数计算的方法。

Method: 将自动微分（AD）引入非线性有限元分析的应力更新环节，用于实现超塑性临界状态模型（广泛应用于岩土工程）。采用向后欧拉应力积分方案，分别用传统解析微分和自动微分两种方式实现本构模型导数，并在不同超固结比的排水三轴压缩试验中进行对比仿真。

Result: 自动微分显著简化了向后欧拉应力积分的实现过程，消除了手动推导导数的需要；同时保持了与解析方法相当的高精度和鲁棒性，两种方法在不同超固结比条件下的仿真结果一致。

Conclusion: 自动微分能够简化非线性材料模型的开发流程，支持任意复杂度的本构定律的高效可靠实现，为更灵活、更易维护的计算力学代码开辟了道路。

Abstract: Computational solid mechanics relies on the discretisation of differential equations, requiring numerical differentiation and integration, often via quadrature. Recently, automatic differentiation (AD) has attracted interest as a tool to support these operations. This paper introduces the use of AD in nonlinear finite element analysis, focusing on stress updating, a key and repeatedly executed step in which stresses are computed from strain increments. Both implicit and explicit schemes require derivatives of constitutive models, which can be complex, error prone, and time consuming to derive analytically. We compare traditional analytical differentiation with automatic differentiation for implementing a hyperplastic Critical State model widely used in geotechnical engineering. Drained triaxial compression tests with varying overconsolidation ratios are simulated using both approaches. The results show that AD significantly simplifies the implementation of backward Euler stress integration by removing the need for manual derivation of derivatives. Despite this simplification, AD maintains high accuracy and robustness comparable to analytical approaches. The findings demonstrate that automatic differentiation can streamline the development of nonlinear material models, enabling efficient and reliable implementation of constitutive laws of arbitrary complexity. This opens the way for more flexible and maintainable computational mechanics codes.

</details>


### [210] [Walk-on-Spheres Monte Carlo and deep neural network approximations of elliptic PDEs with drift and killing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09494)
*Konrad Kleinberg,Thomas Kruse*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带常数扩散、漂移和杀伤项的线性椭圆偏微分方程的随机表示，提出蒙特卡洛和深度神经网络逼近方法，基于修改的Walk-on-Spheres算法建立均匀误差界，并证明了样本复杂度和网络参数量随逆精度和维度多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Beznea等）提出了修改的Walk-on-Spheres算法，但此前复杂度分析仅适用于较窄的椭圆方程类别。本文旨在将该分析推广到包含漂移和杀伤项的更广泛椭圆方程，回答这些更一般情形下蒙特卡洛估计器和深度神经网络逼近的样本/参数复杂度是否仍能多项式增长的问题。

Method: 基于Beznea等人的修改Walk-on-Spheres算法，构造显式纳入随机时间的蒙特卡洛估计器；建立均匀误差界并分析样本复杂度；在假设边界数据和到边界距离函数可由神经网络良好表示的前提下，利用蒙特卡洛估计器设计深度神经网络对随机表示进行均匀逼近。

Result: 建立了蒙特卡洛估计器的均匀误差界；在适当假设下，达到指定逼近精度所需的样本量随逆精度和问题维度最多多项式增长；同时证明了深度神经网络逼近结果，其参数量也随逆精度和维度最多多项式增长。

Conclusion: 本文的结果将此前仅适用于简单椭圆方程的复杂度分析，成功扩展到了包含漂移和杀伤项的更广泛的线性椭圆PDE类别，为高维椭圆问题的数值求解提供了理论和算法支撑。

Abstract: In this paper we provide Monte Carlo and deep neural network approximations for stochastic representations of solutions to linear elliptic partial differential equations with constant diffusion, drift and killing. Building on the modified Walk-on-Spheres algorithm of Beznea et al. (arXiv:2209.01432), we introduce Monte Carlo estimators that explicitly incorporate sampled random times arising in the analyzed stochastic representations. We establish uniform error bounds for these estimators and show that, under suitable assumptions, a prescribed approximation accuracy is achieved with sample complexities growing at most polynomially in both the inverse accuracy and the problem dimension. Furthermore, we prove a deep neural network approximation result for the stochastic representations. Assuming suitable neural network representations of the boundary data and the distance function to the boundary, we use the constructed Monte Carlo to design deep neural networks that approximate the representation uniformly with a number of parameters growing at most polynomially in the inverse accuracy and the problem dimension. These results extend previous complexity analyses to a broader class of elliptic equations involving drift and killing.

</details>


### [211] [Conditional Uniqueness and Optimal Energy-Norm Convergence for the Dynamic Diffusion Finite Element Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09505)
*Isaac P. Santos*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Santos等人(2021)针对稳态对流-扩散-反应问题提出的非线性双尺度动态扩散(DD)有限元格式，在不修改离散格式的前提下建立了两个新的理论结果：人工扩散算子的局部Lipschitz连续性及由此导出的离散解条件唯一性，以及最优一阶能量范数先验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Santos等人(2021)提出的非线性双尺度动态扩散有限元格式虽然已有数学分析，但其能量范数收敛阶尚不明确——此前仅得出O(h^{1/2})的估计，且该估计针对的是一个更强的组合误差度量，无法确定能量误差本身的收敛速率。作者希望在不改变离散格式的前提下，深化对该格式的理论理解，特别是建立更精确的能量范数估计并验证离散解的唯一性。

Method: 通过两个理论途径展开分析：(1)证明定义非线性扩散算子D_h的人工扩散系数关于离散解是局部Lipschitz连续的，进而基于网格相关的小量条件推导离散解的唯 一性；(2)将逼近误差与人工扩散贡献在能量范数下分离，推导连续分片线性有限元的一阶最优能量范数先验估计，同时澄清此前O(h^{1/2})估计的适用范围。辅以纯对流-扩散及对流-扩散-反应问题的数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 建立了人工扩散算子的局部Lipschitz连续性及离散解的条件唯一性；推导出连续分片线性元的最优一阶能量范数先验估计；数值实验证实了能量范数的一阶收敛行为以及人工耗散度量平方根的一阶衰减；强对流主导测试进一步展示了D_h的稳定化效果。

Conclusion: 这些结果在不改变原始DD格式离散结构的前提下，深化了其数学理论理解，明确了此前O(h^{1/2})估计的局限（适用于更强的组合误差度量而非能量误差本身），并确立了该格式在能量范数下的最优一阶收敛性。

Abstract: We revisit the nonlinear two-scale Dynamic Diffusion (DD) finite element formulation mathematically analyzed by Santos et al. (2021) for stationary advection--diffusion--reaction problems. Two additional theoretical results are established without modifying the discrete formulation. First, the artificial diffusivity defining the nonlinear diffusion operator $D_h$ is shown to be locally Lipschitz continuous with respect to the discrete solution. This property leads to conditional uniqueness of the discrete solution under a mesh-dependent smallness condition. Second, by separating the approximation error in the energy norm from the contribution associated with artificial diffusion, we derive an optimal first-order a priori energy-norm estimate for continuous piecewise linear finite elements. This result also clarifies the earlier $O(h^{1/2})$ estimate, which applies to a stronger combined error measure and therefore does not determine the convergence rate of the energy error alone. Numerical experiments for both pure advection--diffusion and advection--diffusion--reaction problems corroborate the predicted first-order energy-norm behavior and the first-order decay of the square-root artificial-dissipation measure. A strongly convection-dominated test with sharp outflow layers further illustrates the stabilizing effect associated with $D_h$. These results extend the mathematical understanding of the original DD formulation while preserving its underlying discrete structure.

</details>


### [212] [A block preconditioner with two parameters for double saddle point systems in liquid crystal director modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09603)
*Mehdi Makhdomi,Davod Khojasteh Salkuyeh,Morad Ahmadnasab*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类用于液晶指向矢建模的有限元离散双鞍点系统的块预条件子，建立了迭代收敛的充分条件，对预条件矩阵进行了谱分析以指导参数选择，并利用灵活的GMRES加速收敛，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双鞍点系统在液晶指向矢建模的有限元离散中广泛出现，求解此类系统的迭代方法通常收敛缓慢，亟需高效的预条件子来加速求解。

Method: 提出一种新型块预条件子；建立迭代格式收敛的充分条件；对预条件矩阵进行谱分析，进而制定有效的参数选择策略；结合灵活的GMRES（FGMRES）加速迭代收敛。

Result: 实验结果表明该预条件子在加速FGMRES收敛方面表现出优异的性能，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该新型块预条件子对液晶指向矢建模产生的双鞍点系统具有高效的加速效果，结合FGMRES可显著提升求解效率。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel block preconditioner for a class of double saddle point systems arising from the finite element discretization of liquid crystal director modeling. We first establish sufficient conditions to guarantee the convergence of the underlying iterative scheme. A spectral analysis of the preconditioned matrix is then performed, leading to an effective strategy for parameter selection. The new preconditioner is used to accelerate the convergence of the flexible version of GMRES. Experimental results confirm the strong performance of the method.

</details>


### [213] [A novel compact scheme for second-order fluxes applied to the Spectral Difference method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09615)
*Guido Lodato,Niccolò Tonicello*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于谱差分（SD）方法中二阶粘性项的紧凑、全中心格式，通过修改辅助梯度的重构方式（采用界面依赖的单侧连续通量），将Bassi-Rebay（BR1）格式的五元素模板缩减为三元素，同时保留BR1的中心性和无参数特性，恢复所有多项式阶次的收敛阶并在湍流计算中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不连续谱元法（DSEM）中二阶（粘性）项的离散通常依赖辅助梯度变量，其在界面处的处理影响格式的精度和稳定性。BR1格式虽简单且无参数，但在偶数多项式阶次存在次优收敛，且需要五元素扩展模板，这促使作者开发更紧凑且保持精度的高阶格式。

Method: 受Huynh的Flux Reconstruction框架启发，作者在SD方法中修改辅助梯度的重构，使用界面依赖的单侧连续通量，将模板从五元素缩减为三元素，同时保持格式的中心性和无参数特性。格式在一维中开发并扩展到多维。利用时间特征值分析表征耗散和色散特性，包括内罚项的影响。

Result: 数值测试涵盖线性扩散方程、分辨不足的局部Dirac delta、非线性多孔介质方程以及Re=1600和5000的三维Taylor-Green涡的隐式大涡模拟。结果表明：紧凑格式恢复了包括偶数阶在内的所有多项式阶次的预期收敛阶；减少了分辨不足和非线性区域的寄生振荡；在标准格式失效的湍流算例中仍保持稳定，这归因于对高波数数值模式阻尼的改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑格式为SD方法中二阶通量的BR1格式提供了一种有吸引力的紧凑替代方案，兼顾了精度、稳定性和计算效率。

Abstract: The discretization of second-order (viscous) terms in Discontinuous Spectral Element Methods (DSEMs) typically relies on an auxiliary gradient variable, whose treatment at element interfaces affects the accuracy and stability of the scheme. The Bassi-Rebay (BR1) formulation is attractive for its simplicity and parameter-free character, but suffers from sub-optimal convergence at even polynomial orders and requires an extended five-element stencil. Inspired by Huynh's Flux Reconstruction formulation, we develop a compact, fully-centered scheme for second-order fluxes within the Spectral Difference (SD) method. The proposed approach modifies the reconstruction of the auxiliary gradient using interface-dependent, one-sided continuous fluxes, reducing the stencil from five to three elements while preserving the centered and parameter-free nature of BR1. The formulation is developed in one dimension and extended to multiple dimensions. Temporal eigenanalysis is used to characterize its dissipation and dispersion properties, including the effects of interior penalty terms. Numerical tests consider the linear diffusion equation, an under-resolved localized Dirac's delta, the nonlinear porous medium equation, and implicit large-eddy simulations of the three-dimensional Taylor-Green vortex at $\mathrm{Re}=1600$ and $5000$. The compact scheme restores the expected convergence order for all polynomial degrees, including even orders, and reduces spurious oscillations in under-resolved and nonlinear regimes. It also remains stable in turbulent cases where the standard formulation fails, owing to improved damping of high-wavenumber numerical modes. The proposed approach provides an attractive compact alternative to BR1 for second-order fluxes in the SD method.

</details>


### [214] [Diffusion Maps Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09623)
*John Harlim,Daning Huang,Jiwoo Song*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散映射（DM）核的核岭回归，证明DM核在适当的缩放和带宽条件下均匀收敛到流形上的热核，其诱导的RKHS与热核RKHS一致，并与高斯核RKHS等距同构、连续嵌入Matérn核RKHS，从而适用Matérn核的标准风险界，最后通过数值实验验证了收敛性及DM核在监督学习中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散映射核作为一种数据驱动的核方法，在流形学习中应用广泛，但其理论性质（特别是其诱导的RKHS结构与核岭回归的风险界）缺乏严格分析。作者希望通过证明DM核与热核的收敛关系，建立其RKHS与已知核（高斯核、Matérn核）之间的嵌入关系，从而将成熟的核岭回归理论风险界推广到DM核上。

Method: 在数据均匀采样自流形的假设下，通过适当的核缩放和带宽调节，证明DM核在数据集规模增大时一致收敛到流形上的热核；在此基础上分析DM核诱导的RKHS结构，建立了与高斯核RKHS的等距同构关系以及与Matérn核RKHS的连续嵌入关系；并通过数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: (1) 证明了DM核一致收敛到热核，其极限RKHS与热核RKHS一致；(2) 建立了DM核RKHS与高斯核RKHS的等距同构以及与Matérn核RKHS的连续嵌入关系，使得Matérn核的标准风险界适用于DM核；(3) 数值实验验证了热核近似的收敛性，并展示了DM核在处理带边界流形上的函数学习及变频率、变余维数函数时的优势。

Conclusion: DM核在理论上与热核一致，其RKHS嵌入关系保证了核岭回归的风险界；在实践中，DM核对高斯核在表达性和学习复杂函数（变化频率、余维数）方面具有显著优势，是一种理论上可靠且实践上有力的数据驱动核方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study kernel ridge regression using the data-driven diffusion maps (DM) kernel, which is constructed through algebraic manipulations of the diffusion maps algorithm. Under the assumptions that the data lie on a manifold and are sampled uniformly, we prove that the appropriately scaled DM kernel converges uniformly to the heat kernel on the manifold for sufficiently large times as the dataset size increases and the kernel bandwidth is scaled appropriately. Consequently, the limiting Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS) induced by the DM kernel coincides with the RKHS associated with the heat kernel on the manifold. We further show that the RKHS induced by the DM kernel is isometrically isomorphic to the RKHS of the Gaussian kernel, which is continuously embedded in the RKHS of a Matérn kernel whose norm is equivalent to an appropriate Sobolev norm. This result implies that standard risk bounds for kernel ridge regression applicable to Matérn kernels also apply to the DM kernel. Finally, we provide numerical results that (1) validate the convergence of the heat kernel approximation, (2) demonstrate the greater expressiveness of the DM kernel compared to the Gaussian kernel for supervised learning over a larger class of functions on manifolds with boundary, and (3) demonstrate the advantage of the DM kernel over the Gaussian kernel in learning functions with varying frequencies and co-dimensions.

</details>


### [215] [Carleman--Picard and time-dimensional reduction for inverse initial-data problems in nonlinear transport with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09665)
*Navaraj Neupane,Loc H. Nguyen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有非线性和记忆效应的拟线性输运方程的反演初值问题，提出了一种结合Legendre-指数时间维数约简与Carleman加权Tikhonov正则化Picard迭代的方法，并证明了收敛性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有非线性和记忆效应的拟线性输运方程，从出流边界的时间依赖测量数据重建未知初始状态是一个具有挑战性的反问题。现有方法难以同时处理非线性、记忆效应以及噪声数据下的稳定重构。

Method: 首先利用Legendre-指数时间维数约简将原方程转化为有限维非线性空间方程组；随后提出一种Carleman加权且经过Tikhonov正则化的Picard方法。每次迭代中非线性项使用上一次迭代值进行线性化，从而转化为具有唯一解的线性最小化问题。利用主输运算子的Carleman估计证明当Carleman参数足够大时，Picard映射在预设可行集上是压缩的。

Result: 理论层面证明了该方法从可行集中任意初始猜测出发均收敛，并建立了对含噪声出流数据的稳定性。二维数值实验表明该方法能够准确重构单个和多个夹杂物，对噪声具有鲁棒性，且Picard迭代收敛迅速。

Conclusion: 本文为带记忆效应的拟线性输运方程反演初值问题提供了一种理论上有保障、数值上高效的求解框架，兼顾了非线性处理、噪声稳定性和快速收敛。

Abstract: We study an inverse initial-data problem for a quasilinear transport equation with nonlinear and memory effects. The unknown initial state is reconstructed from time-dependent measurements on the outflow boundary, with prescribed inflow data. We first apply a Legendre--exponential time-dimensional reduction to transform the governing equation into a finite nonlinear system in space. We then develop a Carleman-weighted and Tikhonov-regularized Picard method. At each iteration, the nonlinear terms are evaluated using the previous iterate, leading to a linear minimization problem with a unique solution. A Carleman estimate for the principal transport operator is used to prove that, for a sufficiently large Carleman parameter, the resulting Picard map is contractive on a prescribed admissible set. Consequently, the method converges from an arbitrary initial guess in that set. We also establish stability with respect to noisy outflow data. These analytical results concern the truncated and regularized reduced problem. Two-dimensional numerical experiments demonstrate accurate reconstruction of single and multiple inclusions, robustness with respect to noise, and rapid convergence of the Picard iteration.

</details>


### [216] [Information geometric regularization for computing sensitivities of flows with shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09759)
*Florian Schäfer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文推导了信息几何正则化（IGR）可压缩欧拉方程系统的前向和伴随敏感性，并在网格细化下证明其收敛到通过有限差分或自动微分获得的敏感性，解决了含激波流动敏感性计算中长期存在的虚假敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含激波的流动中计算（伴随）敏感性是一个长期难题。由于激波传感器和限制器导致的虚假敏感性，实践中常常被迫接受"冻结"限制器和激波传感器所带来的误差。需要一种能够避免这些问题的正则化方法。

Method: 基于最近提出的信息几何正则化（IGR），这是一种无粘性的、基于PDE的可压缩欧拉方程正则化方法，用光滑剖面替代激波而不抑制细尺度结构。本文在周期边界条件下推导IGR系统的前向和伴随敏感性，并在网格细化下验证其收敛性。

Result: 在网格细化下，IGR系统推导出的前向和伴随敏感性收敛到通过有限差分或自动微分对前向求解获得的敏感性结果。

Conclusion: IGR提供了一种可行的途径来获得含激波流动的真实敏感性，避免了传统激波传感器和限制器带来的虚假敏感性问题，为气动优化和不确定性量化等下游应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Computing (adjoint) sensitivities of flows with shocks is a longstanding problem in computational fluid dynamics. The spurious sensitivities due to shock sensors and limiters frequently force practitioners to accept the errors incurred by "freezing" limiters and shock sensors. The recently proposed information geometric regularization (IGR) is an inviscid, PDE-based regularization of the compressible Euler equations that replaces shocks with smooth profiles without damping fine-scale structures. This work derives the forward and adjoint sensitivities for the IGR system with periodic boundary conditions and demonstrates their convergence, under grid refinement, to the sensitivities obtained by finite differences or automatic differentiation through the forward solve.

</details>


### [217] [Theoretical Analysis of Thermodynamic Matrix Inversion: First-order Equivalence to Preconditioned Gradient Descent and Implications for Analog Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09743)
*Gerhard Kirsten,Michael Selby,Janith Petangoda,Olle Halqvist Elias,James Meech,Phillip Stanley-Marbell*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本论文证明耦合电振荡器的热力学矩阵求逆动态可简化为确定性迭代算法。作者分析了Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的矩演化，证明其一阶近似下的协方差动力学与以残差Frobenius范数为目标的预条件梯度下降数学等价，表明热涨落对凸问题是算法冗余的。该算法相比Thermox模拟器提速超10万倍，且与Newton-Schulz迭代具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究展示可利用耦合电振荡器的热力学实现矩阵求逆等计算任务，但物理实现依赖热噪声驱动平衡。作者旨在探究热噪声在算法层面是否必要，并建立热力学计算与确定性优化方法之间的严格理论联系。

Method: 基于Aifer等人的框架，对控制热力学对称正定（SPD）矩阵求逆的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程进行矩演化分析。证明在一阶近似下，协方差动力学与残差A^{-1}A-I的Frobenius范数上的预条件梯度下降数学等价。算法使用Schur补技术加速，并与Thermox随机热力学模拟器进行对比验证。

Result: 获得的确定性算法相比Thermox随机模拟器实现超过100,000倍的加速，同时与Newton-Schulz迭代保持竞争性表现。Schur补技术进一步提供了加速效果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了模拟热力学计算、统计物理与确定性优化方法之间的严格联系，证明热涨落对于具有单一全局最小值的凸问题在算法上是冗余的，尽管它在物理热力学硬件的实现中仍然必不可少。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated the possibility of exploiting the thermodynamics of coupled electrical oscillators to implement computational tasks such as matrix inversion. While physical implementations rely on thermal noise to drive equilibration, we show that the underlying dynamics reduce to a deterministic iterative algorithm. Building on the framework of Aifer et al., we analyze the moment evolution of the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process governing thermodynamic symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrix inversion. We prove that to a first-order approximation, the covariance dynamics are mathematically identical to preconditioned gradient descent on the Frobenius norm of the residual $\tilde{A}^{-1}A-I$. This equivalence demonstrates that thermal fluctuations, while essential for physical thermodynamic hardware, are algorithmically redundant for convex problems with a single global minimum. We validate the resulting algorithm against Thermox (Duffield et al.), a stochastic thermodynamic simulator, achieving speedups exceeding 100,000-fold while remaining competitive with the Newton-Schulz iteration. We also demonstrate acceleration through Schur complement techniques. These results establish a rigorous link between analog thermodynamic computing, statistical physics, and deterministic optimization methods.

</details>


### [218] [Two-sided estimates of Lyapunov exponents for Milstein schemes of non-autonomous stochastic differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09756)
*Vu Thi Hue,Bao Quoc Tang,Do Duc Thuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Milstein格式离散化下乘性噪声对随机微分方程的稳定化效应是否保持，通过建立Lyapunov指数的尖锐上下界估计，证明了从连续设定到离散设定的稳定化保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性噪声对随机微分方程的稳定化效应虽违反直觉但已被广泛研究，实践中需要知道这种稳定化在离散化设定下是否仍然成立，这是一个待解决的重要问题。

Method: 对非自治随机微分方程的Milstein格式建立Lyapunov指数的尖锐上下界估计，利用Milstein格式中关于随机噪声的二阶项，对对数函数和幂函数的Taylor展开进行精确估计。

Result: 获得了几乎必然意义和p-矩意义下精确的长时间行为，证明了稳定化从连续设定到离散设定的保持性。

Conclusion: Milstein格式的离散化设定下，乘性噪声的稳定化效应得以保留，且通过Lyapunov指数的尖锐估计可以精确刻画其长时间行为。

Abstract: The stabilising effect of multiplicative noise for stochastic differential equations, though counterintuitive, has been observed and investigated extensively in last decades. In practice, it is desirable to know if such stabilisation holds also for the discretised setting. In this paper, we address this problem by means of sharp upper and lower estimates for Lyapunov exponents of Milstein schemes for non-autonomous stochastic differential equations. These estimates provide precise large time behaviour in both almost sure and $p$-moment sense. In particular, our results show the preservation of stabilisation from the continuum setting to the discretised setting. One main idea of our analysis is to exploit the second order term concerning the stochastic noise from the Milstein scheme to obtain precise estimates for Taylor expansions of logarithmic and power functions.

</details>


### [219] [Convergence of Galerkin approximations based on asymmetrically-weighted Hermite functions for the Vlasov-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09827)
*Ruiyang Dai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于非对称加权（AW）Hermite函数的Galerkin近似在Vlasov-Poisson（VP）系统上的收敛性。通过引入无加权内积，建立了数值方法在无加权范数下的稳定性，进而证明了方法的收敛性，并推导了数值解与VP系统光滑解之间的误差估计，确认该方法在Sobolev空间中具有谱精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vlasov-Poisson系统在等离子体物理中具有重要应用，其数值求解是计算物理中的关键问题。基于Hermite函数的Galerkin方法被广泛用于求解该系统的速度维度，但此前基于非对称加权Hermite函数的数值方法的稳定性和收敛性缺乏严格的理论分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文将VP系统写成以速度为变量的Hermite函数展开的双曲型系统。为获得数值方法的稳定性性质，采用无加权内积，并在此内积框架下建立方法在无加权范数下的稳定性。基于稳定性结果，进一步推导数值解与VP系统精确光滑解之间的误差估计。

Result: 证明了基于AW Hermite函数的Galerkin近似方法的收敛性，获得了数值解与光滑解之间的误差估计，并确认该方法在Sobolev空间中达到谱精度。

Conclusion: 基于非对称加权Hermite函数的Galerkin方法对Vlasov-Poisson系统具有可靠的数学基础，其在Sobolev空间中展现出谱精度，为该类数值方法的应用提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: The convergence of Galerkin approximations based on asymmetrically-weighted (AW) Hermite functions, applied to the Vlasov-Poisson (VP) system, is analyzed in this work. The VP system is written as an hyperbolic system using Hermite functions in velocity. To obtain stability properties of the numerical methods, we consider an unweighted inner product, and establish naturely the stability with respect to the unweighted norm. Consequently, we prove the convergence of the method. Furthermore, we derive error estimates between the numerical solution and the smooth solution of the VP system. Our results confirm that the Galerkin approximations based on AW Hermite functions exhibit spectral accuracy in Sobolev spaces.

</details>


### [220] [Bijectivity analysis of rational T-spline surfaces via Bernstein representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09838)
*Jia-Xuan Li,Ying-Ying Yu,Ya-Shu Liu,Xin Li,Ye Ji,Chun-Gang Zhu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bézier提取的严格且高效的框架，用于有理T样条曲面的双射性分析。通过将T样条重构为逐元素有理Bézier片，利用Bernstein基的凸包和正性性质推导双射的充分与必要条件，并结合层级细分策略实现自适应、无需密集采样的可靠验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样条参数化的双射性是几何建模和等几何分析的根本要求，无效映射会导致自交、奇异雅可比和数值不稳定。T样条虽通过局部细化提供了更强的灵活性，但这种灵活性使得双射性验证变得更具挑战性，亟需一种严格且高效的验证方法。

Method: 该框架的核心是将T样条表示重构为逐元素有理Bézier片集合，在该表示下映射的Gram行列式具有Bernstein多项式表示。利用Bernstein基的凸包和正性性质，推导出基于Bernstein系数非负性的双射充分条件，以及基于角系数符号一致性的必要条件。对于条件不充分的情况，引入层级细分策略逐步定位和解析模糊区域，实现自适应的精化验证。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能准确检测有效和近退化配置，可有效扩展到包含数千个有理Bézier片的大型模型，且避免了密集数值采样，在计算效率上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该方法为T样条曲面双射性验证提供了认证且自适应的流程，完全兼容标准等几何分析工作流，在保证严格性的同时兼具计算效率。

Abstract: Ensuring the bijectivity of spline-based parameterizations is fundamental in geometric modeling and isogeometric analysis, as invalid mappings may lead to self-intersections, singular Jacobians, and numerical instability. While T-splines offer enhanced flexibility through local refinement, this flexibility also makes bijectivity verification significantly more challenging. In this work, we propose a rigorous and efficient framework for bijectivity analysis of rational T-spline surfaces based on Bézier extraction. The key idea is to reformulate the T-spline representation into a collection of element-wise rational Bézier patches, on which the Gram determinant of the mapping admits a Bernstein polynomial representation. This enables a coefficient-based analysis of local regularity by exploiting the convex hull and positivity properties of the Bernstein basis. Based on this formulation, we derive a sufficient condition for bijectivity from the nonnegativity of Bernstein coefficients, together with a necessary condition based on the sign consistency of corner coefficients. For cases where these conditions are inconclusive, we introduce a hierarchical subdivision strategy that progressively localizes ambiguous regions and resolves them through refinement. The proposed method provides a certified and adaptive procedure for bijectivity verification that avoids dense numerical sampling and remains computationally efficient.
